 Catalog number : I00043
Uniform title: Śivagaṇapatisubrahmaṇyapratiṣṭhā
Manuscript : IFP manuscript bundle RE30970
Description:
RE30970. This bundle titled as Śiva-Gaṇapati-Subrahmaṇyapratiṣṭhā, (शिवगणपतिसुब्रह्मण्य-प्रतिष्ठा), it encompasses a detailed exploration of consecration rituals for Lord Śiva, Lord Gaṇapati, and Lord Subrahmaṇya. It begins with the Samprokṣaṇavidhi (ritual on purification) from Śrīmadaṃśumattantra and continues with the pratiṣṭhā chapters for Lord Śiva, derived from Śivālayanirmāṇadīpikā. The pratiṣṭhā chapters for Lord Subrahmaṇya are sourced from Kumāratantra, while the pratiṣṭhā chapters for Lord Gaṇapati are taken from Śrīmaccandrajñānāgama. Together, these texts offer a comprehensive guide to temple construction and deity consecration, emphasizing the unique procedures and symbolic rituals associated with each deity.

The bundle contains 109 folios and there are 5-8 lines in each folio. The size of the folio is 39x3.3 mm. This manuscript is an invaluable resource for understanding the Āgamic principles and rituals surrounding deity consecration, drawing from significant Tantras and Āgamas. Its blend of ritualistic and architectural details makes it an essential reference for practitioners and scholars of Hindu temple traditions on the Āgamas.

(diśāhomavidhiḥ -)

diśāhomaṃ tato vakṣye dvividhasya udāhṛtam ।
prāyaścittārthamātodyanipatābhyaṅgabhāgbhavet ॥ (l.2)

prāsādāgre maṇṭapaṃ sampādya (l.3) tanmadhye vedi । tatpūrvakuṇḍaṃ vṛttaṃ vā caturaśraṃ vā hastamātreṇa kuṇḍaṃ kṛtvā vitānādyairalaṅkṛtya । tato guruḥ snātvā । sakalīkṛtyā(tya) । puṇyāhaṃ vācayitvā । (l.4) viśeṣārghyaṃ saṃsādya(dhya) । pañcagavyaṃ sampādya । saṃprokṣya । kuṇḍaṃ vediṃ cārghyajalena prokṣya । vedimadhye sasūtrān vastraveṣṭitān gandhodake pūritān hemapaṅkaja(l.5)kūrcapallavasāpidhānanālikerapañcaratnasaṃyuktān kumbhān madhye pūrvayāmyapaścimottareṣu saṃsthāpya । (l.6) madhyakumbhasyottare vardhanīṃ ca saṃsathāpya । madhyakumbhe śivaṃ saṃpūjya । vardhanyāṃ oṃ paṃ śuṃ huṃ phaṭ pāśupadāstramūrtaye namaḥ ।

sihmavaktro(tre)'gnidaṃṣṭrāgnisamaprabhordhvaśikhānvitam । 
kṛṣṇājinanibhā vṛttaratnanetratrayānvitam ॥

kṛṣṇāmbaradharaṃ sāsṛmardhamantajare ratā ॥

dhyātvaivaṃ gandhapuṣpādyairarcayetparameśvarīm ।

	oṃ ślīṃ huṃ phaṭ pāśupatāstrāya namaḥ । iti sāṅgaṃ saṃpūjya । paścāccaturdikṣu kalaśeṣu । oṃ haḥ astrāya namaḥ । (l.2) oṃ haḥ astramūrtaye namaḥ ।

caturvaktraṃ jvalatkeśaṃ vidyutpuñjasamaprabham ।
candramaṇḍalamūrdhānaṃ taccutuṣkarapallavam ॥
aśanidhvanisaṃyuktaṃ asahyā bhītivikramam ।
dvādaśākṣaṃ tathā śaktiṃ *śūlābhayavarānvitam ॥* (l.3)
dhyātvaivaṃ gandhapuṣpādyaiḥ śivāstraṃ svāṇunā yajet ।

	iti dhyātvā । oṃ haḥ astrāya huṃ phaṭ iti saṃpūjya । dakṣaṇe sphura sphura (l.4) ghora ghora tanurūpa tanurūpa caṭa caṭa pracaṭa pracaṭa kaha kaha vama vama bandha bandha ghātaya ghātaya huṃ phaṭ svāhā । ityaghorāstrāsanamūrtimūlaiḥ saṃpūjya । (l.5) paścime – oṃ ślīṃ paṃ śuṃ huṃ phaṭ pāśupatāstrāsanamūrtimūlābhyarcya । saumye – oṃ āṃ īṃ ūṃ rudrāsthiśṛṅkhale hūṃ sarvato bhadre kuṭi(l.6)rudravacanavidhāri rakṣa māṃ pratyaṅgire dhāma dhāma jyoti suve suvaṃ bhave bhavaḥ । pāhyamṛtoha devaṃ satyaṃ tribhiḥ dam pādamanaruṣāṃ lakṣmīṃ namo vaḥ । kiṃ narike'yaṃ karoti tameva (l.7) vo viśajaṃ mahādevaḥ suvrate namo ma svāha । iti pratyaṅgirāstramāsanamūrtimūlaiḥ saha saṃpūjya । iti vijñāpya । pāvakālayaṃ gatvā । pūrvavat kuṇḍāgniṃ samskṛtyā(tya) । agnihṛtpadme (p.8b, f.3, अष्टविद्येश्वराणां mūlamantram l.1) śivaṃ sāṅgaṃ saṃpūjya । plakṣaudumbarāśvattha-vaṭapālāśājyatiladūrvākṣataiścaruśca saha homadravyāṇi śataṃ śataṃ pañcāśadvā pratyekametairjuhuyuḥ । tataḥ (l.2) astrāṇāṃ pūjitakrameṇāgnau āvāhya । etairdravyaiḥ śataṃ pañcāśadvā juhuyuḥ । paścāt prāyaścittārthaṃ śivāstreṇāṣṭottaraśataṃ hutvā । pūrṇāṃ datvā । caruṃ nivedya । rakṣādhānaṃ (l.3) nidhāya । arghyaṃ datvālayāṅgaṃ sannirodhana... antarbaliṃ paścādāvāhanāntamantravigrahaṃ karmavijñāpanaṃ devaṃ prārthayan । tatprabhāsataḥ kartā bhasmapradānaṃ ca kuryāt । (l.4) sarvamanukramāt । devaṃ kumbhasthāpānīyaiḥ saṃsnāpya । abhyarcayet guruḥ । devābhidhāmniṃ kṛtaṃ tattu yadi kumbhodakaistathā । prāyaścittaṃ samuddiśya vartate (l.5) prokṣaṇaṃ kṛtam । diśā homā samākhyātam । sarvāriṣṭanivāraṇam । iti diśāhomavidhiḥ samāptaḥ । śubhamastu śrī śaravaṇabhavāya namaḥ । śrīrastu śrīvāgdevyai namaḥ । śrīrastu । (p.9a, aṣṭavidyeśvarāṇāṃ mūlamantram, l.1) gaṇapatiக்கு – oṃ śrīṃ gaṃ gaṇapataye namaḥ । iti mūlamantram । 

varado varavighneśo gajavaktra umāsutaḥ ।
bhinnadantaḥ śūrpakarṇo vighnarājñāṣṭamūrtinaḥ ॥

subrahmaṇyasya – oṃ sauṃ (l.2) śaravaṇabhavaskandāya namaḥ । iti mūlamantram । 

jayantaśca viśākhaśca kārtikeyaṣṣaḍānanaḥ ।
guhassenāpatiskando subrahmaṇyeṣṭamūrtinaḥ ॥

gaurī gaṅgā ca durgā ca indrāṇī bhūmidevi ca ॥

dakṣiṇāmūrtiக்கு – oṃ namo bhagavate dakṣiṇāmūrtaye namaḥ । mahyaṃ medhāṃ prayaccha svāhā । iti mūlamantram । 
sanatkumāraḥ sanakassanandaśca *sanātanaḥ ।* (l.6)
agastyaśca pulastyaśca kauśikaḥ kāśyapastathā ॥
durga(rgai)க்கு – oṃ duṃ durge dakṣiṇe svāhā । 
durgā kātyāyanī caiva jayā caiva parājitā ।
āryā bhagavatī caiva yantrikā śarvarī tathā ॥ (l.7)
kāntākhyā ca krameṇaiva pūrvādiṣu prapūjayet ।

somāskandaருக்கு – oṃ hāṃ vyāmavyāpine śrīkaṇṭhāya namaḥ । aṣṭavidyeśvarāḥ – anantādayaḥ । kālanigrahasya – oṃ jṛṃ haḥ (l.8) aṣṭavidyeśvarāḥ । anantādayaḥ । naṭeśvarasya – oṃ sakṣamalavarayūṃ tuṃ purāntabhairavāya nṛttamūrtaye namaḥ । anantādyāḥ । vidyeśvarāḥ । (p.9b, f.4a, l.1)

caṇḍeśvarasya – oṃ hāṃ dhvanicaṇḍeśvarāya huṃ phaṇṇamaḥ । indrādiḥ । kṣetrapālasya – oṃ śrāṃ kṣīṃ kṣūṃ kṣetrapālakāya namaḥ । iti mūlamantram । 

asitāṅgo ruruścaḍaḥ krodhaśconmattabhairavaḥ । (l.2)
kapālī bhīṣaṇaścaiva saṃhāraścāṣṭabhairavāḥ ॥

aghorāstrasya – oṃ  hrīṃ sphura sphura prasphura ghora ghora tanulūpa caṭa caṭa pracaṭa pracaṭa kaḥ kaḥ vama vama ghātaya ghātaya oṃ phaṭ svāhā । (l.3) dāruṇo bhīṣaṇo bhīṣṇo? raudraścogro bhayāvahaḥ । ghoro ghorataraścaiva aghorāstramūrtayaḥ । pāśupadāstrasya - oṃ ślīṃ paṃ śuṃ huṃ phaṭ pāśupadāstrāya namaḥ । (l.4) indrādiḥ । oṃ haḥ astrāya phaṭ - iti śivāstram । pratyaṅgirāstrasya – oṃ āṃ īṃ raṃ ūṃ rudrāsthiśri(śṛ)ṅkhā(ṅgā)ṣṭale(tale) hūṃ sarvatobhadre kuṭirudravacanavicāri rakṣa māṃ pratyaṅgire dhāma (l.5) dhāmajyotijyotiḥ suvassubhuve bhuvaḥ prāśyāmṛto'haṃ satyahantribhidan pādo juṣā lakṣmī namo vaḥ kirikeyaṃ karoti tamo vāñco viśaja mahādeva suvrato nama(maḥ) svāheti pratyaṅgirā(l.6)stramūlamantram । oṃ saṃ bhakṣajvālājihvākarālidamṣṭravadani pratyaṅgire । oṃ hrīṃ namo'stu te svāhā । kṣirikāstram । oṃ kṣuṃ kṣurikāstrāya huṃ phaṭ svāhā । reṇukāyāḥ – oṃ hauṃ (l.7) svāhā bhagavatī raktapañcamī reṇukādevī daha daha paca paca sarvajanavaśaṃ kuru kuru svāhā ।

jayā ca vijayā caiva bhoginī mohinī tathā ।
malinī mālinī caiva bhogābhogavatī tathā ॥ (l.8)
śivasāvitrī –
vyāmavyomāyavidmahe sūkṣmasūkṣmāya dhīmahī tanno rudraḥpracodayāt ।
śivagāyatrī –
oṃ tanmaheśāya vidmahe vāgviśuddhāya dhīmahī tannaḥśivapracodayāt । (p.10a, l.1)
astragāyatrī –
oṃ śivāstrāya vidmahe kālānalāya dhīmahī tannaḥśivapracodayāt ।
sadāśivākṣaram –
oṃ hāṃ haṃ sadāśivāya namaḥ ।
navākṣaram –
oṃ hauṃ haṃ saṃ maṃ kṣaṃ raṃ naṃ yaṃ mya? iti (l.2) 
daśākṣaram –
oṃ hauṃ saṃ saṃ mūṃ hrūṃ meṃ naṃ maṃ yaṃ iti 10
subrahmaṇyamālāmantram –
oṃ śaravaṇabhavāya ṣaṇmukhāya kumārāya kukkuṭadhvajāya huṃ phaṭ svāhā ।
skandagāyatrī –
kukkuṭa(l.3)dhvajāya vidmahe vajrahastāya dhīmahī tannaḥskandaḥpracodayāt ।
bhadrakālyāḥ –
oṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ klīṃ mahācāmuṇḍī vo raktacāmuṇḍī hana hana daha daha oṃ svāhā । iti mūlamantram । (l.4)
mālāmantram - 
oṃ hrīṃ mahācāmuṇḍī ugracāmuṇḍī oṃ hrīṃ vīracāmuṇḍīṃ haiṃ raktacāmuṇḍīṃ hoṃ khaḍgaśūlaḍamaruga(ka)kapālahastiṃ śrīṃ srīṃ klīṃ varavaradamahācāmuṇḍīṃ (l.5) svāhā । brāhmyādyaṣṭau śaktayaḥ । anuktaḥ sarvadevānāṃ indrādyāḥ mūrtayaḥ । brahmādyāḥ aṣṭaśaktayaḥ । yogāmbāsametapālīśvarāya namaḥ । (l.6)

yātrāhomasamāyuktaṃ pūrvoktavidhimācaret ।
liṅgāgre sthaṇḍilaṃ kṛtvā kalpapadmamaṣṭadalaṃ likhet ॥
sthaṇḍile vinyasetkumbhaṃ vardhanīṃ tasya vāmake ।
sakūrcān pallavo*petān navaratnasamanvitān ॥* (l.7)
vastreṇaiva samāyuktaṃ kumbhamadhye tu veṣṭayet ।
udakena samāyuktaṃ nālikerasamanvitam ॥
sarvālaṅkārasaṃyuktaṃ kṣelanena samanvitam ।
liṅgagā*ddevaṃ samāvāhya kumbhamadhye tu vinyaset ॥* (p.10b, f.5a, l.1)
pīṭhāddevīṃ samāvāhya tadbījena samanvitam ।
sarvālaṅkārasaṃyuktaṃ kṣelanena samanvitam ॥
vedikopari saṃsthāpya īśānena tu mantrataḥ । (l.2)
tasya vāme tu vardhanyāṃ sthāpayeddhṛdayena tu ॥
parivāraghaṭāṃścaiva tataḥ tatsthāneṣu vinyaset ।
gandhapuṣpādibhiścaiva arcayettu vicakṣaṇaḥ ॥
ṛgvedaṃ pūrvadigbhāge yajurvedaṃ tu yāmyake । (l.3)
paścime sāmavedaṃ tu adharvedaṃ tathottara ।
etat sthānabhāve tu paścime caiva vinyaset ॥
kumbhaṃ prati viśeṣeṇa catvāro vedapāragān ।
kumbhasparśanamevaṃ *tu tattanmantraṃ japettathā ॥* (l.4)
śivasaṃprokṣaṇe caiva tattatpīḍā(ṭhā)kṛtiryathā ।
tathā kuṇḍākṛtiṃ kṛtvā agnikāryaṃ samācaret ॥
sadyena samidhaṃ hṛtvā mūlenājyaṃ *ca homayet ।* (l.5)
aghoreṇa caruṃ hutvā lājaṃ tatpuruṣeṇa tu ॥
mudgaṃ hṛdayamantreṇa māṣaṃ tu śirasā hunet ।
netramantreṇa ca yavaṃ tilaṃ vāmena homayet ॥ (l.6)
śālimīśānamantreṇa godhūmaṃ cāstramantrataḥ ।
śikhāmantreṇa nīvāraṃ kavacena tu saktukam ॥
pāśupatena siddhārthaṃ bījamukhye huneddadhi ।
daśākṣareṇa dugdhaṃ tu madhuṃ ca *juhuyāttataḥ ॥* (l.7)
gulaṃ hṛdayamantreṇa daśākṣaraphalaṃ hunet ।
daśāṣṭadravyasaṃyuktaṃ homayeddeśikottamaḥ ॥
nālikeraśikhāṃ kṛtvā mantravedikasaṃyutam ।
saṃpātāhutimevaṃ ca sparśāhutiṃ *samācaret ॥* (l.8)
bhasmanyāsādikaṃ kṛtvā sakalīkaraṇaṃ kuru ।
maṇṭapasyārcanaṃ kuryāt agnikāryaṃ samācaret ॥
jayādirabhyādhānaṃ ca rāṣṭrabhṛcca kramāddhunet ।
pūrṇāhutiṃ *ca śirasā sviṣṭamagneti mantrataḥ ॥* (p.11a, l.1)
kuṇḍāddevaṃ samāvāhya kumbhamadhye tu yojayet ।
muhūrtāt pūrvakāle tu sarvātodyasamanvitam ॥
svastivācasamāyuktaṃ śivasaṅkalpameva ca । (l.2)
kumbhānuddhṛtya śirasā kṛtvā dhāmapradakṣiṇam ॥
garbhagehaṃ praviśyātha liṅgāgre caiva vinyaset ।
mantranyāsādikaṃ kṛtvā adhvanyāsakramaṃ tathā ॥
brahmaghoṣasamāyuktaṃ nānābhaktajanairyutam । (l.3)
saṃpūjya deśikaṃ caivaṃ dakṣiṇāṃ dāpayettathā ॥
deśikasya manastuṣṭirdevasānnidhyakārakā ।
deśikaṃ toṣayettasmāt sannadhīkaraṇāya ca ॥
gobhūhiraṇyakānya tatra datvā *viprān sutoṣayet ।* (l.4)
muhūrtaṃ nirīkṣya devajño gurave sannivedayet ॥
ācāryaḥ suprasannātmā liṅgasya dakṣiṇe sthitaḥ ।
kumbhādbījaṃ samādāya sthāpayetparameśvaram ॥ (l.5)
vardhanyāṃ ca manonmanyā bījamādāya pūrvavat ।
vidyeśabījamādāya indrādīśāntakaṃ kramāt ॥
kumbhasthaiśca jalaiścetat tatsthāne'bhiṣecayet ।
devakumbhābhiṣekaṃ ca mantrānnaṃ ca nivedayet ॥ (l.6)
vastrairābharaṇairmālyairalaṅkṛtya viśeṣataḥ ।
mahāhaviṃ nivedyaṃ ca nityāgniṃ kārayettataḥ ॥
nityotsavādikaṃ kṛtvā pūrvavatsarvamācaret ।
mokṣārthī labhate mokṣaṃ putrārthī *labhate sutam ॥* (l.7)
arthārthī arthamāpnoti jayārthī yajamāpnuyāt ।
iha loke sukhaṃ prāpya so'nte sāyujyamāpnuyāt ॥
॥ ityaṃśumāntantre saṃprokṣaṇavidhipaṭalaḥ ॥ śubhamastu । śrī yogāmbikādevīsametaśrīpālīśvarasvāmikṛpākaṭākṣasiddhirastu । (p.11b, f.6a, l.1)

rakṣābandhanavidhiḥ –
atha taṇḍule । oṃ hāṃ ādhāraśaktaye namaḥ । tripādī । oṃ hāṃ ātmatattvāya namaḥ । oṃ hiṃ vidyātattvāya namaḥ । oṃ hūṃ śivatattvāya namaḥ । sthalikāyāṃ ṣaḍutthāsanaṃ saṃkalpya । oṃ hāṃ (l.2) anantāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ dharmāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ jñānāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ vairāgyāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ aiśvaryāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ padmāya namaḥ । ityabhyarcya । taṇḍule astrāya huṃ phaṇṇamaḥ । (l.3) bhasmādhāre । māyātattvāya namaḥ । bhasmani । lakulīśvarāya namaḥ । pratisarasūtre oṃ hāṃ anantāya namaḥ । tāmbūle । oṃ hāṃ brahmaṇe namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ viṣṇave namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ rudrāya (l.4) namaḥ । nālikeraphale । vighneśvarāya namaḥ । pūrve lavaṇe । oṃ hāṃ tatpuruṣavaktrāya namaḥ । dakṣiṇe । śuṣkagomaye । oṃ hāṃ aghorāya namaḥ । paścime । rātricūrṇe oṃ hāṃ (l.5) sadyojātāya namaḥ । uttare । taṇḍule । oṃ hāṃ vāmadevāya namaḥ । madhye maṅgalyasūtre oṃ hāṃ īśānābhya (l.6) iti mantreṇa sūtraṃ japitvā । naivedyatāmbūla-sarvopacārān kṛtvā viśvottāteti mantreṇa rakṣāṃ bandhayet । bṛhatsāmeti rakṣāṃ kṛtvā । śrīrastu । (l.7) śrī yogāmbikādevīsametaśrīamṛteśvarasvāmikṛpākaṭākṣasiddhirastu । śrī vāgdevyai namaḥ । śrīrastu । śrī dakṣiṇāmūrtaye namaḥ । viśvanāthaguroḥ karakṛtalikhitam । śrīrastu । (p.12a, l.1) 

antarbalibahirbalī –
tataḥ svadakṣiṇadigbhāge maṇḍaladvayaṃ niṣpādya । prathamamaṇḍale । pūrvāduyattarāntadikṣu । oṃ hāṃ rudrebhyo namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ mātṛbhyo namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ gaṇebhyo namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ yakṣebhyo (l.2) namaḥ । itīśānādikoṇeṣu । oṃ hāṃ gṛhebhyo namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ asurebhyo namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ rākṣasebhyo namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ nāgebhyo namaḥ । maṇḍalamadhye īśānāgneya(l.3)naiṛtakoṇeṣu । oṃ hāṃ nakṣatrebhyo namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ rāśibhyo namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ viśvebhyo namaḥ । vāyuvaruṇayormadhye oṃ hāṃ kṣetrapālāya namaḥ । ityabhyarcya । (l.4) svāhāntamarghyaṃ datvā । dvitīyyamaṇḍale – pūrvādīśāntadikṣu । oṃ hāṃ indrāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ agnaye namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ yamāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ varuṇāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ vāyave (l.5) namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ kuberāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ īśānāya namaḥ । aiśānyāṃ oṃ hāṃ brahmaṇe namaḥ । naiṛṛtyāṃ oṃ hāṃ viṣṇave namaḥ । madhye oṃ hāṃ nīlalohitāya namaḥ । (l.6) iti saṃpūjyārghyaṃ datvā । pūrvaṃ rudrādibhyo baliṃ svāhāntaṃ kṣiptvācamanaṃ datvā indrādibhyo tathaiva balyācamanaṃ datvā bahiḥ oṃ hāṃ vāyasādibhyaḥ samayabhedibhya(bhyaḥ) svāhā ityabhyarcya baliṃ datvā oṃ hāṃ agnyādibhyaḥ svāhā । oṃ hāṃ sarvebhyo grahavāstudevebhya(bhyaḥ) svāhā iti pṛthak pṛthak baliṃ datvārghyaṃ datvā visṛjet । (p.12b, 13a blank, 13b, f.38a, tattvatattveśvaram, l.1) brahmarandhrordhve rudrabhāge । nirodhāśakti(kta)ye namaḥ । śivatattvāya namaḥ । śivatattvādhipataye śivāya namaḥ । mūrdhni – śaktitattvāya namaḥ । śaktitattvādhipataye śaktaye (l.2) namaḥ । gale oṃ hāṃ sadāśivatattvāya namaḥ । sadāśivatattvādhipataye brahmaṇe namaḥ । īśvaratattvāya namaḥ । īśvaratattvādhipataye anantāya namaḥ । (l.3) śuddhavidyā-tattvāya namaḥ । śuddhavidyātattvādhipataye śambarāya namaḥ । vāmapārśve । māyātattvāya namaḥ । māyātattvādhipataye gahaneśāya (l.4) namaḥ । dakṣiṇapārśve viṣṇubhāge – urasi – kalātattvāya namaḥ । kalātattvā-dhipataye kālanāmne namaḥ । hṛdaye – niyatitattvāya namaḥ । (l.5) niyatitattvādhipataye niyāmakāya namaḥ । kālatattvāya namaḥ । kālatattvādhipataye jñānabindave namaḥ । vidyātattvāya namaḥ । (l.6) vidyātattvādhipataye vedavijñānāya namaḥ । rāgatattvāya namaḥ । rāgatattvā-dhipataye kāmadāya namaḥ । puruṣatattvāya namaḥ । puruṣa(l.7)tattvādhipataye sūkṣmadehāya namaḥ । udare – prakṛtitattvāya namaḥ । prakṛtitattvādhipataye pradhānakāya namaḥ । pṛṣṭhe – guṇatattvāya namaḥ । guṇatattvādhipataye (p.14a, l.1) tridehāya namaḥ । nābhimeḍhrāntare – buddhitattvāya namaḥ । buddhitattvādhipataye buddhāya namaḥ । trisandhiṣu – haṃkāratattvāya namaḥ । haṃkāra-tattvā(l.2)dhipataye haṃkṛtaye namaḥ । brahmabhāge – manastattvāya namaḥ । manastattvādhipataye saṃkalpāya namaḥ । śrotratattvāya namaḥ । śrotratattvādhipataye vyomacārāya (l.3) namaḥ । tvaci – oṃ hāṃ tvaktattvāya namaḥ । tvaktattvādhipataye sarvāṅgāya namaḥ । cakṣuṣi – cakṣustattvāya namaḥ । cakṣustattvādhipataye (l.4) prakāśakāya namaḥ । jihvāyām – jihvātattvāya namaḥ । jihvātattvādhipataye mahāvaktrāya namaḥ । nāsāyām – nāsātattvāya namaḥ । (l.5) nāsātattvādhipataye vilumbakāya namaḥ । vaktre – vāktattvāya namaḥ । vāktattvādhipataye vibhave namaḥ । pādatattvāya namaḥ । pāda(l.6)tattvādhipataye ramaṇāya namaḥ । pāṇyau – pāṇitattvāya namaḥ । pāṇitattvādhipataye sāmagāya namaḥ । pāyau – pāyutattvāya namaḥ । (p.14b,  f.39a, l.1) pāyutattvādhipataye tūrṇakṛte namaḥ । upasthe – upasthatattvāya namaḥ । upasthatattvādhipataye ghanānandāya namaḥ । nābhau – śabdatattvāya namaḥ । śabdatattvādhipataye (l.2) sūkṣmanādāya namaḥ । sparśatattvāya namaḥ । sparśatattvādhipataye balavattarāya namaḥ । rūpatattvāya namaḥ । rūpatattvādhipataye bhānumate namaḥ । rasatattvāya namaḥ । (l.3) rasatattvādhipataye jaladāya namaḥ । gandhatattvāya namaḥ । gandhatattvādhipataye gandhāṅgāya namaḥ । yonyām – ākāśatattvāya namaḥ । (l.4) ākāśatattvā-dhipataye sūkṣmāya namaḥ । vāyutattvāya namaḥ । vāyutattvādhipataye mātariśvine namaḥ । ūrū – agnitattvāya namaḥ । agnitattvādhi(l.5)pataye tviṣāṃ nidhaye namaḥ । jalatattvāya namaḥ । jalatattvādhipataye jaleśāya namaḥ । jānvadhaḥ – pṛthivītattvāya namaḥ । pṛthivītattvā(l.6)dhipataye śrīkaṇṭhāya namaḥ । iti tattvatattveśvaraṃ samāptam । (p.15a, mūrtimūrtīśvaram, l.1)

mūrtimūrtīśvaram –
evaṃ vinyasya rudrabhāge - śivatattvam । oṃ hūṃ śivatattvāya namaḥ । oṃ hūṃ śivatattvādhipataye namaḥ । rudrāya namaḥ । viṣṇubhāge - oṃ hīṃ (l.2) vidyātattvāya namaḥ । oṃ hīṃ vidyātattvādhipataye viṣṇave namaḥ । brahmabhāge – oṃ hāṃ ātmatattvāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ ātmatattvādhipataye brahmaṇe namaḥ । evaṃ (l.3) vinyasya । pūrvādikrameṇaiśānāntam । mūrtimūrtīśvaram । oṃ hāṃ lūṃ kṣmāmūrtaye namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ lūṃ kṣmāmūrtyadhipataye (l.4) śarvāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ śūṃ yajamāna-mūrtyadhipataye ugrāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ śūṃ (l.5) yajamānamūrtaye namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ śūṃ yajamānamūrtyadhipataye ugrāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ ṣūṃ arkamūrtaye namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ (l.6) ṣūṃ arkamūrtyadhipataye rudrāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ pruñjalamūrtaye namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ pruñjalamūrtyadhipataye bhavāya (l.7) namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ yūṃ vāyumūrtaye namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ yūṃ vāyumūrtyadhipataye īśvarāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ sūṃ indramūrtaye namaḥ । oṃ (p.15b, f.40a, l.1) hāṃ sūṃ indramūrtyadhipataye mahādevāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ hūṃ khamūrtyadhipataye bhīmāya namaḥ । iti mūrtimūrtipānvinyet । yadvā – (l.2) pañcapakṣe । paścimottarayāmyapūrveśānadikṣu । oṃ hāṃ lūṃ pṛthivīmūrtaye namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ lūṃ pṛthivīmūrtyadhipataye brahmaṇe namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ (l.3) vūṃ jalamūrtaye namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ vūṃ jalamūrtyadhipataye viṣṇave namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ ruṃ vahnimūrtaye namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ rūṃ vahnimūrtya(l.4)dhipataye rudrāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ yūṃ vāyumūrtaye namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ yūṃ vāyumūrtyadhipataye īśvarāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ hūṃ (l.5) ākāśamūrtaye namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ hūṃ ākāśamūrtyadhipataye sadāśivāya namaḥ । iti pratikhaṇḍaṃ vinyasya rodhayet ।

gurūpadeśāt *kandādermantramuccārayecchanaiḥ ।* (l.6)
nādaśaktimayaṃ sūkṣmaṃ khaṇṭānādavisarpaṇam ॥
kāraṇatyāgayogena brahmarandhra*nirgatam ।* (l.7)
divākarasahasrābhaṃ vidyutkoṭisamānakam ॥
sphuradraśmivitānena pradīpitadigantaram ।
mantraṃ ca manasā bhinnaṃ prāptānandarisau....m ॥ (p.16a, l.1)
dvādaśāntāt samānīya niṣkalaṃ vyāpakaṃ śivam ।
aṣṭatriṃśatkalopetaṃ sahasrakiraṇojvalam ॥
sarvaśaktimayaṃ sāṅgaṃ dhyātve dvāvali*sannibham ।* (l.2)
tejo bimbaṃ lalāṭasthaṃ ghaṇḍikādvāramānayan ॥
suṣumnānirgataṃ dhyātvā sphurattārakasarpiṇam ।
viyantaṃ brahmarandhreṇa sakale vyāpakaṃ *nyaset ॥* (l.3)
jīvanyāso bhaveddevaṃ liṅge sarvārthasādhakaḥ ।
sthāpanaṃ sannidhānaṃ ca sannirodhāvakuṇṭhanai ॥ (l.4)
mudrayā darśanaṃ pādyamācamanārghyaprasūnakam ।
ebhiḥ samskṛtya saṃpūjya vastreṇācchādanaṃ tathā ॥
bhasmadarbhatilaiḥ prākāratrayaṃ *bahirvidadhyāt ।* (l.5)

	piṇḍikāyāṃ nālamārabhya oṃ hīṃ śaktitattvāya namaḥ । oṃ hīṃ śaktitattvādhipataye śaktaye namaḥ । pīṭha(l.6)kaṇṭhordhvabhāge, nālabhāge trikhaṇḍakalpanārthaṃ puṣpamālāpratisaraṃ datvā । oṃ hūṃ śivatattvāya namaḥ । oṃ hūṃ śiva(l.7)tattvādhipāyai icchāśaktyai namaḥ । pīṭhakaṇṭhe – oṃ hiṃ vidyātattvāya namaḥ । oṃ hiṃ vidyātattvādhipāyai jñānaśaktyai namaḥ । (p.16b, f.41a, l.1) kaṇṭhādhobhāge – oṃ hāṃ ātmatattvāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ ātmatattvādhipāyai kriyāśaktaye namaḥ । tataḥ partibhāgaṃ pūrvādidikṣu – oṃ hāṃ kṣmāmūrtaye namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ (l.2) kṣmāmūrtyadhipāyai dhārikāyai namaḥ । agnimūrtaye namaḥ । agnimūrtyadhipāyai dīptimatyai namaḥ । yajamānamūrtaye namaḥ । yajamānamūrtyadhachipāyai (l.3) ugrāyai namaḥ । arkamūrtaye namaḥ । arkamūrtyadhipāyai jyotsnāyai namaḥ । jalamūrtaye namaḥ । jalamūrtyadhipāyai cetanāyai (l.4) namaḥ । vāyumūrtaye namaḥ । vāyumūrtyadhipāyai balotkaṭāyai namaḥ । indumūrtaye namaḥ । indumūrtyadhipāyai dhātryai (l.5) namaḥ । ākāśamūrtaye namaḥ । ākāśamūrtyadhipāyai vibhavyai namaḥ । yadvā – pañcapakṣe । oṃ hāṃ prathi(thivī)mūrtaye namaḥ । oṃ (l.6) hāṃ pṛthivīmūrtyadhipāyai vāmaśaktyai namaḥ । jalamūrtaye namaḥ । jalamūrtyadhipāyai yogaśaktyai namaḥ । varahnimūrtaye namaḥ । vahni(l.7)mūrtyadhipāyai kriyāśaktyai namaḥ । vāyumūrtaye namaḥ । vāyumūrtyadhipāyai jñānaśaktaye namaḥ । ākāśamūrtaye namaḥ । ākāśamūrtyadhipāyai (p.17a, l.1) icchāśaktyai namaḥ । iti vinyasya । nirudhya । oṃ hrīṃ kriyāśaktaye namaḥ । kriyāśaktiṃ vinyasya । oṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ saṃ mahāgaurī rudradayite svāhā । iti śaktyantamuccāryā(l.2)vāhya । saṃsthāpya । nirodhayet । evaṃ vṛṣabhe'pi vinyasya । oṃ hrīṃ vṛṣabhāya namaḥ । ityāvāhya । nirodhayet । evamādhāraśilāyāmapi vinyasya । (l.3) oṃ sūṃ ādhāraśaktaye namaḥ । oṃ hrīṃ vā ityāvāhya । eteṣāṃ bhasmadarbhatilaiḥ prākāratri(tra)yaṃ bahirvidadhyāt । iti sādhaka(kaṃ) pratiṣṭhā(l.4)mūrtimūrtīśvaram aṣṭavidyeśvaraṃ vinyaset । iti mūrtimūrtīśvaraṃ pratiṣṭhāyām । nivṛtyādimūrtiṃ sadyojātādimūrtīśvaraṃ (l.5) paścimādīśāntamarcayet । pūrvavadvidhinā caruṃ śrapayitvā । pratikuṇḍāṃ(ḍaṃ) nivedya । śāntikumbhāṃścātreṇa śatā(l.6)-bhimantritān sampātābhihutān kṛtvā । tataḥ kuṇḍamadhye parameśvaraṃ, makhalāsu nivṛtyādikalācatuṣṭayaṃ nābhau kriyā(l.7)-śaktimoṣṭhe nādaṃ vinyasya । ghaṭasthaṇḍilavahnisthaśivairātmanāsaha nāḍīsandhānaṃ kuryāt ।

ūrṇatannutanibhā nāḍī muditena *vinirgatām ।* (p.17b, f.42a, l.1)
anṛtena viśantīṃ tāṃ nirgatāmuditena ca ॥
ātmano'nuditenaiva viśantīṃ cintaye'nyathā ।

tatsandhānārthamāhutitrayaṃ datvā kuṇḍe – oṃ hāṃ (l.2) ādhāraśaktyai namaḥ । iti saṃpūjyāhutitrayaṃ datvātmatattvaṃ tadīśaṃ ca saṃpūjya । pratyekamāhutitrayaṃ datvā tato guruḥ – oṃ hāṃ yakṣmāmūrtaye namaḥ । (l.3) kṣmāmūrtyadhipataye śarvāya namaḥ । vahnimūrtaye namaḥ । vahnimūrtyadhipataye paśupataye namaḥ । yajamānamūrtaye namaḥ । yajamānamūrtyadhipāya ugrāya namaḥ । (l.4) arkamūrtaye namaḥ । arkamūrtyadhipāya rudrāya namaḥ । jalamūrtaye namaḥ । jalamūrtyadhipāya bhavāya namaḥ । vāyumūrtaye namaḥ । (l.5) vāyumūrtyadhipāya īśvarāya namaḥ । indumūrtaye namaḥ । indumūrtyadhipāya mahādevāya namaḥ । ākāśamūrtaye namaḥ । (l.6) ākāśamūrtyadhipāya bhīmāya nama iti saṃpūjya । pratyekaṃ svāhāntenāhutitrayaṃ datvā । pañcapakṣe – oṃ hāṃ pṛthivīmūrtaye namaḥ । (p.18a, l.1) pṛthivīmūrtyadhipāya brahmaṇe namaḥ । jalamūrtaye namaḥ । jalamūrtyadhipāya viṣṇave namaḥ । (l.2) vāyumūrtyadhipāya viṣṇave namaḥ । īśvarāya namaḥ । ākāśamūrtaye namaḥ । ākāśamūrtyadhipāya sadāśivāya namaḥ । iti saṃpūjya । (l.3) svāhāntenāhutitrayaṃ datvā । mūlabrahmāṅgāni pratyekaṃ śataṃ sahasramardhaṃ vā pūrṇāsahitaṃ juhuyāt । dravyakālānurodhena (l.4) mūlena śataṃ juhuyāt । brahmāṅgāni sahasramardhaṃ śataṃ vā daśāṃśaṃ hutvā pūrṇāṃ vidhāya । tato guruḥ śāntikumbhamādāya । (l.5) liṅgasamīpaṃ gatvā । śāntikumbhajalena śivaṃ saṃprokṣya । mūlamantreṇa kuśamūlena brahmāṅgaṃ saṃspṛśya । homasaṅkhyayā japtvā । (l.6) guruḥ paścāddhṛdaiva teṣāṃ sannidhiṃ kṛtvā । varmaṇāvakuṇṭhya । niruddhya tatvādhipamājñāṃ śrāvayet । 

ātmatattvādhipabrahmanyāvaddoṣa*samutthitam ।* (l.7)
liṅgaṃ tava tvayātraiva sthātavyaṃ sannidhānataḥ ॥ iti ।

	oṃ hāṃ hiṃ ātmatattvavidyātattvābhyāṃ namaḥ । iti māyāntaṃ bindvantoccāraṇena (p.18b, f.43a, l.1) saṃpūjayet । oṃ hāṃ hāṃ ātmatattvavidyātattvābhyāṃ brahmaviṣṇubhyāṃ namaḥ । ityabhyarcya । oṃ lūṃ lūṃ kṣmāmūrtibhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ hāṃ kṣmāmūrtyadhipābhyāṃ (l.2) śarvābhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ rūṃ rūṃ vahnimūrtibhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ hāṃ vahnimūrtyadhipābhāyaṃ paśupatibhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ śrūṃ śrūṃ yajamānamūrtibhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ (l.3) hāṃ hāṃ yajamānamūrtyadhipābhyāṃ ugrābhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ ṣūṃ ṣūṃ arkamūrtibhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ hāṃ arkamūrtyadhipābhyāṃ rudrābhyāṃ namaḥ । (l.4) oṃ vūṃ vūṃ jalamūrtibhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ hāṃ jalamūrtyadhipābhyāṃ bhavābhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ yūṃ yūṃ vāyumūrtibhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ hāṃ (l.5) vāyumūrtyadhipābhyām īśānābhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ sūṃ sūṃ indramūrtibhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ hāṃ indramūrtyadhipābhyāṃ mahādevābhyāṃ (l.6) namaḥ । oṃ hūṃ hūṃ ākāśa-mūrtibhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ hāṃ ākāśamūrtyadhipābhyāṃ bhīmābhyāṃ namaḥ । iti sandadyāt । pañcapakṣe – (l.7) oṃ hāṃ hāṃ pṛthivīmūrtibhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ hāṃ pṛthivīmūrtyadhipābhyāṃ brahmabhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ hāṃ jalamūrtibhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ hāṃ jala(p.19a, l.1)mūrtyadhipābhyāṃ viṣṇubhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ hāṃ vahnimūrtibhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ hāṃ vahnimūrtyadhipābhyā(bhyāṃ) rudrābhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ hāṃ vāyumūrtibhyāṃ namaḥ । (l.2) oṃ hāṃ hāṃ vāyumūrtyadhipābhyāmīśvarābhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ hāṃ ākāśamūrtibhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ hāṃ ākāśamūrtyadhipābhyāṃ sadāśivābhyāṃ (l.3) namaḥ । iti saṃpūjya । tataḥ kuṇḍāntikaṃ gatvā saṃpūjya । tatsandhānārtham āhutipañcakaṃ mūlena hutvā । oṃ hāṃ hāṃ vidyātatvābhyāṃ (l.4) namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ hāṃ vidyātattvādhipābhyāṃ viṣṇubhyāṃ namaḥ । iti saṃpūjya । sannidhāpya । pratyekamāhutitrayaṃ datvā । (l.5) saṃhitāṃ ca pūrvavajjuhuyāt । paścālliṅgasamīpaṃ gatvā । pūrvavat śāntikumbhāmbhasā prokṣaṇapurassaraṃ mūnamantreṇa (l.6) kuśamadhyena viṣṇubhāgaṃ saṃspṛśya । homasaṅkhyayā japtvā śāntyambhasā prokṣya । sadā sannadhimavakuṇṭhanaṃ (l.7) nirodhanaṃ ca kṛtvā । viṣṇumājñāṃ śrāvayet । vidyātattvapade viṣṇo yāvaddoṣaiḥ samutthitam । liṅgaṃ tāvat tvayātraiva (p.19b, f.44a, l.1) sthātavyaṃ sannidhāna iti । tataḥ oṃ hrīṃ hrūṃ vidyātatvaśavatatvābhyāṃ namaḥ । iti bindvantaṃ nādāntoccāraṇena saṃpūjya । oṃ hāṃ hāṃ vidyātattvaśivatatvādhipābhyāṃ (l.2) viṣṇurudrābhyāṃ namaḥ । iti saṃpūjya । oṃ lūṃ lūṃ kṣmāmūrtibhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ hāṃ kṣmāmūrtyadhipābhyāṃ śarvābhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ rūṃ rūṃ vahnimūrtibhyāṃ (l.3) namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ hāṃ vahnimūrtyadhipābhyāṃ paśupatibhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ śūṃ śūṃ yajamānamūrtibhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ hāṃ yajamānamūrtyadhipābhyām ugrābhyāṃ (l.4) namaḥ । oṃ ṣūṃ ṣūṃ arkamūrtibhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ hāṃ arkamūrtyadhipābhyāṃ rudrābhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ vūṃ vūṃ jalamūrtibhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ (l.5) vavū(vūṃ) vūṃ jalamūrtyadhipābhyāṃ bhavābhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ yūṃ yūṃ vāyumūrtibhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ oṃ hāṃ vāyumūrtyadhipābhyāṃ īśānābhyāṃ (l.6) namaḥ । oṃ sūṃ sūṃ indumūrtibhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ hāṃ indumūrtyadhipābhyāṃ mahādevābhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ hūṃ hūṃ ākāśamūrtibhyāṃ (l.7) namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ hāṃ ākāśamūrtyadhipābhyāṃ bhīmābhyāṃ namaḥ । iti saṃpūjya । pañcapakṣe – oṃ hāṃ hāṃ pṛthivīmūrtibhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ (p.20a, l.1) hāṃ pṛthivīmūrtyadhipābhyāṃ brahmabhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ hāṃ jalamūrtibhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ hāṃ jalamūrtyadhipābhyāṃ viṣṇubhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ hāṃ (l.2) vahnimūrtibhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ hāṃ vahnimūrtyadhipābhyāṃ rudrābhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ hāṃ vāyumūrtibhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ hāṃ vāyumūrtyadhipābhyāṃ īśvarābhyāṃ namaḥ । (l.3) oṃ hāṃ hāṃ ākāśamūrtibhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ hāṃ ākāśamūrtyadhipābhyāṃ sadāśivābhyāṃ namaḥ । iti saṃdadhyāt । mūrtipāśca sva sva mūrtīnāṃ (l.4) sandhānaṃ kuryuḥ । tataḥ kuṇḍāntikaṃ gatvā । saṃpūjya । sandhānārthaṃ juhuyuḥ । tataḥ śivatatvaṃ tadīśvaraṃ rudraṃ ca pūrvavat sandhānakrameṇa saṃpūjya । (l.5) sannidhāpya saṃpūjya । mūlena pratyekamāhutitrayaṃ datvā । saṃhitāṃ ca pūrvavajjuhuyāt । paścālliṅgasamīpaṃ gatvā । śāntikumbha(l.6)jalena prokṣya । mūlena kuśāgreṇa rudrabhāgaṃ saṃsapṛśya । homasaṅkhyayā japtvā । śāntyambhasā prokṣya । hṛdā sannadhimava(l.7)kuṇṭhanaṃ ca kṛtvā nirudhya । rudramājñāṃ śrāvayet । bhogo rudraśivā jñātaḥ । śivatatvapade tvayā yāvaddoṣotthitaṃ liṅge stheyaṃ (p.20b, f.45a, l.1) tāvadiha prabho । iti vijñāpya । kṣmādimūrti tadīśānakaḥ pṛthivyādimūrtitadīśānāṃ pūrvavat sandhānakrameṇa saṃpūjya । tato puṇḍikāśuddhyarthaṃ kuṇḍāntikaṃ (l.2) gatvā । pūrvavat saṃpūjya । oṃ hāmātmatatvāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ ātmatattvādhipāyai kriyāśaktaye nama ityagnau saṃpūjyāhutitrayaṃ datvā । oṃ hauṃ kṣmāmūrtyai (l.3) namaḥ । kṣmāmūrtyadhipāyai dhārikāyai namaḥ । vahnimūrtaye namaḥ । vahni-mūrtyadhipāyai dīptimatyai namaḥ । yajamānamūrtaye namaḥ । (l.4) yajamānamūrtyadhipāyai ugrāyai namaḥ । arkamūrtaye namaḥ । arkamūrtyadhipāyai jyotsnāyai namaḥ । jalamūrtaye namaḥ । jalamūrtyadhipāyai (l.5) cetanāyai namaḥ । vāyumūrtaye namaḥ । vāyumūrtyadhipāyai balotkaṭāyai namaḥ । indramūrtaye namaḥ । indramūrtyadhipāyai (l.6) dhātryai namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ ākāśamūrtaye namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ ākāśamūrtyadhipāyai vidyāyai namaḥ । iti saṃpūjya । pratyekamāhuti(l.7)trayaṃ datvā । pañcapakṣe । oṃ hāṃ pṛthivīmūrtaye namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ pṛthivīmūrtyadhipāyai vāmāyai namaḥ । jalamūrtaye namaḥ । jalamūrtyadhipāyai (p.21a, l.1) jyeṣṭhāyai namaḥ । vahnimūrtaye namaḥ । vahnimūrtyadhipāyai kriyāyai namaḥ । vāyumūrtaye namaḥ । vāyumūrtyadhipāyai jñānyai namaḥ । ākāśa(l.2)mūrtaye namaḥ । ākāśamūrtyadhipāyai icchāyai namaḥ । iti saṃpūjya । pratyekamāhutitrayaṃ dadyāt । oṃ hrīṃ kriyāśaktaye svāhetyāhutiśataṃ pūrṇāsahitaṃ juhuyāt । (l.3) tato guruḥ śāntikumbhamādāya । pīṭhasamīpaṃ gatvā । śāntikumbhajalena pīṭhaṃ saṃprokṣya । mūlamantreṇa kuśamūlena (l.4) pīṭhamūlabhāgaṃ saṃspṛśya । homasaṅkhyayā japtvā । śāntikumbhajalena prokṣya । guruḥ paścāddhṛdaiva teṣāṃ saridhiṃ kṛtvā । (l.5) varmaṇāvakuṇṭhya nirudhya । tattvādhipamājñāṃ śrāvayet । tattvādhipe kriyāśakte yāvaddoṣasamutthitaṃ pīṭhaṃ tāvat tvayātraiva (l.6) sthātavyaṃ sannidhānata iti । oṃ hrāṃ hrīṃ ātmatattvavidyātattvābhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ ātmatattvavidyātattvādhipābhyāṃ (l.7) kriyājñānābhyāṃ namaḥ । iti saṃpūjya । oṃ hāṃ hāṃ kṣmāmūrtibhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ hāṃ kṣmāmūrtyadhipābhyāṃ dhārikābhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ (p.21b, f.46a, l.1) hāṃ hāṃ vahnimūrtibhyāṃ namaḥ । vahnimūrtyadhipābhyāṃ dīptimatyābhyāṃ namaḥ । yajamānamūrtibhyāṃ namaḥ । yajamānamūrtyadhipābhyām ugrābhyāṃ namaḥ । arkamūrtibhyāṃ (l.2) namaḥ । arkamūrtyadhipābhyāṃ jyotsnābhyāṃ namaḥ । jalamūrtibhyāṃ namaḥ । jalamūrtyadhipābhyāṃ cetanābhyāṃ namaḥ । vāyu-mūrtibhyāṃ namaḥ । vāyumūrtyadhipābhyāṃ (l.3) balotkaṭābhyāṃ namaḥ । indumūrtibhyāṃ namaḥ । indumūrtyadhipābhyāṃ dhārikābhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ hāṃ ākāśamūrtibhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ hāṃ (l.4) ākāśamūrtyadhipābhyāṃ vidyābhyāṃ namaḥ । iti sandadhyāt । pañcapakṣe । oṃ hāṃ hāṃ pṛthivīmūrtibhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ hāṃ pṛthivīmūrtya(l.5)dhipābhyāṃ vāmābhyāṃ namaḥ । indumūrtibhyāṃ namaḥ । indumūrtyadhipābhyāṃ dhārikābhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ hāṃ ākāśamūrtibhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ hāṃ (l.6) ākāśamūrtyadhipābhyām icchābhyāṃ namaḥ । iti sandhāya । kuṇḍāntikaṃ gatvā । saṃpūjya । sannidhānārthaṃ juhuyuḥ । tato vidyātattvaṃ tadīśvarīṃ (l.7) jñānīṃ ca saṃpūjya । sannidhāpya । svāhāntena pratyekamāhutitrayaṃ datvā । śaktimūlamantreṇa pūrvavacchataṃ pūrṇāsahitaṃ juhuyāt । paścāt (p.22a, l.1) pīṭhasamīpaṃ gatvā । pūrvavacchāntikumbhāmbhaḥ prokṣaṇapūrvaṃ mūlamantreṇa kuśamadhyena pīṭhamadhyabhāgaṃ saṃspṛśya । homasaṅkhyayā japtvā (l.2) śāntyambhasā prokṣya । hṛdā sannadhimavakuṇṭhanaṃ nirodhanaṃ ca kṛtvā । jñānaśaktyādhipamājñāṃ śrāvayet ।

vidyātattvādhipe jñānī yāvaddoṣasamutthitam । (l.3)
pīṭhaṃ tāvattvātraiva sthātavyaṃ sannidhānataḥ ॥ iti ।

	tataḥ – oṃ hrīṃ hūṃ vidyātattvaśivatattvābhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ hāṃ vidyātattvaśivatattvābhyāṃ jñānīcchābhyāṃ namaḥ । (l.4) iti saṃpūjya । kṣmādimūrti tadīśānāḥ pūrvavat sandhānaṃ kṛtvā । kuṇḍāntikaṃ gatvā । saṃpūjya । sandhānārthaṃ juhuyuḥ । tataḥ śivatattvānta(ntata)dīśvarīm icchāśaktiṃ ca (l.5) saṃpūjya । sannidhāpya । pratyekamāhutitrayaṃ datvā । mūlamantreṇa śataṃ pūrṇāsahitaṃ juhuyāt । tataḥ pīṭhasamīpaṃ gatvā । śāntikumbhajalena (l.6) prokṣya mūlamantreṇa kuśāgreṇa pīṭhāgrabhāgaṃ saṃspṛśya । homasaṅkhyayā japtvā । śāntyambhasā prokṣya । hṛdā sannadhiṃ kṛtvāvakuṇṭhya । (l.7) nirudhya । tattvādhipamājñāṃ śrāvayet ।

jñānaśakte tvayā jñātaḥ śivatattvaṃ niyantrake । 
yāvaddoṣotthitaṃ pīṭha stheyaṃ tāvadiheśvarī ॥ iti । (l.8)

	evaṃ pīṭhavadādhāraśilāyāmapi sarvaṃ kṛtvā । homasamaye । oṃ sūṃ ādhāraśaktyai svāheti hutvā । japamapi kṛtvā । nirodhayet । vṛṣabhe'pi (p.22b, f.47a, l.1) pūrvavat । kuṇḍasamīpaṃ gatvādhāraśaktinyāsapūrvakaṃ tattvatattvādhipamūrtimūrtīśvarārcanāhutisamarpaṇādi kṛtvā । oṃ hrīṃ vṛṣabhāya svāheti śataṃ pūrṇāsahitaṃ hutvā (l.2) tatsamīpaṃ gatvā । śāntyambhasā prokṣaṇa kuśamūlamadhyāgrasparśanajapakaraṇatatvayoḥ sandhānāni kṛtvā । gatvā । hutvāgatya sarvaṃ pūrvavadācaret । tato liṅgamūlādidoṣa(l.3)nirharaṇārthaṃ śivenāghoreṇāsinā śataṃ śataṃ pūrṇāsahitaṃ hutvā । caruṃ datvā । pūrṇāṃ vidhāyācamanatāmbūlādi datvā । śivaṃ nirudhya vyāhṛtiṃ (l.4) hutvāntarbaliṃ datvā । śrotravandanaṃ kṛtvā । kasalīkṛtya । śivasamīpaṃ gatvā । śivasya dakṣiṇaśravaṇe – 

bhagavanjñānato mohāt brahmādyamśatraye'pi yat । (l.5)
kṛtaṃ karma mayā nyūnamadhikaṃ bhaktavatsala ॥
tatsarvamastu saṃpūrṇaṃ tvatprasādādihādhvare । 
oṃ namo bhagavate tubhyaṃ rudraraudra namo'stu te । (l.6)
karma pūrṇamapūrṇaṃ vā svaśaktyā pūrya gṛhyatām ॥ iti vijñāpya ।

	jñānaśaktiṃ vinyasya । nirudhyāt । pīṭhasamīpaṃ gatvā । bhagavatī mayā bhāgatraye'pi (l.7) karma nivṛttiṃ tvatprasādāt saṃpūrṇatāṃ yātu । oṃ hrīṃ śaṅkarī pūraya pūraya svāheti vijñāpya । kriyāśaktiṃ vinyasya । nirudhyāt । brahmaśilāyāmapi (l.8) sarvakarma pūrṇaṃ pūrya gṛhyatāmiti vijñāpya । oṃ sūṃ ādhāraśaktaye nama ityādhāraśaktiṃ vinyasya nirudhyāt । vṛṣe'pi vijñāpya । oṃ hrīṃ vṛṣabhāya (p.23a, adhivāsānantarakriyā, l.1) namaḥ । iti vṛṣaṃ nirudhyāt ।

homairvedādighoṣaiśca nṛttagītamahotsavaiḥ । 
bhaktitaḥ saptarātraṃ vā pañcarātraṃ trirātrakam ॥
ekarātramadho sadyaḥ kuryā*ddevādhivāsanam ।* (l.2) 
hotavyaṃ pratyahaṃ sva sva mantrairhomaśataṃ śatam ॥ 
śivakumbhādipūjāṃ ca digbaliṃ ca nivedayet । 
ācāryo mūrtipaiḥ sākaṃ pañcagavyādikaṃ *pibet ॥* (l.3)
	iti bahudivasapakṣe kriyākāṇḍe madhyāṃśena svapenmantrī mūrtipaistadanantaram iti śivālayanirmāṇadīpikāyāṃ (l.4) pratiṣṭhādhivāsanavidhiḥ samāptaḥ ॥

	atha prātaḥ samutthāya । kṛtanityavidhiḥ । guruḥ sūryadvārapālapūjā bhūtaśuddhyantaryāga(l.5)viśeṣārghyakaraṇadravyābhimantreṇa śivahastakaraṇaśivabhāvālokapālaśivakumbhavardhanīgaṇapatigurusthaṇḍilapūjāṃ (l.6) kṛtvā । labdhānujño huṃ phaḍantāstramuccaran । prāsādaṃ gatvā । vighnānuccāṭya । astreṇa saṃprokṣya । śivamaṣṭapuṣpikayā (l.7) saṃpūjya । oṃ sūṃ ādhāraśaktye namaḥ । iti saṃpūjya । oṃ namo vyāpinī sthire acale dhṛve laṃ hrīṃ laṃ svāhā । japtvābhyarcya । (p.23b, f.48a, l.1) tvameva paramāśakti । tvamevāsanadhārikā । śivājñayā tvayā devī sthātavyamiha santatamiti vijñāpya । tataḥ kuṇḍasamīpaṃ gatvāstraiṇa prokṣya । mekhalāsu – (l.2) brahmādīnindrādīn lokapālāṃśca saṃpūjya । sṛksṛvājyaṃ ca pūrvavat saṃskṛtya । pūrṇāṃ datvā । vahnau pūrvavacchivaṃ saṃpūjya । mantratarpaṇaṃ kṛtvā । (l.3) śivenāsinā śataṃ śataṃ pūrṇāsahitaṃ juhuyāt । tato śivasannadhiṃ prāpya । sthirobhavetyuktvā sthirībhūte liṅge mastakāddhasta(l.4)muccārya । dhyātvā । sakalarūpiṇaṃ vibhuṃ prāsādamuccārya । yāvanniṣkalagocaraṃ śaktyante saṃsthitaṃ nītvā । tanmūrtau niṣkalaṃ (l.5) nyaset ।

kriyāśakti kṛtādhārajñānaśaktiprakalpitam । 
jñeyādheyasvarūpeṇa sthātavyamiti tatvataḥ ॥ 
āvāhya sammukhaṃ *dhyātvā sānnidhyaṃ sannirodhanam ।* (l.6)
datvā pādyamukhārghyaṃ ca puṣpeṇaiva samarcayet ॥

	oṃ hrīṃ hauṃ kriyāśaktiśivābhyāṃ namaḥ । (l.7) iti liṅgamudrayā pīṭhe liṅge ca saṃpūjya । mumukṣostu – oṃ hauṃ hrīṃ śivaśaktibhyāṃ nama iti liṅgapīṭhe saṃspṛśyādhvaṣaṭkaṃ (p.24a, l.1) nyaset । evaṃ saṃpūjya । kṣmāvahniyajamānārkajala-vāyvindvākāśākhyāmūrtiḥ śarvapaśupatyugrarudrabhaveśānamahādevabhīmākhyān pūjayet । pañca(l.2)pakṣe । pṛthivījalavahnivāyvākāśākhyamūrtiḥ brahmaviṣṇurudreśvarasadāśivākhyāmūrtīśvarānvinyasyādhāraśaktyādiśaktyantamantrān pīṭhe (l.3) vinyasya । liṅge mūrtiṃ brahmāṇi daṇḍabhaṅgyā ca vinyasyāṣṭatriṃśatkalānyāsaṃ vidhāya । vidyādehamāpādya netre nyāsāntaraṃ mūlenāvāhya (l.4) saṃsthāpya । sannidhāpya । nirodhyāṅgāni vinyasya । pādyācamanārghyapuṣpadānāni vidhāya । oṃ hauṃ sauṃ jñānaśaktaye nama iti (l.5) liṅge vinyasya । oṃ hrīṃ kriyāśaktaye nama iti pīṭhe vinyasya । nirodhya । dhūpadīpau datvā । śāntyambhasā ca śaṅkhajalena ca (l.6) saṃsnāpya candanādinā sarvato vilipya । vāsasā paridhāpya । hārakuṇḍalamakuṭayajñopavītapaṭṭādyairalaṅkṛtya (l.7) dhūpadīpau datvā layāṅgamabhyarcya । bhogāṅgāni saṃpūjya । śeṣaṃ pañcamāvaraṇaparyantaṃ saṃpūjyārghyaṃ datvā । naivedyādikaṃ datvā । dhūpadīpā(p.24b, f.49a, l.1)rātrikadarpaṇacchatracāmarairgeyanṛttādibhiḥ santoṣya । japtvā । nivedya । tataḥ śivaṃ vijñāpayet । candrārkatārakaṃ yāvat mantreśairmūrtipaiḥ saha । (l.2) svecchayaiva tvayā nātha sthātavyamiha mandire iti prārthya । tadanujñām avāpya । sāṣṭāṅgaṃ praṇamya bahirgatvā । vṛṣabhaṃ hṛdayena praṇavena vā saṃpūjya । kuṇḍasamīpaṃ (l.3) gatvā । guruḥ śāntyarthamarkacandrabhaumabudhabṛhaspatiśukraśanirāhuketūnāmarkapalāśakhādirāpāmārgāśvatthodumbaraśamī(l.4)dūrvākuśaistilairvā mṛtyuñjayamantraiḥ mṛtyuñjitapāyasena vā mūlamantreṇomāmantrābhyāṃ ca śataṃ hutvā । prāyaścittārthaṃ (l.5) mūlena vāghoreṇa vā śataṃ hutvā caruṃ nivedya । pūrṇāṃ datvācamanādi datvā nirudhya । bhūrādibhirhutvāntarbaliṃ datvācamyāntaḥpraviśya । (l.6) pradakṣiṇīkṛtya । praṇamya । śivaṃ vijñāpayet । 

jñānādajñānato vāpi śāstroktena kṛtaṃ mayā ।
makhe'smin karma vākkāya*mano vai gṛhyataḥ prabho ॥* (l.7)

	tatsarvaṃ tvatprasādena saṃpūrṇaṃ bhavatāṃ mameti vijñāpya । tato rājādhi(di)kartā cet hiraṇyagrāmādidāsī(p.25a, l.1)dāsaparyantaṃ pūjārthaṃ nṛttagītādihetave mukeśvarāyeti nivedayet । rājavyatiriktatajanaḥ svavittasya ṣaṣṭhāṃśaṃ nivedayet । tato (l.2) kuṇḍasamīpaṃ gatvāghoreṇāsinā śivenāsinā śataṃ śataṃ hunet । śivāstrāghorāstrapāśupatāstramūlamantraiḥ sāvitrī gāyatrī (l.3) pratyaṅgiramantraiḥ pṛthak pṛthak sahasramardhaṃ śatam aṣṭāviṃśatisaṅkhyā vā juhuyāt । vāgīvāgīśvarīmantreṇa sahasraṃ śataṃ (l.4) pūrṇāsahitaṃ juhuyāt । tadanu – vedyāṃ pūrve । śivakumbhavat kumbhadvayaṃ saṃsthāpya । saṃpūjya । śivasamīpaṃ gatvā । homa(maṃ) (l.5) samarpayet । gāyatryā śivaṃ saṃpūjya । tataḥ kuṇḍasamīpaṃ gatvā । karmasaṃpūraṇārthaṃ sthirādyāhutisaptakaṃ dadyāt । oṃ śiva sthiro (l.6) bhava svāhā । oṃ śiva aprameyo bhava svāhā । oṃ śiva anādibodho bhava svāhā । oṃ śiva nityo bhava svāhā । oṃ śiva vyāpako (l.7) bhava svāhā । oṃ śiva avinaśvaro bhava svāhā । oṃ śiva tṛpto bhava svāhā । ityāhutisaptakaṃ dadyāt । kṛtākṛtapūraṇārthaṃ (p.25b, f.50a, prāṇapratiṣṭhai, l.1) punaḥ sahasramardhaṃ śataṃ vāghoreṇāsinā śivena pūrṇāsahitaṃ juhuyāt । tato'agnisthadevasya tāmbūlādi datvāṣṭapuṣpikayā saṃpūjyārghyaṃ (l.2) datvāgnisthaṃ śivakumbhe saṃyojya । bhūrādibhirhutvā । antarbaliṃ bahirbaliṃ ca kṛtvā । sthaṇḍilasthamapi kumbhe saṃyojya । godānādidaśadānāni (l.3) kṛtvā । kumbhasthamapi liṅge śive saṃyojayet । kumbha sthāpitakumbhayoḥ ekaikena śive saṃsnāpya । vidyeśvarādi(l.4)kalaśān tattanmantre tattatsthāne'bhiṣicya । tato śivaṃ gandhamālyādinālaṅkṛtya । prāṇapratiṣṭhā jīvanyā(nyāsā)dikaṃ kṛtvā । oṃ prāṇa(l.5)pritiṣṭhāmantrasya brahmaviṣṇumaheśvaraṛṣayaḥ । ṛgyejussāmaadharvaṇacchandāṃsi sakalabhuvanasṛṣṭikāraṇaprāṇapratiṣṭhā parāśaktirdevatā । (l.6) āṃ bījaṃ krauṃ śaktiḥ mantraprāṇapratiṣṭhārthe viniyogaḥ । dhyānam –

raktāmbodhaiḥ sabodhairlasitadaruṇāsarojātirūḍhaiḥ karābjaiḥ ।
pāśāṃ kodaṇḍamikṣurbhavamaṇiguṇamapyaṅguśaṃ pañcbāṇān bibhrāṇām ॥
sṛkkapālaṃ triṇayanavilasat pīna*vakṣoruhāḍhyām ।* (p.26a, l.1)
devīṃ bālārkaśubhrāvarṇabahunibhā prāṇaśaktiḥ parānnaḥ ॥

	oṃ hāṃ hrīṃ krauṃ ya ra la va śa ṣa sa ha so'haṃ haṃ saḥ amuṣyamāṇa mahāprāṇa oṃ āṃ (l.2) hrīṃ krauṃ ya ra la va śa ṣa sa haḥ so'haṃ haṃ saḥ amuṣya jīva iha sthitaḥ āṃ hrīṃ kroṃ ya ra la va śa ṣa sa haḥ sauṃ haṃ haṃ saḥ amuṣya sarve trayāṇāṃ vāṅmanaścakṣuśrotra(l.3)jihvāghrāṇāṃ tvakcarmamāṃsarudhira-medosthimajjāśuklā ihaivāyāntu ihaivāgatyā(tya) ihaiva sukhaṃ ciraṃ tiṣṭhantu śrīṃ hrīṃ klīṃ haṃ saḥ so'haṃ (l.4) saḥ so'haṃsa svāhā । āṃ kroṃ ya ra la va śa ṣa sa ha so'haṃ saḥ āṃ iṃ vaṃ gaṃ ghaṃ kaṃ ākāśādvāyuragnerāpaḥ pṛthivyātmane aḥ (l.5) hṛdayāya namaḥ । āṃ krīṃ ya ra la va śa ṣa sa ha so'haṃ īm jñaṃ jhaṃ jaṃ chaṃ caṃ śabdasparśarūparasagandhātmane maṃ śirase svāhā । āṃ (l.6) krīṃ kroṃ ya ra la va śa ṣa sa ha so'haṃ haṃ saḥ uṃ ūṃ ḍhaṃ ḍaṃ ṭhaṃ ṭaṃ śrotratvakcakṣurmāṃsarudhiramedosthimārja(majjā)śuklātmane uṃ śikhāyai (l.7) vauṣaṭ । āṃ krīṃ kroṃ ya ra la va śa ṣa sa ha so'haṃ haṃ saḥ aiṃ naṃ dhaṃ daṃ thaṃ taṃ vākpādapāṇipāyurupasthātmane aiṃ kavacāya hum । āṃ (p.126b, f.51a, l.1) hrīṃ kroṃ hrīṃ kroṃ ya ra la va śa ṣa sa ha so'haṃ haṃ saḥ oṃ maṃ bhaṃ baṃ phaṃ paṃ vacanagamanadānavisargānandātmane oṃ netratrayāya vauṣaṭ । āṃ hrīṃ kroṃ ya ra la (l.2) va śa ṣa sa haḥ so'haṃ haṃ saḥ । aṃ kṣaṃ laṃ haṃ saḥ ṛṃ ṛṃ ḷṃ ḹṃ ṣaṃ saḥ so'haṃ haṃ saḥ amuṣya sarvendriyāṇāṃ vāṅmanaścakṣuśrotrajihvāghrāṇamanobuddhi(l.3)ahaṅkāracittātmane aṃ astrāya phaṭ । sarvapratiṣṭhānyāsaḥ । oṃ āṃ kroṃ oṃ hāṃ hauṃ yaṃ raṃ laṃ vaṃ śaṃ ṣaṃ saṃ haṃ ḻaṃ kṣaṃ amuṣyaprāṇa iha prāṇa (l.4) amuṣya jīva iha sthitaḥ amuṣya sarvendriyāṇi vāṅmanaḥ kāyakarmabhiḥ śrotratvakcakṣujihvāghrāṇaihaivāgatyā(tya) sukhaṃ ciraṃ tiṣṭhantu (l.5) sarvalokamukhaṃ me vaśamānaya svāhā । oṃ kaṃ iṃkāraṃ vyomavyāpine oṃ evaṃ daśākṣaraṃ nyāsaṃ brahmakūrmāsanasthitam । 

oṃkāraṃ prathamaṃ *bījaṃ haṃkāraṃ tu dvitīyakam ।* (l.6)
tṛtīyaṃ bījamūkāraṃ sarvakāraṇakāraṇam ॥
caturthabījamaṃkāraṃ oṃkāraṃ pañcamaṃ bhavet ।
akāraṃ ṣaṣṭhamevaṃ syāt *vakāraṃ saptamaṃ tathā ॥* (l.7)
sakāraṃ aṣṭamaṃ bījaṃ nakāraṃ navamaṃ smṛtam ।
okāraṃ daśamaṃ bījaṃ sarvātmā veti viśrutaḥ ॥
amuṣya prāṇa iha prāṇa amuṣya (p.27a, jīvanyāsam, l.1) jīva iha sthitaḥ । amuṣya sarvendriyāṇi vāṅmanaśrotratvakcacakṣujihvāghrāṇa iha ihaivendriyāya svāhā । 
॥ iti sakalaniṣkalasaṃvāde paramarahasya(l.2)prāṇapratiṣṭhā samāptā ॥ hariḥ om ॥

jīvanyāsam –
	oṃ vyomavyāpine vyomarūpāya sarvavyāpine śivāya anantāya anāthāya (l.3) anāśra(śri)tāya dhṛvāya śāśvatāya nityayogine dhyānaharāya oṃ namaśśivāya sarvaprabhave śivāya īśānamūrdhāya tatpuruṣavaktrāya (l.4) aghorahṛdayāya vāma-devaguhyāya sadyojātamūrtaye oṃ namo namaḥ । guhyādiguhyāya goptre anidhanidhanāya nidhano(l.5)dbhavāya sarvayogādhikṛtāya sarvavidyāparāya jyotīrūpāya parameśvaraparāya cetanācetana vyomin vyomin (l.6) vyāpin vyāpin arūpannarūpin prathama prathama tejasteja jyotirjyoti arūpinnarūpinagni adhūmābhasma anāte(the')nāte(the) nānā (l.7) nānā dhūdhū dhūdhū oṃ bhūḥ oṃ bhuvaḥ oṃ suvaḥ । oṃ anidha nidhanodbhava sarvabhūtasugha(kha)prada sarvasānnidhyaparā brahmaviṣṇurudrānanta-(p.27b, f.52a, akṣaranyāsam, l.1)paraḥ arcitānarcita stuta stutapūrva sthitapūrva sākṣi sākṣi turu turu pataṅga pataṅga piṅga piṅga jñāna jñāna śabda śabda sūkṣma sūkṣma śiva śarva oṃ namaśśivāya oṃ namo (l.2) namaḥ । oṃ aṃ uṃ oṃ gaṃ saṃ raṃ brahmaṇeśāya namaḥ । guhye । śiṃ rudrāya namaḥ । udare । maṃ viṣṇave namaḥ । nābhau । vaṃ parāśakti(kta)ye namaḥ । lalāṭe । yaṃ (l.3) tāṇḍavasadāśivāya randhre niṃ digbandhanaḥ dhenumudrāṃ darśayet । namaśśivāya । naṃ maṃ śiṃ vāṃ yaṃ ityakṣarapañca । oṃ naṃ pṛthivyai (l.4) namaḥ । savyabāhu । oṃ maṃ appuve namaḥ । vāmabāhu । oṃ śiṃ analāya namaḥ । hṛdaye । oṃ vaṃ vāyave namaḥ । mukhe । oṃ (l.5) yaṃ ākāśāya namaḥ । mūrdhni । iti aṅguṣṭhatarjanīyuktaṃ nyāset ।

akāraṃ mūrdhni vinyasyāt ākāraṃ tu lalāṭake ।
ikāraṃ *dakṣiṇe netraṃ īkāraṃ vāmanetrakam ॥* (l.6)
ukāraṃ dakṣiṇe śrotre ūkāraṃ vāmaśrotrake ।
ṛkāraṃ dakṣiṇe nāse ṝkāraṃ vāmanāsike ॥ (l.7)
ḷkāraṃ dakṣiṇe karṇe ḹkāraṃ vāmakarṇake ।
e ai oṣṭhadvayo nyasyāt o au dantadvayorapi ॥
aṃ tālumūlake nyasyāt aḥ agragrata kramāt । (p.28a, l.1)
kavargaṃ dakṣiṇe haste cavargaṃ vāmahastake ॥
ṭavargaṃ dakṣiṇe pādau tavargaṃ vāmapādayoḥ ।
paphau tu dakṣiṇe pārśve babhau tu vāmapārśvake ॥ (l.2)
makāraṃ dakṣiṇe pṛṣṭhau yakāraṃ vāmapṛṣṭhake ।
raṃ laṃ kaṭideśetu vakāraṃ kaṭisūtrake ॥
śakāraṃ ca ṣakāraṃ ca kuṇḍalopari vinyaset ।
sakāraṃ hṛdayaṃ nyasyāt *hakāraṃ makuṭaṃ nyaset ॥* (l.3)
ḻakāraṃ meṭhra(ḍhra)deśe tu kṣakāraṃ nābhideśake ।

antarmātṛkai –

oṃ hāṃ sadāśivātmane namaḥ । vaktram । (l.4) oṃ rudrātmane namaḥ । hṛdayam । oṃ viṣṇuvātmane namaḥ । udaram । oṃ brahmaṇe namaḥ । pādam । oṃ candramaṇḍalāya namaḥ । āsyam । oṃ ādityamaṇḍalāya (l.5) namaḥ । urasi । oṃ hāṃ haṃ agni-maṇḍalāya namaḥ । nābhau । yaśakti japtvā naivedyadhūpadīpāntaṃ saṃpūjya । (l.6) pracuropacāreṇa saṃpūjya । tataḥ kuṇḍāntikaṃ gatvāgnisthaṃ visṛjya । lokapālakumbhādīn visṛjya । svahastatoyordhvakṣepeṇa (l.7) maṇṭapaṃ visṛjyācamya । sakalīkṛtya । śivāntikaṃ gatvā । karmaṃ(rma) samarpya । karmasamāptiṃ vijñāpya । rudrādibhyo baliṃ datvā । tataḥ sva dakṣiṇa(p.28b, f.53a, antarbali, l.1)digbhāge maṇḍaladvayaṃ niṣpādya । prathamamaṇḍale pūrvādyuttarāntadikṣu oṃ hāṃ rudrebhyo namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ mātṛbhyo namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ gaṇebhyo namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ (l.2) yakṣebhyo nama itīśānādikoṇeṣu – oṃ hāṃ gṛhebhyo namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ asurebhyo namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ rākṣasebhyo namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ nāgebhyo (l.3) namaḥ । maṇḍalamadhye – īśānāgneyanaiṛteṣu oṃ hāṃ nakṣatrebhyo namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ rāśibhyo namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ viśvebhyo namaḥ । (l.4) vāyurvaruṇayormadhye – oṃ hāṃ kṣetrapālāya namaḥ । ityabhyarcya । svāhāntamarghyaṃ datvā । dvitīyamaṇḍale – pūrvādīśāntadikṣu – oṃ hāṃ indrāya namaḥ । (l.5) īśānāya namaḥ । aiśānyām – brahmaṇe namaḥ । naiṛtyām – viṣṇave namaḥ । madhye – nīlalohitāya namaḥ । iti saṃpūjyārghyaṃ datvā । pūrvaṃ rudrādibhyo baliṃ svāhāntaṃ (l.6) kṣiptvācamanaṃ datvā । indrādibhyo tathaiva balyācamanaṃ datvā bahiḥ । tānnirodhayet ।

yāvatkālaṃ mahādevo liṅgamāśra(śri)tya tiṣṭhati । 
tāvatkālaṃ ca *rakṣārthaṃ yūyaṃ tiṣṭhanti sarvadā ॥* (l.7)
	iti vijñāpya । nirmālyamapanīya । pātre nidhāya । śivājñāṃ samādāya । caṇḍeśvaramāsādya । nirmālyaṃ nivedya (p.29a, l.1) brūyāt । 

evaṃ śivasya dravyaṃ ca rakṣan tiṣṭha śivāntike । 
vastrādyaṃ bhuṣaṇaṃ tyaktvā nirmālyaṃ tava vartanam ॥
devaḥ sannihito yāvat tāvat sannidhirbhava । (l.2) 
nyūnādhikaṃ ca yatkiñcit kṛtamajñānato mayā ॥
tvatprasādena caṇḍeśa tatsarvaṃ paripūraya । 
iti vijñāpya । bahirnirgatya । tataḥ śuddhasthāne yajamānaṃ (l.3) sabandhubhāryāsahitaṃ saparikaraṃ sa(saṃ)sthāpya । śiṣṭaśāntighaṭajalaiḥ pūrvasthāpitakumbhajalena maṅgalavādyaiḥ saṃsnāpayet । (l.4) 
sthāpakaṃ yajamāno'pi saṃpūjya ca maheśavat ।
vittaśāṭhyaṃ vinā dadyāt bhūhiraṇyādidakṣiṇām ॥
yathā santoṣamāyāti tathā *pūjyo gurustadā ।* (l.5)
asantuṣṭau gurau yasmā(smāt) na kartuḥ tatphalaṃ labhet ॥
mūrtipānvidhivatpaścājjāpakān *brāhmaṇānstathā(n tathā) ।* (l.6)
dīnāndhakṛpaṇebhyaśca yathāvittānusārataḥ ॥
pratimāliṅgavedīnāṃ yāvanti paramāṇavaḥ । (l.7)
tāvadyugasahasrāṇi kartuḥ bhogaḥ bhujaḥ phalam ॥
sarvayajñatapodānaṃ sarvatīrtheṣu yatphalam ।
tatphalaṃ koṭi*guṇitaṃ sthāpya liṅgaṃ labhennaraḥ ॥* (p.29b, f.54a, l.1)
tāvadyugaliṅge ca sthāpito mokṣo bhūtayaśca nirantarāḥ ।
pitṛjaṃ mātṛjaṃ sapta sapta cātmabhavaṃ tathā ॥ (l.2)
kulaikaviṃśamuttārya tato gacchet parāṃ gatim ।
yadatra sumukhībhāvo vedito bhagavān mayā ॥
kṣamasva nātha tatsarvaṃ kāruṇyāmbunidhe mama । iti kṣamārpaṇaṃ kṛtvā । 
dīnāndhakṛpaṇānsarvān yajamānaḥ prapūjayet ।
tato guruḥ pāśupataṃ japtvā taṃ parameśvaraṃ pradakṣiṇatrayaṃ (l.4) kṛtvā praṇamya svagṛhaṃ labhet । 
॥ iti śivālayanirmāṇadīpikāyāṃ pratiṣṭhāvidhiḥ samāptā(ptaḥ) ॥ śubhamasattu ॥ (l.5)

ṛgvedaṃ diśi pūrvāyāṃ yajurvedaṃ tu dakṣiṇe ।
sāmamuttaradeśe tu paścime'tharvameva ca ॥
prāsādamīśakoṇe tu *aghoraṃ vahnigocare ।* (l.6)
pañcākṣaraṃ ca naiṛtyām umābījaṃ tu vāyave ॥
śivasaṅkalpasūktaṃ ca tato vā rudrasūktakam ।
kadrudrāya *tvamagne ...(ca) pūrvabhāge japedbudhaḥ ॥* (p.30a, ṣaḍu(ḍa)dhvārcanai, l.1)
śrīsūktaṃ pavamānaṃ ca puruṣasūktaṃ triyambakam ।

॥ ṣaḍadhvārcanai ॥ - ॥ trikhaṇḍamarcanai ॥
rudrabhāge – (l.2) oṃ hauṃ śāntyatītakalāyai namaḥ । tattvam । oṃ śivatattvāya namaḥ । bhuvanam । anāśritāyai namaḥ । anantāya namaḥ । onāthāya namaḥ । vyomarūpāya (l.3) namaḥ । vyomavyāpine namaḥ । ūrdhvagāminyai namaḥ । rocikāyai namaḥ । mocikāyai namaḥ । dīpikāyai namaḥ । indhikāyai (l.4) namaḥ । 
kalai – śāntyatītāyai (namaḥ) । śāntyai namaḥ । vidyāyai (namaḥ) । pratiṣṭhāyai (namaḥ) । nivṛtyai namaḥ । 
varṇam – annamaḥ । ānnamaḥ । padam 16. oṃ namaḥ । (l.5) 
mantram – oṃ hāṃ hauṃ śivāya namaḥ । oṃ haum īśānamūrdhāya namaḥ । oṃ haḥ astrāya namaḥ । 
bīja(jam) – haṃkārabījābhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ (l.6) kuhu śaṅkhani nāḍibhyāṃ namaḥ । devadattadhanañjayābhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ sadāśivāya kāraṇeśvarāya namaḥ । 
viṣṇubhāge – oṃ śānti(p.30b, f.55a, l.1)kalāyai namaḥ । 
tattvam – sadāśivatattvāya namaḥ । īśvaratattvāya namaḥ । śuddhavidyātattvāya namaḥ । 3 ॥ 
bhuvanam – sadāśivāya namaḥ । anantāya namaḥ । sūkṣmāya (l.2) namaḥ । śivottamāya namaḥ । ekanetrāya (namaḥ) । ekarudrāya namaḥ । trimūrtaye namaḥ । śrīkaṇṭhāya namaḥ । śikhaṇḍine namaḥ । vāmāyai namaḥ । jyeṣṭhāyai (l.3) namaḥ । raudryai namaḥ । kālyai namaḥ । kalavikaraṇyai namaḥ । balavikaraṇyai namaḥ । balapramathanyai namaḥ । sarvabhūtadamanyai (l.4) namaḥ । manonmanyai namaḥ । 18. 

varṇam – kaṃ namaḥ । kha(khaṃ namaḥ) । ga(gaṃ namaḥ) । 3. padam – vyomavyāpine namaḥ । vyomarūpiṇe namaḥ । sarva(l.5)vyāpine namaḥ । śivāya namaḥ ॥ (...?) anantāya namaḥ । anāthāya namaḥ । anāśra(śri)tāya (namaḥ) । dhṛ(dhru)vāya (namaḥ) । śāśvatāya (namaḥ) । yogapīṭhasaṃsthitāya (namaḥ) । (l.6) nityayogine namaḥ । 11. 
mantram – oṃ heṃ tatpuruṣavaktrāya namaḥ । oṃ haiṃkavacāya namaḥ । 2. 
bījam – yakārabījāya namaḥ । alampūṣāya (l.7) nāḍibhyāṃ namaḥ । kūrmakṛkaravāyubhyāṃ namaḥ । īśvarāya kāraṇeśvarāya namaḥ । viṣṇubhāgaṃ samāptam ॥ (p.31a, l.1)

brahmabhāge – trikā(ka)lā । oṃ sūṃ vidyākalāyai namaḥ ।

tattvam – kālatattvāya (namaḥ) । kalātattvāya (namaḥ) । vidyātattvāya (namaḥ) । rāgatattvāya (namaḥ) । puruṣatattvāya (namaḥ) । (l.2) māyātattvāya (namaḥ) । 

bhuvanam – aṅguṣṭhāya namaḥ । mātṛkāya (namaḥ) । īśānāya (namaḥ) । ekekṣaṇāya (namaḥ) । piṅgalāya (namaḥ) । udbhavāya (namaḥ) । bhavāya (namaḥ) । vāmāya (namaḥ) । mahātejāya (namaḥ) । (l.3) mahāstutyai namaḥ । śikhedāya (namaḥ) । ekavīrāya (namaḥ) । pañcāntakāya (namaḥ) । lakulīśvarāya (namaḥ) । saṃvartāya (namaḥ) । krodhāya (namaḥ) । caṇḍāya namaḥ । jyotiṣāya (l.4) (namaḥ) । ekaśikhāya (namaḥ) । anantāya namaḥ । ajāya namaḥ । umāpataye (namaḥ) । pracaṇḍāya (namaḥ) । īśānāya (namaḥ) । bhavāya (namaḥ), ugrāya (namaḥ) । bhīmāya (namaḥ) । vāmāya (namaḥ) । ū varṇam । (l.5) ghaṃ namaḥ । ṅaṃ (namaḥ) । caṃ (namaḥ) । chaṃ (namaḥ) । jaṃ (namaḥ) । jhaṃ (namaḥ) । ñaṃ namaḥ ॥7 ॥ 

padam – dhyānaharāya namaḥ । śivāya (namaḥ) । sarvaprabhave namaḥ । śivāya (namaḥ) । īśānamūrdhāya (namaḥ) । (l.6) tatpuruṣavaktrāya (namaḥ) । oghorahṛdayāya (namaḥ) । vāmadevaguhyāya (namaḥ) । sadyojātamūrtaye (namaḥ) । oṃ namo namaḥ । guhyātiguhyāya (namaḥ) । goptre (l.7) namaḥ । anidhanāya (namaḥ) । sarvayogādhikṛtāya (namaḥ) । sarvavidyādhipataye namaḥ । jyotīrūpāya (namaḥ) । parameśvarāya (namaḥ) । acetanāya (namaḥ) । cetanāya (namaḥ) । (p.31b, f.56a, l.1) vyominvyomine (namaḥ) । vyāpinvyāpine (namaḥ) । 20.

varṇam – (l.5) kṣaṃ namaḥ ।

padam – mahādevāya (namaḥ) । sadbhāveśvarāya (namaḥ) । mahādejāya namaḥ । yogādhipataye (namaḥ) । muñca-muñcāya (namaḥ) । prathama-prathamāya (namaḥ) । śarva-śarvāya (namaḥ) । (l.6) bhava-bhavāya (namaḥ) । bhavodbhavāya (namaḥ) । sarvabhūtasukhapradāya (namaḥ) । sarvasānnidhyakarāya (namaḥ) । brahmaviṣṇu-rudrarūpāya (namaḥ) । arcitārcitāya (namaḥ) । stutāstutāya (l.7) (namaḥ) । pūrvasthita-pūrvasthitāya (namaḥ) । sākṣi-sākṣiye namaḥ । turu-turuve (namaḥ) । pataṅga-pataṅgāya (namaḥ) । piṅga-piṅgāya (namaḥ) । jñāna-jñānāya (namaḥ) । śabda-śabdāya (namaḥ) । (l.8) sūkṣma-sūkṣmāya (namaḥ) । śivāya (namaḥ) । śarvāya (namaḥ) । oṃ sarvadāya (namaḥ) । śivāya (namaḥ) । oṃ namo namaḥ । oṃ namaḥ । 28.

mantram – akāralakārabījābhyāṃ namaḥ । iḻāpiṅgaḻanāḍībhyāṃ (p.33b, f.58a, l.1) namaḥ । prāṇāpānau vāyubhyāṃ namaḥ । brahmā kāraṇeśvarāya namaḥ । 

tayo(yoḥ) rudraśca viṣṇuśca aṣṭatriṃśatkalāntakam ।
brahmabhāgasamādhvānaṃ dvātriṃśaśca śatatrayam ॥ (l.2)
brahmabhāgamidaṃ mantraṃ padamekena saptatiḥ ।
dvātriṃśamidaṃ bhavet tattvatrayakalāmekatriṃśatiśca kalānāṃ ca trayaṃ bhavet ।
viṣṇubhāgadvayaṃ mantraṃ *padamekādaśastathā ॥* (l.3)
varṇatrayaṃ ca bhuvanam aṣṭādaśamidaṃ bhavet ।
tattvatrayakalāmekaṃ viṣṇubhāgena pūjayet ॥
rudrabhāgatrayaṃ mantraṃ padamekaṃ *ca varṇakam ।* (l.4)
ṣoḍaśaṃ bhuvanaṃ caiva pañcādaśaṃ ca tattvakam ।
dvayaṃ kalā ca me(e)kaṃ ca rudrabhāgaḥ samarcayet ॥
॥ ṣaḍadhvārcanavidhiḥ samāptaḥ ॥ (l.5)

śubhamastu । 
kalādhvoktāṅgasaṃpūrṇo bhuvanādhvoktaromadhṛk । 
varṇādhvai satvajopeto mantrādhvā rudhirāviti ॥ (l.6)
padādhvoktaśiromāmsaḥ śuklamajjāsthitattvakam ।
sadāśivaṣaḍadhvātmā tasya prāṇa(ṇaḥ) śiva(vaḥ) smṛtāḥ ॥
hṛdayādyaṅgasampūrṇo dhyāne sakala*rūpadhṛk ।* (l.7)
viṣṭaraṃ ca samādāya granthiṃ muktvā visarjayet ॥
paristaraṇamānīya ājyenaiva tu secayet ।
ekadarbhaṃ samādāya śeṣamagrādikaṃ dahet ॥ (l.8)
tadekamagnau nikṣipya tadbhasma tilakaṃ kuru ।
rudrādibhyo baliṃ pradāyācamanārghyaṃ dadyāt ।

tathā kāmike –
atraivāntarbaliṃ dadyāt *kṛtvā maṇḍalakaṃ bhuvi ।* (p.34a, l.1)

ghṛtaśiro'rpaṇam - 
ghṛtenaiva kṣa(kṣi)peddhīmānmūrdhni bāhvośca pādayoḥ ।
madhuna(nā) ca gavyapayasā gosiddhaye sarpiṣā ॥ (l.2)
lakṣmyai śarkarayā juhoti yaśase dadhnā tu sarvarthaye ।
annairannasamṛddhaye ca satilairadavyāptaye ॥
taṇḍulairlājaiḥ sadyaśase kusumbhakusumaiḥ *sāsvārijairvāsase ।* (l.3)
patrairbhūpatimutpalai.....vadhūstanmantriṇaḥ kairavairaśvatthādisamidbhiragrajamukhānvarṇānvadhūḥ piṣṭajaiḥ ॥ (l.4)
puttalyādibhiranvahaṃ ca vaśaye jambhaṃ tvanāvṛṣṭaye ।
loṇairviṣṭisamṛddhaye ca juhuyānmantri punarvetasaiḥ ॥
॥ iti siddhāntasārāvalyām ॥ nityāgnivṛddhā taruṇotsaveṣu dīkṣāpratiṣṭhādiṣu  bāla eva । śubhamastu । (l.5) śrī dakṣiṇāmūrtigurave namaḥ ॥ (p.34b, f.59, mṛtsaṅgrahaṇam, l.1)

(mṛtsaṅgrahaṇam -)
atha ācāryaḥ deva paricāraka-rudrakanyakālaṅkāra-pañcavādya-caṇḍeśvara-tra(tri)śūlāstrasahitaṃ praviśya । yāgamaṇṭapālayapūrve agnivāyusoma(l.2)aiśānyadigbhāge – śuddhabhūtale । astreṇa saṃprokṣya । ācāryo sukhāsīna-pavitrakaraṇa-vibhūtidhāraṇa-prāṇāyāma-recaka-kumbhaka-pūrakaṃ (l.3) karanyāsaṃ kṛtvā । oṃ mahādevāya namaḥ । iti mantroccāritaṃ saṅkalpya । puṇyāha-arghyajalam astreṇa saṃprokṣya । darbhatrayaṃ brahma-viṣṇu-(l.4)rudraṃ kuryāt । grāhayet । yo devānāṃ prathamaṃ purastāt । viśvādhiko rudro mahaṛṣiḥ(harṣiḥ) iti mantreṇa madhyarekhā । oṃ (l.5) hāṃ hiraṇyagarbhaṃ paśyati jāyamānam । sano deva......... iti mantreṇa dakṣiṇarekhā । oṃ yasmātparaṃ nāparamasti (l.6) kiñcit । yasmānnāṇiyonijyā yo'sti kaścit । iti mantreṇa uttararekhā । śanno viṣṇururukramaḥ । namo brahmaṇe naste vāyo । (l.7) iti mantreṇa uttarāgramadhyarekhā । aṃ tvameva pratyakṣaṃ brahmāsī । tvāmemeva pratyakṣaṃ brahmā vādiṣam । ṛtamavādiṣam । satyamavādiṣam (p.35a, l.1) iti mantreṇa savyarekhā । oṃ tanmāmāvīt tadvaktāramāvīt । āvīnmām । ācidvaktāram । śāntiḥ śāntiḥ śāntiḥ । iti mantreṇa dakṣiṇavāmarekhā । (l.2) catuṣpadergabhramam । brahmamadhye śaktibījaṃ likhet । kunda(ndā)liṃ[devatā] sthāpya । sumuhūrtamastu । saṅkalpya । vighneśvaramabhyarcya । puṇyāhaṃ kuryāt । puṇyāhajalena (l.3) prokṣya । pañcatrayapuruṣadarbhabandhapavitrakūrcaṃ samarpyā(rpya) । oṃ bhūrbhuvasvaḥ । paristaraṇāni । 
dhyānam –
ekavaktraṃ dvinetraṃ ca *sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam ।* (l.4)
śaṅkhacakrābhayaṃ caiva kaṭihastaṃ tathaiva ca ॥
bhūmidevī ca vāmeṣu mahālakṣmī ca savyake ।
sarvaloka*sthitaṃ caiva mahaviṣṇuśca eva ca ॥* (l.5) śrī mahāviṣṇuṃ dhyāyet ।

	oṃ keśavāya namaḥ । oṃ nārāyaṇāya namaḥ । oṃ govandāya (l.6) namaḥ । daityāripuṇḍarīkākṣāya namaḥ । oṃ puruṣottamāya namaḥ । kundalīṃ(kundālīṃ) ṣaḍaṅgamabhyarcyā(rcya) । catuṣpade – oṃ gaṃ gaṇapataye (l.7) namaḥ । iti bhūsūktamantraṃ japtvā । oṃ sakalaniṣkalaśivāya namaḥ । bhūmau abhyarcya । ācāryaḥ haste kundalīṃ gṛhītvā । catuṣpada(p.35b, f.60a, aṅkurārpaṇam, l.1)bhūmau astreṇa prokṣya । khātvā । svarṇarajatatāmrapātre gṛhītvā । mṛdamastreṇa prokṣya । mṛtpātraṃ rathaśibikāndolikaparicāraka(l.2)rudrakanyārūḍhabhūsūktamantrajaptena yāgamaṇṭapavāyukoṇe saṃsthāpya । sarveṣāṃ tāmbūlaṃ dadyāt । iti ūrdhvanāmnā va siddhāgame (l.3) mṛtsaṃgrahaṇapaṭalaḥ । śubhamastu ।

(aṅkurārpaṇam -)
	atha aṅkurārpaṇavidhirucyate । atha aṅkurārpaṇavidhiṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ । liṅga(l.4)sthāpana-saṃprokṣaṇa-sakalasthāpana-garbhanyāsa-maṇṭapasthāpana-maṇṭapapraveśana-prāsādasthāpana-gṛhapraveśana-(l.5)pavitrārohaṇa-śūlasthāpana-dārusaṃgrahaṇa-nṛttaraṅga-sthāpana-dīkṣābhiṣeka-utsavāṣṭabandhana ityādikasya vāstupūjā (l.6) mṛtsaṃgrahaṇa-aṅkurārpaṇaṃ kariṣye । āha aṣṭādaśa-kriyai । nava-sapta-pañca-eka-taddinaṃ kuryāt । vāyukuberamadhye । (l.7) ṣaḍaṅgulocchrāyapañcaviṃśatyaṅgulapramāṇasamacaturarvedīṃ kṛtvā । śālitaṇḍulaṃ nikṣipya । oṃ śivaśaktiṃ darbheṣu dvayaṃ grāhya । oṃ (p.36a, l.1) hrīṃ nama uccaritaṣoḍaśakoṣṭhaṃ vidhāya । madhyakoṣṭhacatuṣṭaye । yaṃ raṃ vaṃ laṃ likhet । dvādaśakoṣṭheṣu । svarabījadvādaśam । īśānādipradakṣiṇaṃ (l.2) likhet । pālikā- ghaṭikā-śarāvāṇi kumbham astreṇa saṃprokṣya । ntuvastrakūrcahemapratimāratnacūtapatranālikerasahitam । madhye – candrakumbha-(l.3) vardhanīkumbhadvayaṃ saṃsthāpya ।

atha patradarbheṣu brahmaviṣṇuṃ tathaiva ca । 
baddhatantucaturvedaṃ mṛtpāśā śaktireva ca ॥
pālikā *ghaṭikāścaiva śarāvāṇi trayā bhavet ।* (l.4)
mahālakṣmī sarasvatyāṃ gauryāyāṃ ca prakīrtitam ॥
ādhāradvayaṅgulaṃ caiva kaṇṭhamekāṅgulaṃ *bhavet ।* (l.5)
agraṃ tu caturaṅgulyaṃ pālikāditralakṣaṇam ॥

	dvādaśakoṇeṣu । pālikāghaṭikāśarāvāṇi saṃsthāpya (l.6) guruḥ pavitrakaraṇa-prāṇāyāma-karanyāsam aṅganyāsa-bhūtaśuddhiṃ kṛtvā । śuddhapātrairastreṇa prokṣya । parāśaktiṃ dhyātvā (l.7) tilasarṣapamudgamāṣakulasthahāḍhakaśālisaptabījāni nikṣipyā(pya) । 

agastyaṃ ca tilaṃ kuryāt sarṣapaṃ kāśyapaṃ tathā ।
bhāradvājaṃ ca mudgaṃ *syāt(n) māṣaṃ gautamameva ca ॥* (p.36b, f.61a, l.1)
hāḍhakaṃ ca vasiṣṭhaṃ syāt śālikauśikameva ca ।
kulasthaṃ ca pulastyeṣu saptabījādhidevatā ॥
pātreṣu indradevatyaṃ kāmadhenuṃ *ca kṣīrayet ।* (l.2)
śivabījamiti dhyātvā kṣīrapātrasya pūjayet ॥

	saṅkalpamiti puṇyāhaṃ vācayitvā puṇyāhajalena prokṣya । oṃ bhūrbhuvasva svāhā । paristaraṇāni (l.3) kṛtvā । candrakumbhe oṃ sāṃ somāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ somamūrtaye namaḥ । 
dhyānam –
ekavaktraṃ dvinetraṃ ca śvetavastraṃ tathaiva ca ।
dvihastaṃ *kumudopetaprabhāmaṇḍalamutthitam ॥* (l.4)
kṛttikā dakṣiṇe pārśve rohiṇī vāmapārśvake ।
svaraḥ kalādharopetaṃ sarvālaṅkārasaṃyutam ॥
śvetapadma*sthitaṃ śāntaṃ candraṃ dhyāyet vicakṣaṇam ।* (l.5) iti dhyātvā । 

	oṃ sāṃ somamūrtaye namaḥ । iti saṃpūjyā(jya) । oṃ sāṃ hṛdayāya namaḥ । oṃ sīṃ śirase (l.6) namaḥ । oṃ sūṃ śikhāyai namaḥ । oṃ saiṃ kavacāya namaḥ । oṃ soṃ netrāya namaḥ । oṃ saḥ astrāya namaḥ । iti oṃkārādinamontaṃ ṣaḍaṅga(l.7)mabhyarcyā(rcya) । oṃ aiṃ klīṃ sauṃ śrīṃ hrīṃ kṛttikārohiṇyai namaḥ । somakumbhadvayamabhyarcayet । pālikāghaṭikāśarāvāṇi darbhatrayapavitra(l.8)grandhidviśirasā kaniṣṭikāgradīrghakūrcapūrvādipālikeṣu । oṃ .... vaikartanāsanāya namaḥ । vaikartanamūrtaye namaḥ ।

vaikartanasvarūpaṃ tu *ekavaktraṃ dvihastakam ।* (p.37a, l.1)
āyudhaṃ svarṇapuṣpaṃ ca dvihastena tu dhārakam ॥
suvarṇo bhūṣaṇopetaṃ rajanībhasmadhāriṇam ।

	iti dhyātvā । oṃ sāṃ vaikartanāya namaḥ । (l.2) iti saṃpūjyā(jya) । oṃ sāṃ vivasvatāsanāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ vivasvatamūrtaye namaḥ ।

vivasvatasvarūpaṃ tu dvivaktraṃ ca caturbhujam ।
āyudhābjakaraṃ deva abhayaṃ varadā*nvitam ॥* (l.3)
piṅgalaṃ caiva vastraṃ syāt bhūṣaṇaṃ ca pravālakam ।

	oṃ sāṃ vivasvatāya namaḥ । iti saṃpūjya । oṃ sāṃ mārtāṇḍāsanāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ (l.4) mārtāṇḍamūrtaye namaḥ ।

mārtāṇḍasya svarūpaṃ tu trivaktraṃ ṣaḍbhujānvitam ।
abhayaṃ varadaṃ caiva hāramābharaṇaṃ tathā ॥

	oṃ sāṃ mārtāṇḍāya namaḥ । (l.5) iti saṃpūjya । oṃ sāṃ bhāskarāsanāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ bhāskaramūrtaye namaḥ ।

bhāskarasya svarūpaṃ tu dvivaktraṃ ca caturbhujam । (l.6)
namaskāradvihastena parahastāmbujadvayam ॥
śoṇābhaṃ śoṇavastraṃ ca bhūṣaṇaṃ nīlaratnakam ।

	oṃ sāṃ bhāskarāya namaḥ । iti saṃpūjyā(jya) । (l.7) oṃ sāṃ ravyāsanāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ ravimūrtaye namaḥ ।

raveścaiva svarūpaṃ tu caturbhujasamanvitam ।
viśeṣā daṇḍahastaṃ tu akṣamālā*kamaṇḍalum ॥* (p.37b, f.62a, l.1) 
śūlaṃ pāśāṅkuśābjaṃ ca abhayaṃ varadaṃ tathā ।
āyudhasyamitiproktaṃ raktavastraṃ ca dhāriṇam ॥
vibhūṣya maratakaṃ caiva evaṃ dhyātvā *prapūjayet ।* (l.2)

	iti saṃpūjya । oṃ sāṃ lokaprakāśāsanāya namaḥ । lokaprakāśamūrtaye namaḥ ।

lokaprakāśakaṃ varṇaṃ rājāvartasya sannibham । (l.3)
daśahastaṃ pañcamudrākṣamālābharaṇabhūṣitam ॥
pañcavaktraṃ dvihastena abjameva ca hastakam ।
śūlamaṅkuśadaṇḍau ca japamālā *kamaṇḍalum ॥* (l.4)
śaraṃ ca nīlaraktaṃ ca nīlavastraṃ ca dhāriṇam ।

	oṃ sāṃ lokaprakāśāya namaḥ । iti saṃpūjya । oṃ sāṃ lokasākṣiṇānāya (l.5) namaḥ । lokasākṣiṇi mūrtaye namaḥ । 

lokasākṣiṃ pravakṣyāmi ekavaktraṃ dvihastakam ।
kalitāñjalihastaṃ ca śvetavarṇaṃ ca *bhāvayet ॥* (l.6)
kāṣāvastraṃ ca mevaṃ tu bhasmoddhūlitavigraham ।

	oṃ sāṃ lokasākṣiṇe namaḥ । iti saṃpūjyā(jya) । oṃ trivikramāya namaḥ । (l.7) trivikramāsanāya namaḥ । trivikramamūrtaye namaḥ । 

trivikramasvarūpaṃ tu dvibhujaṃ ca catuḥ karau ।
ūrdhvahastadvaye'bjasya namaskāramadhaḥkarau ॥
śyāmaraktaṃ ca *vibhūṣaṃ ca vastraṃ śyāmaṃ ca varṇa vā ।* (p.38a, l.1)

oṃ sāṃ trivikramāya namaḥ । iti saṃpūjyā(jya) । oṃ ādityāsanāya namaḥ । ādityamūrtaye namaḥ ।

ādityaṃ ca svarūpaṃ tu ekavaktradvihastakam । (l.2)
ḍāḍimīkusumaprakhyā nālābjaṃ yugmadhāriṇam ॥
ratnābharaṇamevaṃ tu sañcityādityarūpakam ।

	oṃ sām ādityāya namaḥ । iti saṃpūjyā(jya) । oṃ sāṃ (l.3) sūryāsanāya namaḥ । sūryamūrtaye namaḥ ।

sūryasyāpi svarūpaṃ tu ekavaktraṃ bhujāṣṭakam ।
abjadvayakaropetaṃ ghaṇṭadhūpakamaṇḍalum ॥ (l.4)
abhayaṃ varadopetam akṣamālāsamanvitam ।
hemavastraṃ ca hemābhaṃ bhūṣaṇaṃ svarṇameva ca ॥
evaṃ dhyātvā viśeṣeṇa sūryamūrtiṃ *prapūjayet ।* (l.5)

	oṃ sāṃ sūryāya namaḥ । iti saṃpūjyā(jya) । oṃ sāṃ aṃśumālyāsanāya namaḥ । aṃśumālīmūrtaye namaḥ । 

aṃśumālī*svarūpaṃ tu ṣaḍvaktraṃ raktavarṇakam ।* (l.6)
dvādaśaṃ hastamevaṃ tu parahastāmbujadvayam ॥
tomarāsijakhaḍgaṃ ca kheṭakaṃ pāśamaṅkuśam । (l.7)
śaktibhetālamabhayaṃ varadaṃ dakṣavāmataḥ ॥

	oṃ sāṃ aṃśumāline namaḥ । iti saṃpūjyā(jya) । oṃ sāṃ divākarāsanāya namaḥ । divākaramūrtaye namaḥ । (p.38b, f.63a, l.1)

divākarasya rūpaṃ tu dvimukhaṃ ca catuḥkaraiḥ ।
bilvapatradvayakaraṃ padmapuṣpakaradvayam ॥
rajanīvastrasaṃyuktaṃ hemābharaṇabhūṣitam ।

	oṃ sāṃ divākarāya (l.2) namaḥ । evaṃ dhyātvārcayet । yadvā । 

vaikartane vivasvāṃśca mārtāṇḍo bhāskaro raviḥ ।
lokaprakāśakaścaiva lokasākṣi trivikramaḥ ॥ (l.3)
ādityaśca tathā sūryo aṃśumālī divākaraḥ ।

	iti caturthyāntena pūrvādīśāntadvādaśādityān pūjan pūjayet । pakṣāntaramahāṣṭapakṣe । (l.4) indrādīn pūjayet । saptapakṣe saptamātṛkān pūjayet । pañcapakṣe nivṛtyādīn pūjayet । gandhādīnāmabhyarcya । dhūpadīpau (l.5) datvā । bīja-kṣīrapātre candrakumbhajalenāplāvayet । pañcabrahmaṣaḍaṅgamabhyarcya । oṃ sāṃ sauṃ śrīṃ hrīṃ klīṃ sakalaguhāya (l.6) namaḥ । iti mantram ekādaśavāramabhimantrya āvāhanādyaṣṭamudrāṃ darśai(yi)tvā । dhūpadīpau datvā । śaṅkhamaṅgaladhvanitaṃ oṣadhisūktaṃ japet । (l.7) oṃ sāṃ brahmaṇe namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ sṛṣṭaye namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ pitāmahāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ vidhiye namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ hiraṇyagarbhāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ (p.39a, l.1) sāvitrī gāyatrīpuruṣāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ pītavarṇāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ kamala-saṃbhavāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ satyalokādhipāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ vedāya surajyeṣṭhāya (l.2) namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ prajāpataye namaḥ । iti pālikā-ghaṭikāśarāvāṇi kṣīrasaṃyutabījaṃ nikṣipya । dhūpadīpau datvā । homakarmaṇi candrakumbhasannidhiṃ (l.3) kṛtvā । pañcadarbhakūrcaṃ samarpyā(rpya) । suṣumnāgniṃ dhyātvā । ṣaḍaṅgamabhyarcyā(rcya) । pañcamudrayā tālatrayadigbandhanadhenumudrāṃ darśai(yi)tvā । (l.4) indhanamastreṇa saṃprokṣyā(kṣya) prajvālyā(lya) । sṛksṛvapātrapalāśasamidhājyasahitam । homadravyāṇi sarvamastreṇa prokṣyā(kṣya) । (l.5) kuṇḍāgnisaṃskārājyasaṃskāraṃ kuryāt । ājyapātre dvā tiraḥ pavitrakūrcam । oṃ brahmaṇe namaḥ । iti mantreṇa (l.6) samabhyarcyā(rcya) । oṃ hāṃ īśānatatpuruṣāghoravāmadevasadyojātābhyāṃ saṃ śrīṃ hrīm amṛteśvarāya svāhā । iti mantreṇā(l.7)ṣṭottaraśatāhutiṃ hutvā । oṃ sāṃ candrāya svāhā । iti mantreṇa homadravyāṇi sarvam akṣaśuktamātraṃ hunet । oṃ sāṃ śrīṃ hrīṃ klīṃ sāṃ sīṃ (p.39b, f.64a, vāstupūjai, l.1) sūṃ sauṃ siḥ candrāyāmṛteśvarāya svāhā । iti pūrṇāhutiṃ hunet । agne aṣṭapuṣpaṃ samarpyā(rpya) । kumbhe saṃyojyā(jya) । ghṛtagulapāyasānnaṃ nivedyā(dya) । (l.2) dhūpadīpatāmbūlaṃ datvā । yajamānasya darśanaṃ kuryāt । iti ūrdhvānmāya siddhāgame aṅkurārpaṇapaṭalaḥ । śrī subrahmaṇyāya namaḥ । 

(vāstupūjai(jā) -) (l.3)
	atha vāstupūjāvidhirucyate । atha vāstupūjāvidhiṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ । svagṛhamadhye । āsthānamaṇṭape yāgaśālāṃ bālasthāpane (l.4) nidhāya । hastamātrasamacaturaśradya(dvya)ṅgulocchrāyavedīṃ kuryāt । tatpūrve hastamātravistāra-dvādaśāṅgulocchrāya sama(l.5)caturaśrahomakuṇḍaṃ saṃkalpya paścimavedikāyām । vrīhitaṇḍulatilaṃ saṅkalpyā(lpya) । padmamullikhyā(khya) । madhye śaktibījaṃ likhyā(khya) । (l.6) madhye kumbhaṃ sthāpayitvā । caturdikṣu kalaśaṃ sthāpayitvā । kumbhe vāstvadhipataye brahmaṇe namaḥ । iti brahmāṇamāvāhya (l.7) caturdikṣu kalaśeṣu । oṃ hāṃ sṛṣṭaye namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ prajāpataye namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ vedhase namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ vidhaye namaḥ । iti (p.40a, l.1) pūrvādimantramāvāhyārcayet । lokapālāna-rcayet । āvāhanāditālatrayadigbandhanadhenumudrāṃ darśayet । dhūpadīpaṃ kuryāt । tatpūrve vedikāyāṃ (l.2) astreṇa saṃprokṣyā(kṣya) । oṃ hāṃ śrīṃ hrīṃ klīṃ vāstupadebhyo namaḥ । ekāśītipadaṃ likhyā(khya) । śrī vāstupadayantraṃ namaḥ । ityāvāhyā(hya) । gandhapuṣpadhūpadīpau (l.3) kuryāt । kumbhaṃ dakṣiṇapārśve strīdarbhāyāṃ tattvadarbheṣu kūrcarūpaṃ kuryāt ।
dhyānam –
ekavaktraṃ dvinetraṃ ca śikhā yajñopavītī tam । (l.4)
kamaṇḍallakṣasūtraṃ ca abhayaṃ varadaṃ tathā ॥
brahmayajñaṃ śuddhācāraṃ pītavarṇaṃ tathaiva ca ।
śvetavastradharaṃ bhīmaṃ daityabrahmāṇavāstunam ॥ (l.5)

	iti dhyātvā । astreṇa snānārthaṃ prokṣyā(kṣya) । oṃ hāṃ vāstupuruṣāya namaḥ । ityāvāhyārcyā(rcya) । oṃ hāṃ śrīṃ hrīṃ iti mūrdhni(l.6)mukhaguhyaṃ nyaset । digbandhanadhenumudraṃ darśayitvā । dhūpadīpau datvā । ekāśītipadeṣu oṃ śrīṃ hrīṃ iti puṣpāñjali । kṣīraghṛta(l.7)gulasūpasaṃyutānnam astreṇa saṃprokṣyā(kṣya) । dṛ[mu]ṣṭimātrānna kuryāt । madhye navapade oṃ hāṃ brahmaṇe namaḥ । caturmukhāya namaḥ । (p.40b, f.65a, l.1) ānandavedāya namaḥ । kamaṇḍallakṣamālyadharāya namaḥ । viriñcāya namaḥ । pitāmahāya namaḥ । kamalasambhavāya namaḥ । prathamāya namaḥ । vidhiye namaḥ । iti pūrvādisaṃpūjya । (l.2) pītavarṇaṃ kṛtvā । pūrve ṣaṭpade oṃ hāṃ mārīcyai namaḥ । kāruṇyāya namaḥ । sasyāya namaḥ । mukhyāya namaḥ । sakalāya namaḥ । ṛṣibhyo namaḥ । iti śukla(l.3)varṇaṃ kṛtvā । dakṣiṇe ṣaṭpade oṃ hāṃ vivasvadhā(tā)ya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ bījāya namaḥ । aṅkurāya namaḥ । gandharvāya namaḥ । devāya namaḥ । rākṣasāya (l.4) namaḥ । iti kālavarṇaṃ kṛtvā । paścimaṣaṭpade । oṃ hāṃ mitrāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ yogāya namaḥ । suvratāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ kālāya namaḥ । (l.5) oṃ hāṃ manuṣyāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ sthūlāya namaḥ । iti dhūmravarṇaṃ kṛtvā । uttare ṣaṭpade oṃ hāṃ budhāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ bhogāya (l.6) namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ kalyāṇāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ sāhasāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ bhīmāya namaḥ । oṃ hām unmattāya namaḥ । iti raktavarṇaṃ kṛtvā । īśānadvidvipadeṣu (l.7) oṃ hāṃ āpāya namaḥ । oṃ hām āpavatsāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ ...dhyāya namaḥ । oṃ hām anantāya namaḥ । śvetavarṇaṃ kṛtvā । āgneyādi(p.41a, l.1)dvidvipadeṣu । oṃ hāṃ savitre namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ sāvitre namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ sarvāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ sarvatroya namaḥ । iti āsumbhavarṇaṃ kṛtvā । naiṛtyādidvidvipadeṣu oṃ hām (l.2) indrāya namaḥ । oṃ hām indrajāya namaḥ । oṃ hām indrarājāya namaḥ । oṃ hām indrapataye namaḥ । iti śyāmavarṇaṃ kṛtvā । vāyavyāṃ dvidvipadeṣu oṃ hāṃ rudrāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ rudrajāya (l.3) namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ rudrapataye namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ rudrasvāmine namaḥ । iti dhūmravarṇaṃ kṛtvā । īśānāddvātriṃśatpadeṣu । oṃ hām īśānāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ (l.4) parjanyāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ jayantāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ satyakāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ mahendrāya namaḥ । oṃ hām ādityāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ bhraṃśakāya namaḥ । oṃ hām (l.5) antarikṣāya namaḥ । oṃ hām agnaye namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ pūṣṇave namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ vidhātre namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ grahakṣatāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ yamāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ (l.6) vidyādharāya namaḥ । mṛgarājāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ mṛgāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ niṛṛtaye namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ dauvārikāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ sugrīvāya namaḥ । (l.7) oṃ hāṃ puṣpadantāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ varuṇāya namaḥ । oṃ hām asurāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ śoṣāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ rogāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ (p.41b, f.66a, l.1) vāyave namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ rāgāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ mukhyāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ bhallāṭakāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ somāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ ṛbhave namaḥ । oṃ hām aditaye namaḥ । oṃ (l.2) hāṃ ditaye namaḥ । śyāmavarṇaṃ kṛtvā । pūrve śūleṣu । oṃ hāṃ vidāriṇe namaḥ । dakṣiṇaśūle । oṃ hāṃ pāparākṣasyai namaḥ । paścimaśūleṣu । oṃ (l.3) hāṃ skandāya namaḥ । uttaraśūleṣu । oṃ hāṃ balipiñchāya namaḥ । iti raktavarṇaṃ kṛtvā । īśānakoṇacatuṣkoṇeṣu । oṃ hāṃ (l.4) cari(ra)kine namaḥ । agnikoṇacakreṣu । oṃ hāṃ pūtanāya namaḥ । naiṛtakoṇacakreṣu । oṃ hāṃ aryamṇe namaḥ । (l.5) vāyukoṇacakreṣu । oṃ hāṃ jambhāya namaḥ । iti pītavarṇaṃ kṛtvā । evaṃ sva sva nāmādipraṇavādinamontamarcayet । āvāhanādi(l.6)aṣṭamudrāṃ darśai(yi)tvā । dhūpadīpaṃ datvā pūjayet । pūrvoktakuṇḍa-saṃskāraṃ kuryāt । oṃ bhūrbhuvasvasvamantreṇa darbhaviṣṭaraṃ kuṇḍamadhye (l.7) oṃ hāṃ hrīṃkāraṃ śivamabhyarcyā(rcya) । oṅkārādi-namo'ntaṃ lokapālānabhyarcya । śuddhapātre agnibījaṃ gṛhītvā । agnisaṃskāraṃ kuryāt । (p.42a, l.1) kuṇḍodare bījaṃ nikṣipya । pālāśasamidhena prajvālyā(lya) । trikuśaiḥ kūrcaiḥ samarpyā(rpya) । 
dhyānam –
āsyadvayaṃ dviṣaṇṇetraṃ jaṭāmakuṭamaṇḍitam ।
śveta*yajñopavītaṃ ca abhayaṃ varadaṃ tathā ॥* (l.2)
sṛksṛksṛvaṃ ghṛtapātraṃ ca dadhānaṃ raktavarṇakam ।
svāhā svadhābhyāṃ saṃyuktam ajavāhanasaṃsthitam ॥
saptajihvā*samāyuktaṃ prasannavadanāmbujam ।* (l.3)
evaṃ dhyāyet svarūpaṃ ca śivāgneḥ paramādbhutam ॥

	iti dhyātvā । oṃ hāṃ śaviṃ yaṃ iti mūrdhni(l.4)mukhahṛdayādi nyaset । ṣaḍaṅgairabhyarcyā(rcya) । lokapālānabhyarcyā(rcya) । īśānādīśānamabhyarcyā(rcya) āvāhanādyaṣṭasaṃskāraiḥ (l.5) saṃskṛtyā(tya) । dhūpadīpau datvā । praṇītā prokṣaṇaṃ ca kṛtvā । sthālī sṛksṛvasamiddhomadravyāṇi sarvāṇi saṃprokṣyā(kṣya) । ājyapātre – ājyapātraṃ oṃ hāṃ hrīṃ iti mantreṇa saṃpūryā(rya) । madhye tridarbhaiḥ kṛtadvitiraḥ pavitraṃ nikṣipyā(pya) । (p.42b, f.67a, l.1) śuklapakṣe sade raṃ rave namaḥ । kṛṣṇapakṣe vāme । candrāya namaḥ । ityabhyarcyā(rcya) । prokṣaṇīpātre jalaṃ pūryā(rya) । dvitiraḥ pavitraṃ oṃ lamiti jalamadhye nikṣipyā(pya) । (l.2) praṇītāpātre jalaṃ pūryā(rya) । hastaśuddhiṃ kṛtvā । sṛksṛvau prokṣayet । śuklapakṣe । ājyaṃ niṣkamātraṃ oṃ hrīṃ vāstupuruṣāya svāhā (l.3) iti mantreṇa pañcaviṃśatyā hunet । agnikuṇḍamaṇḍalalokapālaśeṣabījaṃ madhye gaja(jaḥ?) oṃ hrīṃ vāstupuruṣāya (l.4) heti pūrṇāṃ datvā । homapuṣpaṃ kumbhe saṃyojyā(jya) । vāstupradakṣiṇakrameṇa yantrasavyabhāge saṃsthāpya kumbhajalena (l.5) prokṣyā(kṣya) । kūrcamabhyarcyā(rcya) । oṃ hrāṃ hṛdayāya namaḥ । oṃ brīṃ(hrīṃ) śirase namaḥ । oṃ brūṃ(hrūṃ) śā(śi)khāya namaḥ । oṃ braiṃ(hraiṃ) kavacāya namaḥ । (l.6) oṃ । hrauṃ netratrayāya vauṣaṭ । oṃ braḥ(hraḥ) astrāya phaṭ । ityaṅgaṃ vinyasya । oṃ hām icchāśaktyādhipataye brahmaṇe namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ vāstu(l.7)pu?

ekavaktraṃ dvinetraṃ ca pralambitajaṭānvitam ।
dvihastaṃ ca dvipādaṃ ca dīrghagātraṃ tathaiva ca ॥
khaḍgaṃ dakṣiṇahaste tu kapālaṃ vāmahastake ।
evameva krameṇaiva vāstunāthaṃ prapūjayet ॥ (p.43a, l.1)

	puruṣāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ kriyāśaktyādhipataye brahmaṇe vāstupuruṣāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ jñānaśaktyādhipataye brahmaṇe vāstupuruṣāya namaḥ । ityabhyarcya । (l.2) prāyaścittārthaṃ oṃ hāṃ hauṃ śivāya namaḥ । iti svadheti mantreṇa śatāhutiṃ hunet । ghṛtagulasūpavyañjanakṣīrānnanaivedyatāmbūlaṃ (l.3) dhūpadīpau datvā । oṃ hrāṃ hrīṃ icchājñānakriyāśaktyādhipataye śrīṃ klīṃ vāstupuruṣāya svāhā । iti mantreṇa (l.4) pūrṇāhutiṃ hunet । agnipuṣpaṃ vāsau samarpyā(rpya) । tālatrayadigbandhanadhenumudrāṃ darśayitvā । vāstupradakṣiṇahomakuṇḍa(l.5)savya-bhāge nidhāya । kṣīradadhyājyagulasammiśrānnaṃ baliṃ kṛtvā । kūśmāṇḍakadalīphalabaliṃ kṛtvāstreṇa sthūlarūpaṃ (l.6) kṛtvā । tanmadhye vāstuśayanaṃ kuryāt ।

pūrve kanyā tulāmāse dhanurmakarayāmyake । 
vāruṇaṃ mīnameṣaṃ syāt saumye mithunakarkaṭe ॥ (p.43b, f.68a, l.1)
anale kṛttikāmāse naiṛtyāṃ kumbhameva ca ।
vṛṣabhaṃ vāyudigbhāge siṃhamīnakameva ca ॥
śayanaṃ vāstupuruṣo kṛtavāniti *paṭhyate ।* (l.2)

	astreṇa prokṣyā(kṣya) । ekapuṣpamabhyarcya । darbheṇa prajvālya । tasya śikhāyām ājyamiśrāgnimaghoramantreṇā-gnisandhānaṃ (l.3) kuryāt । vāstupuruṣapādadvayaṃ paricārake astreṇa prokṣya । vibhūtiratnamālādarbhamālālaṅkṛtya kiñcidbhūta-(l.4)māvāhya । tasmin samaye tāmbūlaṃ dadyāt । vāstupuruṣapādadvayaṃ brāhmaṇaparicāri(ra)kaḥ dakṣiṇahastena gṛhītvā (l.5) vāmahaste balipātramādāya grāmālaya(yayā)gaśālādisarvapradakṣiṇaṃ kuryāt । pūrvottarapaścime dakṣiṇe kuryāt । (l.6) sapta-pañcadhanurdūre vāstuṃ visṛjya । nadītaṭākādiṣu snāyāt । devarākṣasamānuṣyairvitaṃ? yatra vighnakam । vāstunāthasya pūjena (p.44a, ratnanyāsavidhi(dhiḥ), l.1) dagdhavighnaṃ na saṃśayaḥ । svātmāyā siddhāgame vāstupūjāvidhipaṭalaḥ ॥ śrī subrahmaṇyāya namaḥ ।

(ratnanyāsavidhiḥ -)

maṇṭapaṃ tatra saṅkalpya *vitānadhvajasaṃyutam ।* (l.2)
devaṃ bhadrāsanaṃ sthāpya prāṅmukhasya kramādguruḥ ॥
tadagre sthaṇḍilaṃ kṛtvā kalpya hastamātrapravistaram ।
likhennavapadaṃ kṛtvā *tatpade pātrasaṃsthitam ॥* (l.3)
tanmadhye padmarāgaṃ syāt aindre vaiḍūryasaṃsthitam ।
agne vajraṃ tu vinyasyāt puṣparāgaṃ tu dakṣiṇe ॥ (l.4)
naiṛte sphaṭikaṃ caiva vāruṇe mauktikaṃ tathā ।
indranīlaṃ tu vāyavyāṃ māṇikkamuttare tathā ॥
aiśānyāṃ ca pravālaṃ ca sthāpaye*nnavaratnakam ।* (l.5)
puṣpaṃ pavitraṃ gandhaṃ ca toyaratnena saṃyutam ॥
nirīkṣaṇādiśuddhiṃ syāt ṣaḍudhvā(tthā)sanamarcayet ।
indrādi*lokapālānāṃ madhye brahmāṇamarcayet ॥* (l.6)
dhyānapūrvaṃ ca saṃpūjya dhūpadīpaṃ nivedayet ।
bimbāṅgaṃ vinyase*dratnaṃ sugandhena tu saṃyutam ॥* (l.7)
mūrdhnilalāṭakaṇṭhe ca hṛdaye nābhipādayoḥ ।
bāhudvayaṃ ca saṃsthāpya saṃhitāmantramarcayet ॥ (p.44b, f.69, nayanonmīlanam, l.1)
naivedyaṃ pāyasānnaṃ caiva dhūpadīpaṃ tathaiva ca ।
aṣṭapuṣpeṇa saṃpūjyābhiṣekamathācaret ॥
ratnanyāsaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā nayanonmīlanaṃ tathā । (l.2)

(nayanonmīlanam -)
śāntaṃ sadguṇamācāraṃ śilpinā śāstramuttamam ।
rudrākṣamupavītaṃ ca uṣṇīṣaṃ cottarīyakam ॥
uttarābhimukho bhūtvā rucirāsanamācaret । (l.3)
yathāsaṅkalpapūrvaṃ tu vighneśaṃ pūjayettathā ॥
aṅganyāsakaranyāsam antaryāgaṃ tu pūjayet ।
ātmasthānaṃ ca śuddhiṃ syāt *arghyaṃ sāmānyakalpitam ॥* (l.4)
devāgre sthaṇḍilaṃ kalpya aṣṭadroṇaiśca śālibhiḥ ।
tanmadhye padmamālikhya madhye kumbhaṃ pratiṣṭhitam ॥ (l.5)
sasūtraṃ nālikeraṃ ca kūrcapallavasaṃyutam ।
pratimābimbasaṃyuktaṃ dhyānamāvāhayet kramāt ॥
ādhārādīn tu pūjāṃ ca *kuṭilāntasya ma(a)rcayet ।* (l.6)
āvāhanādipūjāṃ ca pūrvoktavadhivat kramāt ॥
tatpūrve sthaṇḍilaṃ kṛtvā agnikāryoktamārgataḥ ।
pañcasaṃskāra*mevaṃ tu homayettu vicakṣaṇaḥ ॥* (l.7)
samidājyacaruṃ hutvā netramantraṃ hunetkramāt ।
homānte arcayeddevaṃ pracchi(ccha)nnapaṭaveṣṭitam ॥ (p.45a, l.1)
śilpamāyudhasaṃgrāhya netramantramanusmaran ।
padmarekhā purā kṛtvā tato vai kṛṣṇamaṇḍalam ॥
jyotirmaṇḍalamālikhya samadṛṣṭiṃ ca kārayet ।
śrotradvayaṃ ca nāsā *dvau vaktraṃ gudaṃ ca guhyayoḥ ॥* (l.2)
śaktibimbastanāgre vā sāmānyarandhramācaret ।
vāhanāyudhasamaṃ dri(dṛ)ṣṭi hemasūciṃ tu rokhayoḥ ॥
tat randhramadhu*saṃspṛśya abhiṣekamathācaret ।* (l.3)
kumbhābhiṣekavidhinā arcanāvāhanaṃ kuru ॥
homasya phaladattena devahaste pradāpayet । (l.4)
dhūpadīpena saṃyojya praśchi(ccha)nnapaṭavarjitam ॥
prathamaṃ dhenuvatsaṃ ca dvitīyaṃ kannidarśanam ।
tṛtīyaṃ kṣīragavyādi caturthaṃ navadhānyakam ॥ (l.5)
pañcamaṃ sarvadhānyaṃ ca ṣaṣṭhamaṃ darpaṇaṃ tathā ।
sanyāsaṃ saptamaṃ caiva aṣṭamaṃ vedaghoṣaṇam ॥
navamaṃ śivabhaktānāṃ *yajamānaṃ tadantaram ।* (l.6)
sarve bhaktajanānāṃ tu sevitaṃ bhaktimānataḥ ॥
śilpikṛtyaṃ purā kṛtvā aparaṃ gurukṛtyakam ।
atha gavyābhiṣekena *puṇyāhaprokṣaṇaṃ tathā ॥* (l.7)
nayanonmīlanaṃ kṛtvā pūrvoktavidhinā saha ।
paścāt berasya śuddhīnāṃ śāstradṛṣṭena karmaṇā ॥ (p.45b, f.70a, pratimāśuddhi, grāmapradakṣiṇam, l.1)

(pratimāśuddhi -)
kulīrāvṛṣagośṛṅgaṃ gajaśṛṅgamṛtaṃ tathā ।
parvataṃ ca samudraṃ ca valmīkaṃ kuśamūlakam ॥
aṣṭamṛtyuṃ ca saṃgrāhya bhāṇḍamekaṃ pratiṣṭhitam । (l.2)
jambū āmraṃ tu aśvatthaṃ vaṭodumbarameva ca ॥
pañca tvakkāṣāyodaṃ bhāṇḍamekaṃ nidhāpayet ।
kāṣāyapatra(traṃ) saṃsthāpya sthāpitaṃ *pañcagavyakam ॥* (l.3)
pratyekaṃ sthaṇḍilaṃ kalpya pātrasaṃsthāpyameva ca ।
pālāśodumbaramaśvatthe jambuvaṭasya pallavam ॥
sasūtraṃ sāpidhānaṃ ca kūrcapallavasaṃyutam ।
ṣaḍu(ḍa)dhvā(tthā)*sanamabhyarcya saṃhitāmantramarcayet ॥* (l.4)
nirīkṣaṇādiśuddhiṃ syāddhenumudrāṃ pradarśayet ।
naivedyadhūpadīpaṃ ca *vyomavyāpijapaṃ tathā ॥* (l.5)
gośṛṅgāgrapramāṇena abhiṣekavidhikramāt ।
beraśuddhiṃ tataḥ kṛtvā paścāt grāmapradakṣiṇam ॥ (l.6)
abhiṣekaṃ tu kartavyam alaṅkṛtya vidhīyate ।
citravastrairalaṅkṛtya sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam ॥ 
sarvagandhasaṃyuktaṃ puṣpamālyāmalaṅkṛtam । (l.7)
citraprabhāsamāyuktaṃ puṣpaprabhasamanvitam ॥
śabikā bhadrapīṭhaṃ vā paścādgrāmapradakṣiṇam ।
caralagnaṃ tu kartavyaṃ tat lagnaṃ *śuddhipañcakam ॥* (p.46a, jalādhivāsanam, l.1)
vīthyāgataṃ tu devānāṃ vīthīsammārjanaṃ kuru ।
kadalīstambhasaṃsthitvā toraṇadhvajasaṃyutam ॥
tṛṇavarjitavīthīnāṃ jalaṃ sampūrṇapūritam । (l.2)
jhallarī bhramariṃ caiva paṭahaṃ maddalaṃ tathā ॥
pañcavādyamahāghoṣaṃ piñchacāmarasaṃyutam ।
chatradhvajasamāyuktaṃ dhūpaṃ vai bhaktimārgataḥ ॥
bahudīpena saṃyuktaṃ *nānāvādyasamanvitam ।* (l.3)
nānāgītasamāyuktaṃ sarvālaṅkāraśobhitam ॥
brāhmaṇān ghoṣayettatra caturvedasamanvitam । (l.4)
ācāryadakṣiṇe pārśve nṛpādivāmapārśvake ॥
rudrakanyasamāyuktaṃ gāndharvaṃ tu śivāgrake ।
grāmapradakṣiṇaṃ kṛtvā jalādhivāsanaṃ *tathā ॥* (l.5)

(jalādhivāsanam -)
samudrakāyānnadyāṃ vā taṭākaṃ vā viśeṣataḥ ।
jalatīre tu sa(saṃ)sthāpya devaṃ vai pūrvatomukhāḥ ॥ (l.6)
puṇyāhaprokṣaṇaṃ caiva saṅkalpavidhipūrvakam ।
kumbhasthāpana(naṃ) kartavyam arcayet kramā vidhiḥ ॥
tadbimbāvāhanaṃ kṛtvā *pūrvoktavidhinā saha ।* (l.7)
dhūpadīpaṃ ca naivedyam upacāraṃ gurukramāt ॥
jalamadhye prabhā kṛtvā catustambhasamanvitam ।
vitānadhvajasaṃyuktaṃ *puṣpamālābhiśobhitam ॥* (p.46b, f.71a, rakṣābandhanam, l.1)
tanmadhye bimba(mbaṃ) saṃsthāpya śivaśabdaṃ ca ghoṣayet ।
pīṭhe vā phalake vāpi kaṇṭhamātre jale guruḥ ॥
pratimāṃśca yayettatra *prākśiraścordhvavaktrakam ।* (l.2)
namakaṃ camakaṃ caiva śrīsūktaṃ puruṣasūktakam ॥
brāhmaṇān svastighoṣeṇa caturvedasamanvitam ।
japetkumbhasamīpena *ācāryāya kramaṃ tathā ॥* (l.3)
jape(pet) dvādaśa(śā)sā?ha(hā)ntaṃ tadardhaṃ vā tadardhakam ।
trirātram ekarātraṃ vā yāmadvaya(yaṃ) vidhīyate ॥
brāhme muhūrte ma(a)thavā kāraye*jjalaśāyinā ।* (l.4)
paścāduddhṛtya bimbena abhiṣekaṃ tu pūrvavat ॥
kumbhābhiṣekasaṃyuktam arcanāvāhanaṃ kuru ।
dhūpadīpaṃ ca naivaidyaṃ *tāmbūlamupacārakam ॥* (l.5)
devayānaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā praveśādālayaṃ prati ।
jalādhivāsanaṃ kṛtvā rakṣābandhana(naṃ) kārayet ॥

(rakṣābandhanam -) (l.6)
devāgre sthaṇḍilaṃ kalpya saṅkalpavidhipūrvakam ।
puṇyāhaprokṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā sāmānyārghyaṃ ca kalpitam ॥
aṅganyāsakaranyāsaṃ devānā*marcayet kramāt ।* (l.7)
ādhārādīṃ tu pūjāṃ ca dhyānamāvāhanaṃ tathā ।
dhūpadīpaṃ nyasettatra upacārakramaṃ bhavet ॥
svarṇasūtraṃ viśeṣeṇa *dukūlaṃ vā viśeṣataḥ ।* (p.47a, l.1)
tūlikāsūtramathavā kanyā saṃkārayet kramāt ॥
navarudropacāraṃ syāt tantusaṅkhyā prakīrtitāḥ ।
tatsūtraṃ rajanīmiśraṃ *sarpākāramalaṅkṛtam ॥* (l.2)
tatpātraṃ nālikeraṃ ca tāmbūlaṃ bhasma taṇḍulam ।
kūrcadvayaṃ ca saṃsthāpya vastreṇopari veṣṭitam ॥
paricāraka*mūrdhnistu prākāre tu pradakṣiṇam ।* (l.3)
śaṅkhadundubhinirghoṣaistotramaṅgalavācakaiḥ ॥
devasannadhimāpnoti arcanādikramaṃ tathā । (l.4)
ṣaḍuddhvā(tthā)sanamabhyarcya tasyopari nidhāpayet ॥
nirīkṣaṇādiśuddhiṃ syāddhenumudrāṃ pradarśayet ।
saṃhitāmarcayet *kṛtvā dhūpadīpaṃ nivedayet ॥* (l.5)
triyambakena mantreṇa bhasmaṃ sūtreṇa yojayet ।
taṇḍulaṃ hastamuddhṛtya *tāmbūlamupari nyaset ॥* (l.6)
devaṃ karāgrakaṃ datvā paścāt kautukabandhanam ।
viśvettāte tu mantreṇa dhārayeddakṣiṇe *kare ॥* (l.7)
sṛṣṭisthityantasaṃhāraṃ tirobhāvamanugraham ।
pañcakṛtyaṃ ca mūrtīnāṃ dhārayet sarpakaṅkaṇam ॥
gaurīmimāyeti mantreṇa devī vāma*karaṃ(re) nyaset ।* (p.47b, f.72, śayanādhivāsanam, l.1)
bṛhatsāmiti mantreṇa sitabhasmaṃ tu lepayet ।
ācāryasahitaṃ dhārya arcayedaṣṭapuṣpakam ॥
dhūpadīpaṃ ca naivedyaṃ tāmbūlaṃ dāpayettathā । (l.2)
parāṅmukhārghyaṃ vinyasyāt paścāddhūpaṃ tu dīpayoḥ ॥
rakṣābandhanamityuktaṃ paścādyāgasya kalpitam । (l.3)

(śayanādhivāsanam -?)
pratyekaṃ vedikāṃ caiva vāyudeśe prakalpitam ।
tālamātrasamutsedhaṃ vistāraṃ tu dvihastakam ॥
upavedīṃ ca kartavyaṃ kāraye*ccaturaśrakam ।* (l.4)
athavā madhyavedīṃ tu anukūlyakramā bhavet ॥
tadūrdhve dhānyarāśistu aṣṭadroṇaiśca śālibhiḥ ।
tadardhaṃ taṇḍulaṃ caiva *tadardhaṃ tu tilairapi ॥* (l.5)
navadhānyastu lājānāṃ darbhapuṣpaiśca vinyaset ।
tasyopari nyasedvastram aṇḍajaśca vidhi(dhiḥ) kramāt ॥
mayūraṃ *kukkuṭaṃ haṃsaṃ balākāśca varāvadam ।* (l.6)
ścadāni? pañca saṃgrāhya aṇḍajaṃ ca nidhāpayet ॥
tasyopari nyasedvastram aṇḍajaṃ ca nidhāpayet । (p.48a, l.1)
kārpāsaṃ patrabījaṃ ca puṣpānāṃ tuścalāṭuvā ॥
kulikāṃ ca vidhā pañca upadhā muṇḍajaṃ tathā ।
tasyopari nyasedvastraṃ romajaṃ ca vidhikramāt ॥ (l.2)
mūṣikāśca paśūṃ caiva bhallūkaṃ ca marīmṛgam ।
pañcaromādisaṃgrāhya śayanopari vinyaset ॥
tasyopari nyasedvastraṃ carmajaṃ ca nidhāpayet ।
ha(a)ṅkuraṅga*hariṇaṃ dhenumūṣikasaṃyutam ॥* (l.3)
varmajaṃ ca vidhā pañca śayanasya viśeṣataḥ ।
tasyopari nyasedvastraṃ vāmajaṃ tu vidhīyate ॥
śvetaṃ raktaṃ *tathā pītaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ śyāmalakambaram ।* (l.4)
vāmajaṃ tu vidhā pañcamadaḥ parivinyaset ॥
aṇḍajaṃ śvetarūpaṃ tu muṇḍajaṃ raktavarṇakam । (l.5)
romajaṃ kṛṣṇavarṇaṃ ca vāmajaṃ paridhā bhavet ॥
tadrūpaṃ vāmajaṃ japya pañcavarṇamiti smṛtam ।
tasyordhve tu samabhyarcya pañcatattvena *arcayet ॥* (l.6)
ādhārādīṃ tu pūjāṃ ca kuṭilāntasya arcayet ।
calāsanasya pūjāṃ ca śāyayet devatā bhavet ।
arcayetu(ttu) vidhiṃ *caiva adhikaṃ beramarcayet ।* (l.7)
tattvatattveśvaraṃ caiva mūrtimūrtīśvaraṃ tathā ॥
tattvaṃ bhuvanavarṇānāṃ padamantrakalā nyaset ।
