aṣṭatriṃśatkalā*nyāsam akṣaranyāsapūrvakam ॥* (p.48b, f.73a, śayanalakṣaṇam, l.1)

(śayanalakṣaṇam -)
prākśiraścordhvavaktreṇa vastreṇācchādya homayet ।
nidrākumbhaṃ tu saṃsthāpya pūrvoktavidhinā sahā(ha) ॥ (l.2)
aiśānyāṃ śivakumbhaṃ tu aṣṭavidyeśvaraṃ tathā ।
kṣityādimarcaye kṛtvā sāyāntasya tu mā(ā)sanam ॥
āvāhanādipūjāṃ ca dhūpadīpe nivedayet ।
lambakūrcaṃ *samabhyarcya paścāddhomaṃ samācaret ॥* (l.3)
yāgeśvarasya pūjāṃ ca aṣṭapuṣpaṃ tu vinyaset ।
dhūpadīpārghyaṃ vinyasyāt paścā*dvijñāpayecchivam ॥* (l.4)
arghyepātreṇa saṃgrāhya yāgaśālāpradakṣiṇam ।
paścātpūrvoktamārgeṇa agnikāryeṇa homayet ॥ (l.5)
śivāgniṃ kalpayet kṛtvā pūrṇāṃ hutvā samācaret ।
homānte kumbhayeddevaṃ arcayet sāṣṭapuṣpakam ॥
dhūpadīpaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā *tataḥ sthāpanakarmaṇi ।* (l.6)
śilpaśāstroktamārgeṇa ālayasya prakalpitam ॥
gomayālepanaṃ kṛtvā garbhagrahārtha(rdha)maṇṭape ।
garbha*gṛhaṃ tu vistāraṃ viṃśatirbhāgamācaret ॥* (l.7)
avatāraṃ tyajet pūrve prathamaṃ śaṅkarasya tu ।
dvitīyaṃ brahmabhāgaṃ tu tri(tṛ)tīyaṃ viṣṇurucyate ॥
caturthaṃ ṣaṇmukhaṃ *sthāpya pañcamaṃ tu sarasvatī ।* (p.49a, l.1)
ṣaṣṭhamam arhaboddhaṃ ca saptamaṃ tu vināyakam ॥
aṣṭamaṃ bhairavasthānaṃ navamaṃ śaktirucyate ।
daśamaṃ bhadrakāliṃ ca tatpadaṃ *kṣudradevatam ॥* (l.2)
tattadbhāgena saṃsthāpya pūjayedviśeṣataḥ ।
yathā devaṃ tathā bhāgaṃ pīṭhayecchilpimārgataḥ ॥
puṇyāhaprokṣaṇaṃ caiva pañcagavyena śuddhitam । (l.3)
tadbhāgaṃ sthaṇḍilaṃ kṛtvā navabhāgaṃ tu mā(ā)likhet ॥
pūrvoktenaiva mārgeṇa arcayennavaratnakam ।
vajramauktika*vaiḍūryaṃ sphaṭikaṃ puṣyarāgakam ॥* (l.4)
māṇikkaṃ padmarāgaṃ ca indranīlapravālakam ।
kāñcanaṃ navaratnānāṃ nikṣipettu viśeṣataḥ ॥ (l.5)
tasyopari śilāyānāṃ vinyasettu kramāvi(dvi)dhiḥ ।
ālayasya alaṅkāraṃ antarbāhyaṃ ca śuddhitam ॥
vātānadhvajasaṃyuktaṃ *kadalīstambhasaṃyutam ।* (l.6)
ghoṣayetsarvavādyena vedaghoṣasamanvitam ॥
śayanoddhṛtabimbānāṃ sarvālaṅkārasaṃyutam ।
ācāryasādhakā *grāhya ālayasya praveśayet ॥* (p.49b, f.74a, aṣṭabandhanam, l.1)
sumuhūrtena saṃveṣṭya sthāpayet sthiralagnakam ।
kiñcidaiśānyaprācīnāṃ samadṛṣṭiṃ ca kārayet ॥
arcayedvidhānena *ādhārādīn tu pūjayet ।* (l.2)
sthirāsanasya ma(?)bhyarcya śivāṅgasya prapūjayet ॥
dhūpadīpaṃ tu naivedyaṃ tāmbūlaṃ tu nivedayet ।
sthāpayedviśeṣeṇa *aṣṭabandhanakarmaṇi ॥* (l.3)


(aṣṭabandhanam -)
devāgre caturaśraṃ ca gomayā(ye)nānulepayet ।
lūkhalaṃ musalaṃ caiva prokṣayet *śuddhavāriṇā ॥* (l.4)
ṣaḍuddhvā(tthā)sanamabhyarcya tasyopari nidhāpayet ।
lūkhalaṃ brahmadaivatyaṃ musalaṃ viṣṇudevatam ॥
vastradarbhai*ralaṅkṛtyā(tya) lūkhalaṃ musalasya tu ।* (l.5)
kadalīśakhacūrṇaṃ ca lākṣādgudgulaṃ tathā ॥
śarkarāmadhusaṃyuktaṃ kārpāsaṃ tailameva ca ।
ete tu *samabhāgāni kārayedaṣṭabandhanam ॥* (l.6)
mahiṣā navanītaṃ ca cūrṇagudgulasaṃyutam ।
kārpāsaṃ lākṣasaṃyuktaṃ kārayet pañcabandhanam ॥ (l.7)
cūrṇagudgulasaṃyuktaṃ navanītaṃ ca miśra(śri)tam ।
tribandhanamiti jñeyaṃ pakvāpakvaṃ tu bandhanam ॥
ekārdhaṃ cūrṇabhāgaṃ tu bhāgamekaṃ tu gudgulam ।
navanītaṃ ca sammiśraṃ *śīghrakuṭṭanabandhanam ॥* (p.50a, l.1)
sādhakācārya(rcaṃ) saṃgrāhya deva(vaṃ) mūlena bandhayet ।
aṣṭabandhanakarmāṇi abhiṣekamathācaret ॥
ācārya(ryaḥ) snānasaṃkṛtvālaṅkṛtya *vidhīyate ।* (l.2)
bhasmarudrākṣasaṃyuktaṃ sapavitrakarānvitam ॥
sakalīkṛtya sāmānyaṃ viśeṣārghyaṃ ca kalpitam ।
snapanaṃ pañcagavyena pañcāmṛtasya kalpitam ॥
hasta*mātrapravistāraṃ sthaṇḍilopari vinyaset ।* (l.3)
pañca pañca padaṃ kṛtvā pañcapātreṣu pūjayet ॥
īśānādīśasarvāntaṃ pūjaye*tkramādvidhiḥ ।* (l.4)
āvāhanārcanaṃ kuryāt pañcamudrāṃ pradarśayet ॥
naivedyaṃ dhūpadīpaṃ ca prokṣayedastramantrataḥ ।
kṣīraṃ dadhi*ghṛtaṃ caiva madhuśarkarasaṃyutam ॥* (l.5)
pañcāmṛtasya pañcāṅgam abhiṣekaṃ pṛthak pṛthak ।
kadalī nālikeraṃ ca madhuśarkara*goghṛtam ॥* (l.6)
phalā ca pañcadhā proktaṃ pañcāmṛtasya kalpitam ।
paścādvighneśamabhyarcya dvārapūjāmataḥparam ॥
gaṇapatiṃ ca sarasvatyaṃ madhye *lakṣmīṃ ca pūjayet ।* (l.7)
nandigaṅgāmahākālaṃ yamunāṃ ca prapūjayet ॥
astradvāramabhyarcya arghyaṃ datvā krameṇa tu । (p.50b, f.75a, l.1)
vāhanādipūjāṃ ca dhenumudrāṃ pradarśayet ॥
dhūpadīpaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā divyadṛṣṭyāvalokayet ।
tasmāt pāśupatāstreṇa vighnānuccāṭanakramāt ॥ (l.2)
vimalaṃ ca subāhuṃ ca arcayetkavāṭayoḥ ।
vāstubrahmāṇamabhyarcya naiṛtyāṃ tu prapūjayet ॥
ghaṇṭānutāḍayet kṛtvā sarvavādyaṃ ca ghoṣayet । (l.3)
uttarābhimukho bhūtvā sārghyapātraṃ ca kalpitam ॥
saṅkalpamiti puṇyāhaṃ karanyāsādipūrvavat ।
ātmasthānaṃ tu dravyaṃ syāt mantraliṅgasya *śuddhitam ॥* (l.4)
namakaṃ camakaṃ japya rudranyāsādi pūrvavat ।
tilaṃ tailābhiṣekaṃ ca likucai(caiḥ) phalasārakam ॥
piṣṭāmalarajanyaṃ ca pañcagavyābhiṣecanam । (l.5)
kumbhābhiṣekavidhivat punarabhyaṅgamācaret ॥
pañcāmṛtādisaṃsnāpya paścāt phalādipañcakam ।
kṣīraṃ dadhi ghṛtaṃ *caiva madhuśarkarapūjayet ॥* (l.6)
ikṣusārābhiṣekaṃ ca nālikerodakaṃ tathā ।
svarṇaratnodakaṃ caiva kuśairbilvodakaṃ tathā । (l.7)
gandhamantrodakaṃ caiva karpūrasnānamācaret ॥
madhye śuddhābhiṣekaṃ ca mūrdhni puṣpaṃ tu vinyaset ।
śuklavastreṇa saṃmṛjyā(jya) *śodhayettu viśeṣataḥ ॥* (p.51a, l.1)
śuddhavastrairalaṅkṛtya gandhamālyamalaṅkṛtam ।
kastūriṃ caiva karpūraṃ kuṅkumāṅgena kalpitam ॥
arcayettu vidhānena ācāryāya kramaṃ tathā । (l.2)
ādhārādilayāṅgaparyantaṃ pūjayet । 
mṛtsaṃgrahaṇamārabhya dvitīyamaṅkurārpaṇam । 
tri(tṛ)tīyaṃ tu nayanonmīlanaṃ catuḥ pañcamaṃ *pratimāśuddhi ṣaṣṭhaṃ grāmapradakṣiṇam ॥* (l.3)
jalādhivāsanaṃ sapta rakṣābandhanamaṣṭamam ।
navamaṃ vāstupūjā ca daśamaṃ pañcagavyakam ॥
rudre maṇṭapa*samskāraṃ dvādaśaṃ śayanaṃ tu vā ।* (l.4)
homakarma trayodaśyāṃ sthāpanaṃ ca caturdaśā(śa) ॥
tithisaṅkhyeṣu bandhaṃ ca ṣoḍaśaṃ mantra*pūjanam ।* (l.5)
saptādaśaṃ tathā nāmaṃ vivāho'ṣṭādaśaṃ bhavet ॥
aṣādaśavidhānena mṛtsaṃgrahaṇapūrvake । (p.51b, f.76a, subrahmaṇyamaṇṭapapūjai, l.1)

(subrahmaṇyamaṇṭapapūjai -)
atha maṇṭapapūjāvidhirucyate –
prāsādi(da)nirmāṇāya aṅkurārpaṇaṃ kṛtvā । vāstupūjāṃ vidhāya । maṇṭapādikuṇḍaveditoraṇasṛk(l.2)sṛvāṣṭamaṅgaladaśāyudha-vitānadhvajadarbhamālākadalīpūgamālikuśapuṣpadukūlabadarikeśagātraveṣṭanadarpaṇamuktāśūḍādiralaṅkṛtya (l.3) ghaṭakalaśavardhanyāṃ lakṣaṇoktavatra(ttri)tantunā tiladhāmya(dhānyā)ntarāṇi saṃveṣṭya । tasya kṣālanadhūmraśuddhi(ddhiḥ) syāvi(dvi)dhivacchāli-taṇḍulatila(l.4)lājapuṣpadarbhān saṃgrāhyā(hya) । salilaratnakūrcāmrapallavanālikeravastragandhākṣatadravyādīn samarpya । (l.5) varṇe yedyuttarapaścima(me) prastīrya । guruḥ pādau prakṣālyācamya । yāgaśālāyāṃ paścimadvāraṃ praviśya । naiṛtideśamāsādyā(dya) । (l.6) gaṇeśaṃ saṃpūjya । nālikerādi naivedya dhūpadīpārghyaṃ datvā । yāgarakṣārthaṃ prārthya । puṇyāhaṃ vācayitvā (l.7) gurupādaṃ namaskṛtya । tacchirasi dhṛtvā deśiko navavastropavītagandhamakuṭakuṇḍalahārakeyūrakaṭakabhasmarudrākṣa-mālādibhi(p.52a, sūryapūjai(jā), sāmimaṇṭapapūjaiலே இது வாசிக்க, l.1)रलङ्कृत्य मण्टपे तत्तत्स्थानेषु व्रीहितण्डुलानास्तीर्य । वेदिमध्ये । यवतण्डुललाजसप्तधान्याष्टपत्रमालिख्या(ख्य) । कुम्भेषु <<(l.2) salilamāpūryā(rya) । saptaviṃśatikumbhādisarvakumbhān jalamāpūrya ratnakūrcāmrapallavavidhānanālikeraḍāḍimīphalanavavastrābhyāṃ saṃveṣṭyā(ṣṭya) । (l.3) darbhānāstīrya । vedikāmadhye pṛthivyādidevatāḥ saṃpūjya । dhūpadīpārghyaṃ datvā vardhanīdvayasahitaekādaśakumbhān saṃsthāpyā(pya) (l.4) tadīśāne yāgaguhe-śvaravardhanīṃ saṃsthāpya । parivārakumbhān tattatsthāne saṃsthāpya । sarvālaṅkāraṃ kṛtvā maṇṭapapūjāṃ (l.5) kuryāt । śa(śā)krānaladiśormadhye । nityavacchivasūryaṃ saṃpūjya । mūrtipaiḥ sahitamakhālayaṃ gatvā kṛtasakalīkaraṇaḥ । (l.6) praṇava-sahitasāmānyārghyapātre astreṇa saṃśodhyā(dhya) । hṛdayena vauṣaḍantena śuddhajalenāpūrya । praṇavena saptadhā ca (l.7) saṃpūjyā-bhimantrya । saṃrakṣyāvakuṇṭhyā(ṭhya) । amṛtīkaraṇāni vidhāya । sāmānyārghyapātre āsthānamaṇṭapāt bāhye pūrvādidvārastha-toraṇānyastreṇa (p.52b, f.77a, l.1) saṃprokṣya । kavacenāvakuṇṭhya । pūrvatoraṇamadhye oṃ hāṃ śāntitoraṇāya namaḥ । ūrdhve – oṃ sāṃ ṛgvedāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ kṛtayugāya namaḥ । triśūle oṃ sāṃ pūṣṇe (l.2) namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ dhātrai namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ vidhātre namaḥ । dakṣiṇetara(ṇottara)śākhāmūlayoḥ । oṃ sāṃ bhūlokāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ bhuvarlokāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ pratyūṣāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ prabhāsāya (l.3) namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ somāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ bhaumāya namaḥ । punaḥ ūrdhaave । oṃ sāṃ gaṇapataye namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ sarasvatyai namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ mahālakṣmyai namaḥ । punaḥ śāśayoḥ (l.4) oṃ sāṃ sudehāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ sumukhāya namaḥ । oṃ sām astrāya dvārapālāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ avargāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ kavargāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ (l.5) īśvarāya kāraṇeśvarāya namaḥ । oṃ seṃ tatpuruṣavaktrāya namaḥ । evaṃ pūrvadvāreṇa saṃpūjya । dakṣiṇadvāratoraṇe – astreṇa saṃprokṣya । kavacenā(l.6)vakuṇṭhya । toraṇe – oṃ sāṃ bhūtitoraṇāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ vijayāya namaḥ । oṃ sām indrajayāya namaḥ । śākhāmūlayoḥ । oṃ sāṃ suvarlokāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ (l.7) maharlokāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ ajakha(gha)nyāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ jakha(gha)nyāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ budhāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ bṛhaspataye namaḥ । dvāraūrdhve – vināyakāya namaḥ । oṃ (p.53a, l.1) sāṃ sarasvatyai namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ mahālakṣmyai namaḥ । śākhayoḥ – oṃ sāṃ mahāvalyai namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ senāpataye namaḥ । adhaḥ – oṃ sām astrāya dvāra(l.2)pālāya namaḥ । śākhayoḥ – oṃ sāṃ cavargāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ kavargāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ rudrāya kāraṇeśvarāya namaḥ । oṃ sām aghorahṛdayāya namaḥ । iti dakṣiṇadvāreṇa (l.3) saṃpūjya । paścimadvāre – astreṇa saṃprokṣya । kavacenāvakuṇṭhya । teraṇe – oṃ sāṃ balatoraṇāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ vajrāya namaḥ । (l.4) ūrdhve – oṃ sāmavedāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ dvāparayugāya namaḥ । triśūle – oṃ sām aryamṇe namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ mitrāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ tvaṣṭre namaḥ । śākhayoḥ – (l.5) oṃ sāṃ janalokāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ taparlokāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ vidhātre namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ dhātre namaḥ । bhārgavāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ śanaiścarāya (l.6) namaḥ । ūrdhve – oṃ sāṃ gaṇapataye namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ sarasvatyai namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ mahālakṣmyai namaḥ । śākhayoḥ – oṃ sāṃ mayūrāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ (l.7) gajendrāya namaḥ । adhaḥ – oṃ sām astrāya dvārapālāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ tavargāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ pavargāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ brahmaṇe kāraṇeśvarāya namaḥ । oṃ (l.8) oṃ sāṃ sadyojātamūrtaye namaḥ । iti paścimadvāreṇa saṃpūjya । uttaradvāre – astreṇa saṃprokṣya । kavacenāvakuṇṭhya । toraṇe oṃ sām (p.53b, f.78a, l.1) ārogyatoraṇāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ nīlalohitāya namaḥ । ūrdhve – oṃ sāṃ atharve(rvave)dāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ kaliyugāya namaḥ । triśūle – oṃ sāṃ viṣṇave namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ sāvitre namaḥ । oṃ sām (l.2) aṃśumate namaḥ । śākhāmūlayoḥ । oṃ sāṃ satyalokāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ dhṛvarlokāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ sarasvatyai? namaḥ । oṃ sām āvāhāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ vivāhāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ (l.3) rāhave namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ ketave namaḥ । ūrdhve – oṃ sāṃ gaṇapataye namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ sarasvatyai namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ mahālakṣmyai namaḥ । śākhayoḥ – oṃ sāṃ (l.4) devasenāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ sumitrāya namaḥ । adhaḥ oṃ sām astrāya dvārapālāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ yavargāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ śavargāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ viṣṇave (l.5) kāraṇeśvarāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ vāmadevaguhyā namaḥ । iti uttaradvāreṇa saṃpūjyārghyaṃ datvā । bhavadbhiryajñarakṣakatvena sthātavyamāhitādare(l.6)riti vijñāpya । pūrvadvāramastreṇa saṃprokṣya । pūrvadvārasya tripadottarakumbhe – oṃ sāṃ śāntikalāsanāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ śāntikalāmūrtaye (l.7) namaḥ ।
dhyānam –
caturvaktraṃ caturbāhuṃ kṛrṇavarṇaṃ mahābalam ।
dhvajābhayākṣamālābjā śāntiḥ sarpopavītinam ॥

	iti dhyātvā । oṃ sāṃ śāntikalāsanāya namaḥ । (p.54a, l.1) iti saṃpūjya । naivedyadhūpadīpasarvopacāraiḥ saṃpūjya । dakṣiṇadvāramastreṇa saṃprokṣya । dakṣiṇadvārasya pūrvapadakumbhe । oṃ sāṃ vidyākalāsanāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ vidyā(l.2)kalāmūrtaye namaḥ ।

raktavarṇaṃ kapardendutriyakṣāśca caturbhujām ।
śaktyābhayākṣamālābjāṃ vidyājvalanasannibhām ॥

	iti dhyātvā । oṃ sāṃ vidyākalāya namaḥ । iti (l.3) saṃpūjya । naivedyadhūpadīpaṃ datvā । paścimadvāramastreṇa saṃprokṣya । paścimadvārasya dakṣiṇapadakumbhe । oṃ sāṃ nivṛttikalāsanāya namaḥ । (l.4) oṃ sā(sāṃ) nivṛttikalāmūrtaye namaḥ । 

taptacāmīkarā(rāṃ)bhasā caturvaktrendubhūṣitām ।
vajrābhayākṣamālābjā nivṛttisvarṇakāntikā ॥

	iti (l.5) dhyātvā । nivṛttikalāya namaḥ । iti saṃpūjya । naivedyadhūpadīpasarvopacāraiḥ saṃpūjya । uttaradvāramastreṇa saṃprokṣya (l.6) uttaradvārasya paścimapadakumbhe – oṃ sāṃ pratiṣṭhākalāsanāya namaḥ । pratiṣṭhākalāmūrtaye namaḥ ।

śuddhasphaṭikasaṃkāśaṃ ekavaktraṃ *caturbhujam ।* (l.7)
śaktyābhayākṣamālābjā pratiṣṭhāratnabhūṣitām ॥

	iti dhyātvā । oṃ sāṃ pratiṣṭhākalāya namaḥ । iti saṃpūjya । naivedyadhūpadīpasarvo(p.54b, f.79a, l.1)pacāraiḥ saṃpūjya । pūrvadvārasya uttarapadakumbhe – oṃ sāṃ sudehāsanāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ sudehamūrtaye namaḥ ।

ekavaktraṃ dvibāhu(huṃ) ca gadābhayadharo *tathā ।* (l.2)
raktaśyāmalavarṇābhau sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam ॥
sudaṃṣṭrau smitavaktrau ca karaṇḍamakuṭojvalau ।

	iti dhyātvā । oṃ sāṃ sudehāya namaḥ । iti (l.3) saṃpūjya । naivedyādisarvopacāraiḥ saṃpūjya । pūrvadvārasya dakṣiṇapadakumbhe – oṃ sāṃ sumukhāsanāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ (l.4) sumukhamūrtaye namaḥ । 

ekavaktraṃ dvibāhū cā(ca) gadābhayadharo tathā ।
varṇaṃ śyāmalaṃ raktaṃ ca sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam ॥

	iti dhyātvā । oṃ sāṃ (l.5) sumukhāya namaḥ । iti saṃpūjya । dhūpadīpasarvopacāraiḥ saṃpūjya । dakṣiṇadvārakumbhasya pūrvapadakumbhe – oṃ sāṃ mahālakṣmyā(l.6)sanāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ mahālakṣmīmūrtaye namaḥ ।

kuñcitaṃ vāmapādaṃ tu savyapādaṃ pralambitam ।
padmadhṛk vāmahastaṃ ca savyahastaṃ pralimbitam ॥ (p.55a, l.1)
stanakañcukasaṃyuktaṃ mahālakṣmisvarūpakam ।

	iti dhyātvā । oṃ hāṃ mahāvalyai namaḥ । naivedyādisarvopacāraiḥ saṃpūjya । tadvārasya paścimapadakumbhe – (l.2)

oṃ sāṃ senāpatāsanāya namaḥ । senāpatamūrtaye namaḥ । 
ṣaḍvaktraṃ ca daśabhujaṃ priyāliṅganavāmaka ।
koṭisūryapratīkāśaṃ dhyāyet senā*patiṃ vibhum ॥* (l.3)

	iti dhyātvā । oṃ sāṃ senāpataye namaḥ । naivedyādisarvopacāraiḥ saṃpūjya । paścimadvārakumbhasya dakṣiṇa-(l.4)padakumbhe – oṃ sāṃ mayūrāsanāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ mayūramūrtaye namaḥ । 

citravarṇaṃ surārighnaṃ bhujagoddhṛtavaktrakam ।
mahābalamahā*kāyaṃ mayūraṃ smitavaktrakam ॥* (l.5)

	iti dhyātvā । oṃ sāṃ mayūrāya namaḥ । iti saṃpūjya । naivedyādisarvopacāraiḥ saṃpūjya । (l.6) taddvārasya uttarapadakumbhe – oṃ sāṃ gajendrāsanāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ gajendramūrtaye namaḥ । 

erāvatakule jātaṃ śvetarūpaṃ mahā*balam ।* (p.55b, f.80a, l.1)
catuśṛṅgasamāyuktaṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam ॥ 

	iti dhyātvā । oṃ sāṃ gajendrāya namaḥ । iti saṃpūjya । naivedyādisarvopacāraiḥ saṃpūjya । uttara(l.2)dvārakumbhasya paścimapadakumbhe oṃ sāṃ devasenāsanāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ devasenāmūrtaye namaḥ । 

kuñcitaṃ dakṣiṇe(ṇaṃ) pādaṃ vāmapādaṃ pralambitam । (l.3)
padmadhṛk savyahastaṃ ca vāmahastaṃ pralambitam ॥
raktavarṇaṃ viśālākṣaṃ devasenāsvarūpakam ।

	oṃ sāṃ devasenāya namaḥ । (l.4) ityabhyarcya । naivedyādisarvopacāraiḥ saṃpūjya । tadvārasya pūrvapadakumbhe । oṃ sāṃ sumitrāsanāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ sumitramūrtaye (l.5) namaḥ । 

dvinetraṃ dvibhujaṃ raktaṃ suprasannaṃ susauvanam ।
dakṣiṇe śaktisaṃyuktaṃ karadvayakṛtāñjalim ॥

	iti dhyātvā । oṃ sāṃ (l.6) sumitrāya namaḥ । naivedyādisarvopacāraiḥ saṃpūjya । iti pratidvāraṃ tayoḥ pārśvayoḥ kalaśayoḥ sthaṇḍile kūrcau bhittau ca tattaddhyānapurassaram । dvārapālān saṃpūjya । lokasamaprabhāṃ dhyātvā । dhvajāṣṭakaṃ pūjayet । tathaiva tacchākhayoḥ । (p.56a, l.1) iti pratiṣṭhāyāṃ viśeṣa uttamaḥ । pūrvadvārabāhyottare – oṃ sāṃ praśāntapatākāya namaḥ । dakṣiṇe oṃ sāṃ śiśirapatākāya namaḥ । dakṣiṇadvārapūrve oṃ sāṃ (l.2) parjanyapatākāya namaḥ । tatpaścime – oṃ sāṃ jayantapatākāya namaḥ । paścimadvāradakṣiṇe oṃ sāṃ bhūtasañjīvapatākāya namaḥ । taduttare – oṃ sām amṛta(l.3)patākāya namaḥ । uttaradvārapaścime - oṃ sāṃ nādapatākāya namaḥ । tatpūrve oṃ sāṃ śrīpradāyapatākāya namaḥ । 

raktavarṇāḥ praśāntādyāḥ *dvibhujāśca dvilocanāḥ ।* (l.4)
kṛtāñjalipuṭā hyeṣāṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitāḥ ॥

	iti dhyātvā । patākān saṃpūjya । tathaiva tacchākhayoḥ (l.5) gajāṣṭakaṃ pūjayet । pūrve - oṃ sāṃ kumudāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ kumudākṣāya namaḥ । dakṣiṇe – oṃ sāṃ puṇḍarīkākṣāya namaḥ । oṃ (l.6) sāṃ śaṅkukarṇāya namaḥ । paścime – oṃ sāṃ sarvanetrāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ sumukhāya namaḥ । uttare – oṃ sāṃ supratiṣṭhāya namaḥ । (l.7) oṃ sāṃ vāmanāya namaḥ ।

dhyānam –
kumudādyān sitārkālīn kṛṣṇābhendraśarāḥ śubhān ।
dvilocanān dvibāhūnśca daṇḍābhayakarānvitān ॥ (p.56b, f.81a, l.1)

	praṇavabījapurassaraṃ dhyānādinā saṃpūjya । paścimadvāre nityavaddvārapālānabhyarcya । tasyordhve udumbaradakṣiṇabhāge ।

lambodaraṃ śyāmanibhaṃ gaṇeśaṃ *kuṭhāramakṣaśrajamūrdhvagābhyām ।* (l.2)
salaḍḍukaṃ dantamadhaḥkarābhyāṃ vāmetarābhyāṃ ca dadhānamīḍe ॥

	iti dhyātvā । oṃ sāṃ gaṇapataye namaḥ । iti saṃpūjya (l.3) taduttarapārśve –

śuklāṃ śuklāmbarāmakṣamālā pustakadhāriṇīm ।
savyavāmakarābhyāṃ ca suprasannāṃ sarasvatīm ॥

	iti dhyātvā । (l.4) oṃ sāṃ sarasvatyai namaḥ । ityabhyarcya । tayormadhye –

vāmadakṣiṇahastābhyāṃ dadhatīṃ śrīphalāmbuje ।
hemābhāṃ *saghaṭebhābhyāṃ plāvyamānāṃ mahāśriyam ॥* (l.5)

	iti dhyātvā । oṃ sāṃ mahālakṣmyai namaḥ । ityabhyarcya । ātmano dakṣiṇaśākhāyām – (l.6) oṃ sāṃ sudehāya namaḥ । vāmaśākhāyām – oṃ sāṃ sumukhāya namaḥ ।

dhyānam –
ekavaktraṃ dvibāhū ca gadābhayadharau tathā । (l.7)
raktaśyāmalavarṇābhau sarvābharaṇabhūṣitau ॥
sudaṃṣṭrau smitavaktrau ca karaṇḍamakuṭojvale ।

	iti dhyātvā । saṃpūjya । dakṣiṇaśākhe – (p.57a, l.1) oṃ sāṃ medhāyai namaḥ । vāmaśākhe – oṃ sāṃ prajñāya namaḥ । adhodumbare – oṃ sāṃ pṛthivyai namaḥ । gandhapuṣpadīpairabhyarcya । rakṣāmeṣaṃ (l.2) vijñāpya । nyastamūlena cakṣuṣordhvaṃ vilokya । digbandhanaastrāyaphaḍiti jvalannārājamudrayāstreṇa ....kṣiptvā । antarikṣakān huṃ phaḍiti (l.3) pāśupatāstramuccācarya । dakṣiṇapārṣṇikhātatrayeṇa bhūmiṣṭān trividhānvighnān yāgagrahamudvāsya । dvārasya madhye – astraṃ saṃpūjya । (l.4) kavāṭayoḥ । oṃ sāṃ jayāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ vijayāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ sarvebhyo dvārapālebhya(bhyaḥ) svāhetyarghyaṃ datvā । dhūpadīpaṃ (l.5) datvā । kiñcidvāmaśākhāyāṃ samāśrayan dakṣiṇapādapurassaram antaḥ praviśya । dehalyā oṃ saḥ astrāya dvārapālāya (l.6) namaḥ । ityastraṃ vakṣyamāṇarūpaṃ vibhāvya । saṃpūjyā(jya) । yāgaśālāyāṃ pradakṣiṇyena gatvā । naiṛtiṃ praviśya ।
prāk (l.7) tripadamadhyasthitakalaśe – oṃ sāṃ brahmāsanāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ brahmamūrtaye namaḥ । 

dhyānam –
brahmā caturmukho dhātā kamaṇḍalvakṣa*mālikā ।* (p.57b, f.82a, l.1)
siṃhārūḍhasamāyātu tvāsīno dvibhujāśrayam ॥

	iti dhyātvā । oṃ hāṃ vāstvadhipataye brahmaṇe namaḥ । ityabhyarcya । pādyācamanaṃ saṃpūjya । (l.2) tṛṇādyācchādita-yāgagrāścet । maṇṭapordhvasthiteṣu । kambyādiṣu । kambyeṣu । oṃ sāṃ pṛthivītattvāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ pṛthivītattvādhipataye (l.3) brahmaṇe namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ sadyojātamūrtaye namaḥ । tṛṇeṣu – oṃ sām aptattvāya namaḥ । oṃ sām aptattvādhipataye viṣṇave (l.4) namaḥ । oṃ siṃ vāmadevaguhyāya namaḥ । bandheṣu । oṃ sāṃ tejastattvāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ tejastattvādhipataye rudrāya namaḥ । (l.5) oṃ sūm aghorahṛdayāya namaḥ । vaṃśeṣu oṃ sāṃ vāyusta(ta)ttvāya namaḥ । vāyusta(ta)ttvādhipataye īśvarāya namaḥ । (l.6) oṃ seṃ tatpuruṣavaktrāya namaḥ । sthūṇeṣu । oṃ sām ākāśatattvāya namaḥ । oṃ sām ākāśa-tattvādhipataye sadāśivāya namaḥ । (l.7) oṃ som īśānamūrdhāya namaḥ । iti saṃpūjya । vitāneṣu । oṃ sāṃ candrāya namaḥ । taraṅgeṣu oṃ sāṃ jāhvai(jāhnavyai) namaḥ । darpaṇe oṃ sāṃ (p.58a, l.1) sūryāya namaḥ । darbhamālāyām oṃ sāṃ vāsukyai namaḥ । guhatattvāntavyāptikamaṇṭapaṃ vibhāvya । paścādupavedyāntarāleṣu aṣṭamaṅgaladevān (l.2) saṃpūjya । pūrve oṃ sāṃ darpaṇāya namaḥ । oṃ sām ūrvaśyai namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ skandāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ pūrṇakumbhāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ menakāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ (l.3) balodarāya namaḥ । dakṣiṇe oṃ sāṃ mayūrāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ rambhāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ vīrabhadrāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ cāmarābhyāṃ namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ (l.4) tilottamāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ gaṅgāyai namaḥ । paścime – oṃ sāṃ svastikāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ sumukhyai namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ rudrāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ (l.5) śrīvatsāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ sundaryai namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ mahālakṣmyai namaḥ । uttare - oṃ sāṃ śaṅkhāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ kāminyai namaḥ । oṃ sāndrāya (l.6) namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ dīpāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ kāmavardhanyai namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ śauryai namaḥ । evamaṣṭamaṅgaladevānabhyarcya । astraprākāra(l.7)kavacāvakuṇṭhanābhyāṃ yāga-maṇṭapaṃ saṃrakṣya । maṇḍalāddakṣimataḥ । kūrmāsanopari vyāghrājinādau saumyāśyopaviśya । pratiṣṭhāyām । (p.58b, f.83a, svāmimaṇṭapapūjai(ஜை)யிலெடுத்து வாசிக்க bhūtaśuddhi, l.1) मण्टपपूजामविहीनत्वात् । अत्र मुख्यपक्षम् । गुहमण्टले-श्वरयजनम् । गुहागमेषु निश्चितं नित्यवत् करन्यासं कृत्वा । अस्त्रेण हस्ततलपृष्ठौ <<(l.2) saṃśodhya । aṅguṣṭhādi-kaniṣṭhāntam oṃ somīśānāya namaḥ । oṃ seṃ tatpuruṣāya namaḥ । oṃ sūm aghorāya namaḥ । oṃ sīṃ vāmadevāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ (l.3) sadyojātāya namaḥ । talayoraṅguṣṭhābhyām oṃ sauṃ vidyādehāya namaḥ । madhyamābhyām oṃ sauṃ netrebhyo namaḥ । aṅguṣṭhābhyāṃ (l.4) punaḥ oṃ sāṃ sauṃ subrahmaṇyāya namaḥ । kaniṣṭhāṅguṣṭhāntam oṃ sāṃ hṛdayāya namaḥ । oṃ sīṃ śirase namaḥ । oṃ sūṃ śikhāyai namaḥ । (l.5) oṃ seṃ kavacāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ subrahmaṇyāya namaḥ । iti karanyāsaṃ kṛtvā । bhūtaśuddhiṃ vidhāya । pūrvavadaṅganyāsaṃ vidhāya । oṃ sāṃ (l.6) guhāsanāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ saṃ sāṃ guhamūrtaye namaḥ । oṃ som īśānamūrdhāya namaḥ । oṃ seṃ tatpuruṣavaktrāya namaḥ । oṃ sūm (l.7) aghorahṛdayāya namaḥ । oṃ sīṃ vāmadevaguhyāya namaḥ । oṃ saṃ sadyojātamūrtaye namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ sauṃ vidyādehāya namaḥ । oṃ sauṃ (p.59a, l.1) netrebhyo namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ sauṃ guhāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ hṛdayāya namaḥ । oṃ sīṃ śirase namaḥ । oṃ sūṃ śikhāyai vaiṣaṭ । oṃ saiṃ kavacāya hum । kaṇṭaḥ (l.2) prāvaraṇatvena stanamadhyāntaṃ tarjanībhyām oṃ sāṃ sauṃ guhāya vauṣaḍiti mahāmudrāṃ vinyaset । iti aṅganyāsaḥ ॥

	atha mātṛkānyāso yathā – oṃ am (l.3) amareśāya namaḥ । mūrdhni । ām ārādhyāya namaḥ । lalāṭe । im indrasutāpriyāya namaḥ । dakṣiṇanetre । ī īśasutāya namaḥ । vāmanetre । um umāputrāya namaḥ । (l.4) dakṣiṇakarṇe । ūm ūrdhvaretase namaḥ । vāmakarṇe । ṝṃ rudrabhuve namaḥ । dakṣiṇanāsāyām ṝṃ kumārāya namaḥ । vāmanāsikāyām । ḹṃ (l.5) lāvaṇyāya namaḥ । vāmagaṇḍe । oṃ ḹṃ kālakaṇṭhasutāya namaḥ । vāmagaṇḍe । eṃ vallipriyāya namaḥ । ūrdhvoṣṭre aim aiśvaryapradāya (l.6) namaḥ । adhoṣṭre om ojasvine namaḥ । ūrdhvadantapaṅktau aum aupamyarasitāya namaḥ । adhodanta-paṅktau am (l.7) ambujāsanāya namaḥ । tālumadhye – aḥ akṣaṇi tejase namaḥ । bhrūmadhye – kaṃ kamaṇḍaludharāya namaḥ । dakṣiṇahasteṣu – kakārādidakārāntaṃ (l.8) vinyasya । oṃ khaṃ khaḍgapāṇaye namaḥ । gaṃ gaurīsutāya namaḥ । ghaṃ ghaṇṭahastāya namaḥ । ṅaṃ ...?ṅapriyāya namaḥ । iti vinyasya । cakārādiñakārāntaṃ (p.59b, f.84a, l.1) vāmahasteṣu vinyaset । caṃ candraśekharāya namaḥ । chaṃ chandomayāya namaḥ । jaṃ jagadbhuve namaḥ । jhaṃ jhaṇḍi(ṇḍī)śāya namaḥ । ñaṃ ñānadāya namaḥ । iti vinyasya । (l.2) ṭakārādiṇakārāntaṃ dakṣiṇapādeṣu nyaset । ṭaṃ ṭam kahastāya namaḥ । ṭham ṭhasvarūpāya namaḥ । ḍam ḍambhāya namaḥ । ḍhaṃ ḍhakkāpriyāya namaḥ । ṇaṃ ṇagamyāya namaḥ । iti vinyasya । (l.3) takārādinakārāntaṃ vāmapādeṣu nyaset । tam tattvarūpāya namaḥ । tha sthaviṣṭāya namaḥ । dam daṇḍapāṇaye namaḥ । dham dhanuṣpāṇaye namaḥ । nam naga(l.4)randhrakṛte namaḥ । iti nyaset । dakṣiṇapārśve । pam pāśahastāya namaḥ । pham phaṇīndrabhugvāhanāya namaḥ । vāmapārśve – baṃ bahulāputrāya (l.5) namaḥ । bham bhavātmajāya namaḥ । iti nyaset । maṃ mahāsenā dakṣiṇatapṛṣṭhe । yaṃ yajñamūrtaye namaḥ । vāmapṛṣṭhe । raṃ ramaṇīyāya namaḥ । laṃ (l.6) lambodarānujāya namaḥ । iti kaṭipradeśe । vaṃ vacadbhuve namaḥ । kaṭisūtre । śaṃ śaravaṇabhavāya namaḥ । ṣaṃ ṣaḍānanāya namaḥ । (l.7) kuṇḍalopari vinyasya । saṃ sāramocanāya namaḥ । hṛdaye । haṃ harātmajāya namaḥ । makuṭe । ḻaṃ ḻakuḻīśāya namaḥ । meḍhradeśe । kṣaṃ (p.60a, l.1) kṣmāmūrtaye namaḥ । nābhideśe । iti mātṛkānyāsaḥ samāptaḥ । om sāṃ sadāśivātmane namaḥ । vaktre om rudrātmane namaḥ । hṛdaye । om sāṃ viṣṇuvātmane (l.2) namaḥ । udare । om sām brahmātmane namaḥ । nābhau । om sāṃ candramaṇḍalāya namaḥ । om sām ādityamaṇḍalāya namaḥ । urasi । om sām agnimaṇḍalāya namaḥ । (l.3) nābhau । 

aṣṭatriṃśatkalānyāsam – 
1.	oṃ sāṃ jagadbhuvaṃ jayāyai namaḥ । īśānamūrdhni ।
2.	oṃ sāṃ bahutobalāyai namaḥ । pūrvamūrdhni । (l.4)
3.	hutamunnādāyai namaḥ । dakṣiṇamūrdhni ।
4.	vacadbhuvaṃ varadāyai namaḥ । uttaramūrdhni ।
5.	namaste'stu nandinyai namaḥ । paścimamūrdhni ।
6.	viśvadbhuve (l.5) vijayāya namaḥ । naiṛtamūrdhni ।
7.	jagadbhuvo'dhipati jayāyai namaḥ । īśānavaktre ।
8.	senānisenāyai namaḥ । pūrvavaktre । (l.6)
9.	mayūrapriyāyai namaḥ । dakṣiṇavaktre ।
10.	ṣaḍānanāṅgāya śāntyai namaḥ । uttaravaktre ।
11.	satvāyāhaṃ namaste'stu nandinyai namaḥ । (l.7) paścimamukhe ।
12.	sukhaṃ vaha sūkṣmāyai namaḥ । naiṛtimukhe ।
13.	subrahmaṇyasumatyai namaḥ । hṛdaye ।
14.	buhaspate sutāprajñāyai namaḥ । gaḻe । (p.60b, f.85a, l.1)
15.	asya padmayo amṛtāyai namaḥ । dakṣiṇabāhau ।
16.	yasyātmayaśase namaḥ । vāmabāhau ।
17. vāhane vahantu variṣṭhāyai namaḥ । nābhau । (l.2)
18. subrahmaṇyāsupratiṣṭhāyai namaḥ । kukṣau ।
19. rudrabhuve rucāyai namaḥ । pṛṣṭhe ।
20. brahmabhuve brahmiṇyai namaḥ । urasi ।
21. bhuvadbhuve bhūtyai namaḥ । guhye ।
22. māmṛtāya (l.3) mānyai namaḥ । śiśne ।
23. jagadbhuve ṛtāyai namaḥ । dakṣiṇorau ।
24. subrahmaṇyaśuddhāyai namaḥ । vāmorau ।
25. kṛttikābhyusutakṛpāyai (l.4) namaḥ । dakṣiṇajānuni ।
26. ṣaṣṭhāṅgāya satvarāyai namaḥ । vāmajānuni ।
27. jagadbhuvo jagatyai namaḥ । dakṣiṇajaṅkhā(ṅghā)yām ।
28. vacadbhuvo (l.5) varadāyai namaḥ । vāmajaṅkhā(ṅghā)yām ।
29. skandaviśākhāti(di)ne kāmadāyai namaḥ । dakṣiṇapāde ।
30. havyaṃ yasmit(n) kariṇyai namaḥ । vāmapāde । (6)
31. ṣaṇmukhāya śyāmāyai namaḥ । vyāpakāñjaliḥ ।
32. oṃ sāṃ sauṃ vidyādehāya namaḥ । ?
33. oṃ sauṃ netrebhyo namaḥ । mūrdhādi(l.7)pādaparyantam ।
34. oṃ som īśānajagadbhuve namaḥ । ?
35. oṃ seṃ tatpuruṣavacadbhuve namaḥ । ?
36. oṃ sūm aghoraviśvabhuve namaḥ । ?
37. oṃ siṃ vāma(p.61a, l.1)devarudrabhuve namaḥ । ?
38. oṃ sāṃ sadyojātabrahmabhuve namaḥ । ?
39. oṃ soṃ seṃ sūṃ sīṃ saṃ sarvatrabhave namaḥ । iti daṇḍabhaṅgī ॥

īśānādiṣaṇmukhe – 
1. jagadbhuvaṃ jayāyai (l.2) namaḥ । īśānamūrdhni ।
2. bahuto balāyai namaḥ । pūrvamārdhni ।
3. hutamunnadāyai namaḥ । dakṣiṇamūrdhni ।
4. vacadbhuvaṃ vāmāyai namaḥ । uttaramūrdhni ।
5. namaste'stu (l.3) nandinyai namaḥ । paścimamūrdhni ।
6. viśvabhuve vijayāyai namaḥ । naiṛtamūrdhni ।

oṃ sāṃ hṛdayāya namaḥ । 1
oṃ sīṃ śirase namaḥ । 2
oṃ sāṃ (l.4) sūṃ śikhāyai vauṣaṭ । 3
oṃ sāṃ sauṃ kavacāya huṃ phaṭ । 4
oṃ sāṃ sauṃ netrebhyo vauṣaṭ । 5
oṃ sāṃ saḥ astrāya phaṭ (l.5) iti muṇḍabhaṅgī । 6.

(oṃ) soṃ i(ī)śānamūrdhāya namaḥ ।
oṃ seṃ tatpuruṣavaktrāya namaḥ ।
oṃ sūm aghorahṛdayāya namaḥ ।
oṃ sīṃ vāma(l.6)devaguhyāya namaḥ ।
oṃ saṃ sadyojātamūrtaye namaḥ ।
pūrve oṃ sauṃ netrebhyo namaḥ ।
aiśānyām oṃ sāṃ hṛdayāya namaḥ ।
āgneye (l.7) oṃ siṃ śirase namaḥ ।
aiśānyām oṃ sūṃ śikhāyai vauṣaṭ ।
naiṛte oṃ saiṃ kavacāya hum ।
vāyavye oṃ saḥ astrāya phaṭ । (p.61b, f.86a, l.1)
pūrvādicaturdaleṣu – tatpuruṣādibhyo vāmabhāge । iti vaktrabhaṅgī ।

	tataḥ antaryāgaṃ kṛtvā । antarhavanaṃ ca kṛtvā । hṛnnābhibindusthānāni pūjāhoma(l.2)samādhīnāṃ sthānaṃ saṅkalpya । vakṣyamāṇakrameṇa bhāvopanītapuṣpādairantarhṛtpadme guhaṃ sarvopacārairmanasā saṃpūjya । nābhikuṇḍe (l.3) svatassiddhe'gnau jñānānalaṃ vinyaset । tatra sūryāyutasamaprabhaṃ hṛtpuṇḍarīkamadhyā tu sauṣumnena padhā(thā)nalaṃ dvādaśāntapade (l.4) yojya tattejaḥ puñjapiñjaram । tena vinyastamātreṇa sarvakarmārhako bhavet । ityuktavat jñānānalaṃ vinyasya । guhaṃ saṃpūjya । (l.5) pūrakākṛṣṭena binduprasṛtāmṛtarūpeṇājyena homaṃ ca kṛtvā । antaḥ pūjitadevāya samarpya । pūjāṃ samāpya । bindau (l.6) sarvatomukhaṃ sphurattārakākāramīśvaraṃ dhyātvā । bahiḥ pūjayāmīti prārthya । labdhānujñasthānaśuddhimācaret । astreṇa (l.7) tālatrayaṃ datvā choṭikābhisthānān vighnānuccāṭyāstreṇa parito jvaladagnivarṇavajraprakārakavacena dakṣiṇatarjanī-bhramaṇena (p.62a, l.1) parikhātatrayaṃ sañcintya dhenumudrayā oṃ srauṃ hrīṃ śaktaye namaḥ । śaktijālamūrdhvādho vibhāvya । sarvatrakṛtarakṣāṃ bhāvayet । tataḥ । svarṇādinirmitamarghya(l.2)pātrādikamastreṇa prakṣālya । binduprasṛtāmṛtadhārāmayena śuddhā-mbunā vauṣaḍantahṛdayenāpūrya । gandhapuṣpadūrvākṣatādiarghyadravyāṇi ca nikṣipya । (l.3) tasminnāsanamūrtibrahmapañcaka-vidyādehanetramūlāṅgaiḥ punarmūlena ca saṃpūjya । saṃhitayābhimantryāstreṇa saṃrakṣya । kavacenā(l.4)vakuṇṭhyā(ṭhya) dhenumudrayā-mṛtīkṛtya । svāsane oṃ sāṃ guhāsanāya namaḥ । iti puṣpaṃ datvā । mūrtiṃ vinyasyā(sya) । candanena lalāṭe tilakaṃ (l.5) kṛtvā । ātmānamārādhyā(dhya) । mantraśuddhiṃ vidhāyātmani sāsanaṃ sabrahmāṅgaṃ sakalaṃ vinyasya । sṛṣṭimārgeṇeśo dhyātmānaṃ (l.6) sakalākhyaṃ vinyaset । oṃ sāṃ śāntyatītakalāyai namaḥ । oṃ saiṃ śāntikalāyai namaḥ । oṃ sūṃ vidyā-kalāyai namaḥ । oṃ sīṃ (l.7) pratiṣṭhākalāyai namaḥ । oṃ hlāṃ nivṛtti-kalāyai namaḥ । 

brahmarandhralalāṭāntaṃ tasmāt grīvāvadhiṃ tathā । 
nābhyantajānuparyantaṃ pādāntaṃ kramaśaḥ kalāḥ ॥ (p.62b, f.87a, pañcagavyam, sāmamaṇṭapapūjai(ஜைலெ எடுத்து வாசிக்க), l.1)

	इति तद्व्यापकं निष्कलं गुहमाविष्कृत्याङ्गन्यासावकुण्ठनादिकं विधाय । आत्मानं वस्त्र-गन्धाद्युष्णीषान्तै(न्तैः) प्रसाधनैरलङ्कृत्य । गुहहस्तं <<(l.2) kuryāt । tato dakṣahastaṃ tale vāmāṅguṣṭhena gandhalepite āsanamūrtimadhye vinyasyā....(ṅgu)ṣṭhābhyām īśānādipañcakaṃ vinyasya । vidyādeha(l.3)mūrtinetranyāsaṃ vidhāyā(ya) । guhamāvāhyāṅgāni kaniṣṭhādiṣu vinyasya paramīkṛtya svaśirasyadhiropya । brahmarandhrapraviṣṭena (l.4) tejasā bāhya(hyā)-bhyantaraṃ tamaḥpaṭalamādhūya pradyoditadigantaraṃ sambhāvya । jñānakhaḍgaṃ saptadhāstrajaptamādāyā(ya) । dravyaśodhanārthaṃ (l.5) pañcagavyaṃ sādhayet । pañcagavyārghyajalairastreṇa yāgagṛhaṃ dravyāṇi saṃprokṣya । maṇṭapasamskāraṃ mūlena (l.6) nirīkṣaṇam । astreṇa prokṣaṇam । kuśairhuṃphaḍantāstreṇa tāṭa(ḍa)nam । vauṣaḍantakavacenābhyukṣaṇam । astreṇa huṃphaḍantena gha(kha)nanāvakīraṇa-(l.7)pūraṇasamīkaraṇasecana-kuṭṭanasammārjanasamālepanāni vajrīkaraṇacatuṣpadāntacaturdaśasamskārān samskṛtya । pradakṣiṇa-krameṇa (p.63a, l.1) vighnanivāraṇārthaṃ hastagatadarbheṇollikhya tatsamāhṛtya vardhanyāsanāyai aiśānyāṃ saṃsthāpya । naiṛte vāstunāthaṃ saṃpūjya । tatpaścāt । paścime (l.2) varuṇasyottarakalaśe oṃ sāṃ mahālakṣmyāsanāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ mahālakṣmīmūrtaye namaḥ । 

dvārasya sammukhīṃ śyāmāṃ puṣparatnaughavarṣaṇīm । 
dhyātvā*vāhya yajedvidvān smaret maṇṭaparūpiṇīm ॥* (l.3)

	oṃ sāṃ mahālakṣmyai namaḥ । iti saṃpūjya । aiśānyāṃ tilādisaptadhānyasyopari padma(l.4)maṣṭadalaṃ samullikhya । uttare – guhakumbhaṃ dakṣiṇe vardhanīṃ ca nālottarām । paścimānane mūrtibhūtakumbhe mayūrāsanaṃ saṃpūjya । (l.5) oṃ sāṃ yāgeśvarāsanāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ yāgeśvaramūrtaye namaḥ । oṃ saṃ sāṃ sauṃ yāgeśvarāya guhāya namaḥ । anantadharma(l.6)jñāna-vairāgyaiśvaryapadmabhūtacalāsanaṃ saṃkalpya tasmin mūrtiṃ saṃpūjya ।

ekavaktraṃ dvinayanaṃ caturdordaṇḍamaṇḍitam ।
śakti*vaktraśaropetavaradābhayapāṇinam ॥* (l.7)
kundamandasmitaṃ śāntaṃ kuṇḍalābharaṇojvalam ।
dhyātvā tu śarasaṃbhūtaṃ mayūrārūḍhamavyayam ॥

	iti dhyātvā । (p.63b, f.88a, l.1) bhagavantaṃ śaktyādiāvaraṇasahitaṃ saṃpūjya । taddakṣiṇataḥ । tasyāṃ siṃharūpiṇīṃ ādhārarūpiṇīm astravardhanīāsanaṃ saṅkalpya । tasyopari (l.2) calāsanaṃ saṃkalpya । sampūjya । oṃ sāṃ guhāstrāya huṃphaṇṇamaḥ । ityāsanaṃ saṃpūjya । tadupari । oṃ sāṃ guhāstramūrtaye huṃ phaṇṇamaḥ । (l.3) iti mūrtidhyānam ।

anekajyotisaṅkāśamicchājñānakriyātmikam । 
sarvadevālayaṃ śaktiṃ guhāstraṃ kalaśarūpakam ॥

	iti dhyātvā । (l.4) oṃ sīṃ paṃ śuṃ huṃ phaṭ । guhāstrāya huṃ phaṇṇamaḥ । iti saṃpūjya । vastrobhayaṃ pratyekaṃ datvā pādyādinā pūjayet । kumbha(l.5)vardhanyau jalenābhiṣicya । kevalena āsanamūrtimūlenābhiṣicya । sāsanaṃ samūrtiṃ guhāstra-māvāhya । sakalīkṛtyā(tya) (l.6) saṃpūjya । maṇṭapasyābhyantaradigbhāge lokapālān vāhanāyudhasaṃyuktāṃ dhyātvā । prācyādidikṣu । indrādīn pūjayet । oṃ (l.7) sāṃ indrāsanāya namaḥ । indramūrtaye namaḥ ।

airāvatagajārūḍhaṃ svarṇavarṇaṃ kirīṭinam ।
sahasranayanaṃ śakraṃ vajrapāṇiṃ vibhāvayet ॥ (p.64a, l.1)

	iti dhyātvā । oṃ laṃ indrāya vajrahastāya airāvatagajārūḍhāya hemavarṇāya namaḥ । iṃ sāṃ indramāvāhayāmi । oṃ sāṃ indrāya namaḥ । iti saṃpūjya । naivedyadhūpa(l.2)dīpasarvopacāraiḥ saṃpūjya । japāntaṃ saṃpūjya । bho bho indra tvayā svasya diśi vighnapraśāntaye । sāvadhānena yāgāntaṃ yāva(vat) stheyaṃ guhājñayā । ityanena (l.3) guhājñāṃ śrāvayet । āgneye oṃ sāṃ agnyāsanāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ agnimūrtaye namaḥ । 

saptārciṣaṃ ca bibhrāṇāmakṣamālā kamaṇḍalum ।
jvālāmālākulaṃ raktaṃ *śaktihastaṃ chagāsanam ॥* (l.4)

	oṃ ram agnaye śaktihastāya meṣādhirūḍhāya raktavarṇāya namaḥ । ityagnimāvāhayāmi । oṃ sām agnaye namaḥ । āvāhana-(l.5)sthāpanā(na)japāntaṃ saṃpūjya । bho bho agne tvayā svasyāṃ diśi vighnapraśāntaye sāvadhānena yāgāntaṃ yāva(t) stheyaṃ guhājñayā । ityanena (l.6) guhājñāṃ śrāvayet । yāmye । oṃ sāṃ yamāsanāya namaḥ । yamamūrtaye namaḥ ।

kṛtāntaṃ mahiṣārūḍhaṃ daṇḍahastabhayānakam ।
kālapāśadharaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ dhyāyeddakṣiṇadikpatim ॥

	oṃ yaṃ yamāya daṇḍahastāya mahiṣārūḍhāya kṛṣṇavarṇāya namaḥ । iti yamamāvāhayāmi । oṃ sāṃ yamāya (l.7) namaḥ । sthāpanādijapāntaṃ saṃpūjya । bho bho yama tvayā svasyāṃ diśi vighnapraśāntā ye sāvadhānena yāgāntaṃ yāva(vat) stheyaṃ guhājñayā । (p.64b, f.89a, l.1) ityanena guhājñāṃ śrāvayet । naiṛte – oṃ saṃ naiṛtyāsanāyai namaḥ । naiṛtimūrtaye namaḥ । 

raktanetraṃ śavārūḍhaṃ nīlotpaladalaprabham ।
kṛpāṇapāṇi*māsraughaṃ pibantaṃ rākṣaseśvaram ॥* (l.2)

	oṃ hāṃ niṛṛtaye khaḍgahastāya pretārūḍhāya nīlavarṇāya namaḥ । iti niṛṛtimāvāhayāmi । oṃ sāṃ niṛṛtaye namaḥ । (l.3) sthāpanādijapāntaṃ sampūjya । bho bho niṛṛte tvayā svasyā(syāṃ) diśi vighnapraśāntaye sāvadhānena yāgāntaṃ yāva(vat) stheyaṃ guhājñayā ityanena (l.4) guhājñāṃ śrāvayet । varuṇe । oṃ sāṃ varuṇāsanāya namaḥ । varuṇamūrtaye namaḥ । 

nāgapāśadharaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ raktaukha(gha)dyutivigraham । (l.5)
śaśāṅkadhavalaṃ dhyāyedvāruṇaṃ makarāsanam ॥

	oṃ varuṇāya pāśahastāya makarārūḍhāya śvetavarṇāya namaḥ । iti varuṇamāvāhayāmi । oṃ (l.6) sāṃ varuṇāya namaḥ । sthāpanādijapāntaṃ saṃpūjya । bho bho varuṇa tvayā svasyāṃ diśi vighnapraśāntaye sāvadhānena yāgāntaṃ yāva(vat) stheyaṃ (l.7) guhājñayā । iti guhājñāṃ śrāvayet । vāyavye । oṃ sāṃ vāyuvāsanāya namaḥ । vāyumūrtaye namaḥ । 

āpīnahariṇacchāyāṃ vālolāladhvajadhāriṇīm । (p.65a, l.1)
prāṇabhūtaṃ ca bhūtānāṃ hariṇasthaṃ samīraṇam ॥

	oṃ ghaṃ vāyave namaḥ । sthāpanādijapāntaṃ saṃpūjya । bho bho vāyu tvayā svasyā(syāṃ) diśi vighnapraśāntaye (l.2) sāvadhānena yāgāntaṃ yāva(vat) stheyaṃ guhājñayā । iti guhājñāṃ śrāvayet । uttare । oṃ sāṃ kuberāsanāya namaḥ । puberamūrtaye namaḥ । 

kuberamanujāsīnaṃ *sakharvaṃ kharvavigraham ।* (l.3)
svarṇacchāyāṃ gadāhastaṃ uttarāśāpatiṃ smaret ॥

	oṃ kṣaṃ kuberāya gadāhastāya nṛpārūḍhāya svarṇacchāyāya namaḥ । iti kuberamā(l.4)vāhayāmi । oṃ sāṃ kuberāya namaḥ । sthāpanādijapāntaṃ saṃpūjya । bho bho kubera tvayā svasyāṃ diśi vighnapraśāntaye । sāvadhānena (l.5) yāgāntaṃ yāva(vat) stheyaṃ guhājñayā । guhājñāṃ śrāvayet । īśāne । oṃ sāṃ īśānāsanāya namaḥ । īśānamūrtaye namaḥ । (l.6)

īśānaṃ vṛṣabhārūḍhaṃ triśūlavyāladhāriṇam ।
śiraścandravadākāraṃ candramauliṃ trilocanam ॥

	oṃ hāṃ triśūlāya (l.7) īśānāya triśūlahastāya vṛṣabhārūḍhāya śvetavarṇāya namaḥ । iti īśānamāvāhayāmi । oṃ sām īśānāya namaḥ । sthāpanādi(l.8)japāntaṃ saṃpūjyaca । bho bho īśa tvayā svasya diśi vighnapraśāntaye sāvadhānena yāgāntaṃ yāva(t) stheyaṃ guhājñayā । iti guhājñāṃ śrāvayet । (p.65b, f.90a, l.1) īśānadakṣiṇe । oṃ sāṃ brahmāsanāya namaḥ । brahmamūrtaye namaḥ । 

pītaṃ pīnaṃ caturbāhuṃ brahmāṇaṃ caturānanam ।
haṃsayānaṃ ca bibhrāṇaṃ daṇḍākṣa*sṛkkamaṇḍalum ॥* (l.2)

	oṃ kaṃ brahmaṇe sṛghastāya haṃsārūḍhāya pītavarṇāya namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ brahmāṇamāvāhayāmi । oṃ sāṃ brahmaṇe namaḥ । (l.3) sthāpanādijapāntaṃ saṃpūjya । bho bho brahman tvayā svasyāṃ diśi vighnapraśāntaye sāvadhānena yāgānta(ntaṃ) yāva(t) stheyaṃ guhājñayā (l.4) guhājñāṃ śrāvayet । naiṛtottare । oṃ sāṃ viṣṇuvāsanāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ viṣṇumūrtaye namaḥ । 

anantaṃ puṇḍarīkākṣaṃ *phaṇādaśaśatairyutam ।* (l.5)
vidyuddāmapratīkāśaṃ kūrmārūḍhaṃ kṛtāñjalim ॥

oṃ bhaṃ(viṃ) vuṣṇuve cakrahastāya garuḍārūḍhāya (l.6) kṛṣṇavarṇāya namaḥ । viṣṇumāvāhayāmi । viṣṇave namaḥ । ityabhyarcya । sthāpanādijapāntaṃ saṃpūjya । bho bho viṣṇu tvayā (l.7) svasyāṃ diśi vighnapraśāntaye । sāvadhānena yāgāntaṃ yāva(vat) stheyaṃ guhājñayā । iti guhājñāṃ śrāvayet । sarvān lokapālān (p.66a, l.1) vāhanāyudhasaṃyuktān guhājñāṃ śrāvayet । 

vajrādidaśāyudhadhyānam – 
vajraṃ tu puruṣaṃ sthūlaṃ dṛḍhakarkaśavigraham ।
balinaṃ vajramūrdhānaṃ dīptaṃ *prākpañcamāvṛtau ॥* (l.2)
	oṃ sāṃ vajrāya huṃ phaṇṇamaḥ । āgneye । 
śaktiṃ ca yoṣitākāram āgneyyāṃ diśi locanam । 
bibhrāṇaṃ śirasā śaktiṃ tra(tri)koṇastha*mupāśraye ॥* (l.3)
	oṃ sāṃ śaktaye  huṃ phaṇṇamaḥ । dakṣiṇe । 
daṇḍaṃ ca dakṣiṇe bhāge kṛṣṇaṃ puruṣavigraham ।
daṇḍa(ṇḍā)kāraśiroyuktaṃ vande *lohitalocanam ॥* (l.4)
	oṃ sāṃ daṇḍāya namaḥ । naiṛte । 
naiṛtyāṃ puruṣākāraṃ khaḍgaśyāmakalebaram ।
khaḍgālaṅkṛta*mūrdhānaṃ kruddhaṃ krūradṛśaṃ namaḥ ॥* (l.5)
	oṃ sāṃ khaḍgāya huṃ phaṇṇamaḥ । vāruṇe । 
nābhyadho bhujagākāra(raṃ) tadūrdhvaṃ puruṣākṛtim । (l.6)
pāśaḥ saptaphaṇopetaṃ mūrdhānaṃ paścimā bhavet ॥
	oṃ sāṃ pāśāya huṃ phaṇṇamaḥ । vāyau । 
pītāṅgaṃ *puruṣākāraṃ vyāvṛtāsyaṃ mahābhujam ।* (l.7)
prapadye vāyudigbhāge dhvajamūrdhni dhvajānvitam ॥ (p.66b, f.91a, l.1)
	oṃ sām aṅkuśāya namaḥ । uttare । 
kanyārūpagadā(tā) pītamāpīnajaghanasthalīm ।
gadākāraśiroyuktām uttarasyāṃ tatomyaham ॥
	oṃ sāṃ (l.2) gadāya huṃ phaṇṇamaḥ । naiṛtottare – īśānadakṣiṇe । 
śaṅkhābhaṃ padmakośāṅgaṃ mūrdhānaṃ puruṣākṛtim ।
śyāmadehamukundāstraṃ seve niṛtikoṇakam ॥
	oṃ sāṃ (l.3) padmāya huṃ phaṇṇamaḥ । naiṛtottare । 
śakraṃ śatāracakrāṅgamūrdhānaṃ puruṣākṛtim ।
śyāmadehaṃ mukundāstraṃ seve niṛtikoṇakam ॥
	oṃ sāṃ (l.4) candrāya huṃ phaṇṇamaḥ । 
sarvāṇyañjalihastāni padmasaṃsthāni pūrvavat ।
vajrādyaṅkitaśīrṣāṇi bhāvayetpaścimāvṛtau ॥
vajrādi*daśāyudhān guhājñāṃ śrāvayet ।* (l.5)

	tadanu । guhavardhanīmādāya kumbhāgragāminīṃ gṛhītvā । maṇṭapaṃ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya tajjalādhārānugaṃ guhakumbhaṃ (l.6) namaḥ sarvātmakayetyuktvā । mūlamantreṇāstradarbhamanusmaran īśānādīśāntaṃ bhrāmaitvā puna(naḥ) svasthāne । pūrvaṃ kumbhā(mbha)samīpaṃ paścāt (l.7) vardhanīṃ ca saṃsthāpya । guhakumbhe śaktyādiśaktiparyantaṃ sthirāsanaṃ saṃpūjya । mūrti-vidyādehaṃ netraṃ vinyasyādhvanyāsaṃ pūjya । vardhanyāmastramarcayet (p.67a, l.1) oṃ sām astrāsanāya huṃ bha(pha)ṇṇamaḥ । oṃ sām astramūrtaye huṃ phaṇṇamaḥ ।

anekajyotisaṅkāśaṃ icchājñānakriyātmikam ।
sarvadevālayaṃ śaktiṃ guhāstraṃ *kalaśarūpakam ॥* (l.2)

	iti dhyātvā । oṃ hiṃ paṃ śuṃ huṃ phaṭ guhāstrāya huṃ phaṇṇamaḥ । ityāvāhya । om astrahṛdayāya huṃ phaṇṇamaḥ । oṃ ślīm astraśirase huṃ phaṇṇamaḥ । oṃ pām (l.3) astraśikhāya huṃ phaṇṇamaḥ । oṃ śum astrakavacāya huṃ phaṇṇamaḥ । oṃ hum astranetrāya huṃ phaṇṇamaḥ । oṃ huṃ phaṭ astrāya huṃ phaṇṇamaḥ । ityaṅgaiḥ (l.4) sakalīkṛtya । tau gandhādibhiḥ sāvaraṇaiḥ sarvopacāraiḥ sampūjya । oṃ sāṃ sauṃ guhaśaktibhyāṃ namaḥ । iti liṅgamudrayāṅguṣṭhena (l.5) saṃspṛśya । 

umāyai bhagarūpiṇyai liṅgarūpadharāya ca ।
śaṅkarāya namastubhyaṃ iti stutvānumodya ca ॥
yajñasyāsya patistvaṃ hi *mūrtireṣā tavācalā(la) ।* (l.6)
eṣa te jñānanistṛṃśat gṛhāṇa tvamumāyudham ॥

	iti vijñāpya । yāgarakṣārthaṃ devasya haste huṃ phaṭantāstreṇa (l.7) iti jñānakhaḍgaṃ samarpya । mūlenāṣṭottaraśataṃ japtvā । nivedya । taddaśāṃśena vardhanyāmastraṃ *japtvā nivedya ।* (p.67b, f.92a, l.1)

mayā pravṛttitacchāyāṃ yajvanā bhavatuḥ kratuḥ ।
rakṣaṇīyastvayaitaddhi samāptiryāvatasya hi ॥
	iti rakṣārthaṃ vijñāpya । nirodhārghyaṃ vidhāya । (l.2) tadambhasā nirodhāyāghoreṇārghyaṃ datvā । tatpātraṃ tatraiva niścalaṃ sthāpya । vardhanyāsanavikiroddhāstrairyāgagṛhamapasāritā (l.3) śeṣavighnasamantāt bhāvayet । vāyavyāṃ tatpūrvaṃ tripadamadhya-kalaśe । kṣetrapālāsanāya namaḥ । kṣetrapālamūrtaye namaḥ । (l.4) 

raktajvālajaṭādharaṃ śaśidharaṃ raktāṅgatojomayam ।
haste śūlakapālapāśaḍamaruṃ lokasya rakṣākaram ॥
nirvāṇaṃ śvanavāhanaṃ *tra(tri)ṇa(na)yanam ānandakolāhalam ।* (l.5)
vande bhūtapiśācanāthavaṭukaṃ kṣetrasya pālaṃ śivam ॥

	oṃ sāṃ kṣetrapālāya namaḥ । (l.6) iti saṃpūjya । sthāpanādijapāntaṃ saṃpūjya । aiśānyāṃ paścimatripadamadhya-kalaśe । guruṃ dṛṣṭvādhyeṣya praṇamya vijñāpya । (l.7) sadāśivādisaptagurubhyo namaḥ ।

sadāśivamanantaṃ ca śrīkaṇṭhaṃ punarambikām ।
guhaṃ viṣṇuṃ ca brahmāṇaṃ gurūn sapta smaran nyaset ॥ (p.68a, vedikopari kalaśapūjai(jā), l.1)

	तेषामाज्ञां लब्ध्वा । नैवेद्यादिजपान्तं संपूज्य । वायव्ये ओं सां विनायकासनाय नमः । विनायकमूर्तये नमः । 
स्कन्दाग्रजं महाविघ्नं नायकं गुह पूजयेत् ।
अविघ्नेन*मवाप्त्यर्थमाश्रयेत्गजवक्त्रकम् ॥* <<(l.2)

	iti saṃpūjya । sthāpanādyupacāraiḥ saṃpūjya । prārthya । vedyordhvakumbhe pūjāṃ kuryāt । kumbheśvaravedikāyāṃ dakṣiṇe (l.3) sthitvā । 

śāntyatītakalāmūrdhni śāntivaktrasaroruham ।
vidyāvihitavakṣoṅgaṃ pratiṣṭhāguhyamaṇḍalam ॥
nivṛttijānujaṅghāṅghriṃ bhavanādhvā tanūruham ।
varna(rṇa)tvakmantrarudhiraṃ *padmāṃsasivirārjitam? ॥* (l.4)
tattvāsthimajjāśuklādidhyānaṃ sadbhogamokṣadam ।
guhāntatattvagarbhādhvā vyāpinaṃ sarvadā śivam ॥

	guham । iti (l.5) dhyātvāvāhanaiḥ saṃpūjya । kumbhasyādhobhāgamadhyataḥ । 

adhaḥkūrmaśilāsīnāṃ kṣīrodasitavigrahām ।
maulībījāṅkurākāraṃ *varadābhayapradam ॥* (l.6)
pāśāṅkuśadharāṃ śaktiṃ kriyāmādhārarūpiṇīm ।

	iti dhyātvā । oṃ sām ādhāraśaktaye namaḥ । adhaḥkūrmaśilāsanāya (l.7) namaḥ । iti saṃpūjya ।

brahmaśilopari pṛthvī kandaṃ kalāntaikaṃ nālakaṃ bhāvakandakam ।
kṣubdhamāyāmahāpadmamanekadalasaṅkulam । 

	oṃ (l.8) sām anantāsanāya namaḥ । iti saṃpūjya । 

tanmadhyagamanaṃ teśaṃ vibhuṃ vyāpyasthitaṃ tvadhaḥ । 

dhyātvā । brahmaśilāmadhye yajeśaśu(cchu)bhraṃ guhāsane । oṃ (p.68b, f.93a, l.1) sām anantāsanāya namaḥ । iti saṃpūjya । tatsāmarthyarūpān vicitrakesariprakhyānanyonyaṃ pṛṣṭhadarśanaḥ । kṛtatretādirūpeṇa guhasyāsanapādukān sitakāśmīra-tālāli(l.2)cārusiṃhākṛtiṃ smaran । 

āgneyādīśaparyantaṃ koṇeṣu ca yathākramam ।

oṃ sāṃ dharmāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ jñānāya namaḥ । vairāgyāya namaḥ । aiśvaryāya namaḥ । (l.3) ityabhyarcya । 

kṛṣṇaśvetaṃ pūrvabhāge śvetaraktaṃ tu dakṣiṇe ।
raktapītaṃ paścimataḥ pītakṛṣṇamudagdiśi ॥
catvāri gātrakāṇyevāṃ(va) ananteśabalāni tu । (l.4)
yadvā ।
gātrakān sphaṭikaprakhyā tryakṣān siṃhaśiroddhṛtān ।
stabdhabāhuśiragrīvān pādukāsaktamastakān ॥
agragātrakapādasthā *mastakānnararūpiṇaḥ ।* (l.5)

	dhyāyan buddhiguṇādharmamukhādhiṣṭhātṛkān nyaset । oṃ sām adharmāya namaḥ । oṃ sām ajñānāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ (l.6) vairāgyāya namaḥ । anaiśvaryāya namaḥ । ityabhyarcya । pīṭhasyordhvamekhalāyām । adho raktavarṇamadhacchadanaṃ kṣubdhamāyāntaṃ vibhāvya । naiṛte (l.7) oṃ sām adhacchadanāya namaḥ । ūrdhvacchadanam । tadūrdhve pāṇḍuvarṇaṃ śuddhavidyādhobhāgamīśāne । oṃ sām ūrdhvacchadanāya namaḥ । iti saṃpūjya । tanmadhyordhvadiśi । (l.8) 

māṇikkasadṛśaṃ kandaṃ nālaṃ nīlasamaprabham ।
aṅkuraṃ tu pravālābhaṃ dalaṃ rajatasa(sama)prabham ॥
kesaraṃ hemavarṇābham agre muktāvalīyutam ।

	oṃ sāṃ kandāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ (p.69a, l.1) bījāya namaḥ । aṅkurāya namaḥ । nālāya namaḥ । patre dalebhyo namaḥ । iti vidyeśvarāṣṭakarūpāṇi dalāni saṃcintyābhyarcya । pūrvādidikṣu । aṣṭakalaśeṣu oṃ sāṃ (l.2) jayantāsanāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ jayamūrtaye namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ jayāya namaḥ । dhyānādijapāntaṃ saṃpūjya । oṃ sām agniśikhāsanāya namaḥ । oṃ sām agniśikhāmūrtaye namaḥ । oṃ sām agniśikhāya (l.3) namaḥ । dhyānapurassaraṃ saṃpūjya । oṃ sāṃ kṛttikā-putrāsanāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ kṛttikāputramūrtaye namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ kṛttikāputrāya namaḥ । dhyānādijapāntaṃ saṃpūjya । (l.4) oṃ sāṃ bhūtapatāsanāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ bhūtapatimūrtaye namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ bhūtapataye namaḥ । dhyānādijapāntaṃ saṃpūjya । oṃ sāṃ (l.5) senānyāsanāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ senānimūrtaye namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ senānyai namaḥ । dhyānādijapāntaṃ saṃpūjya । oṃ sāṃ guhāsanāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ (l.6) guhamūrtaye namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ guhāya namaḥ । dhyānādijapāntaṃ saṃpūjya । oṃ sāṃ hemaśūlāsanāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ hema(l.7)śūlamūrtaye namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ hemaśūlāya namaḥ । iti dhyānādijapāntaṃ saṃpūjya । oṃ sāṃ viśālākṣāsanāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ viśālākṣa(l.8)mūrtaye namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ viśālākṣāya namaḥ । iti dhyānādijapāntaṃ saṃpūjya ।

vidyeśvarādayaḥ sarve devasyābhimukhasthitaḥ । 
jayantādidalāgreṣu *pīṭhakaṇṭhe gaṇeśvaraḥ ॥* (p.69b, f.94a, l.1)
pīṭhapāde tu lokeśastadastrāṇi śilopari ।
pūjānyāvaraṇānyevaṃ sva sva vyāptivibhedataḥ ॥

	iti guhājñāṃ śrāvayet । tanmūlapīṭhopari (l.2) oṃ sāṃ kesarebhyo namaḥ । pīṭhonnatabhāgamadhye ।

taptacāmīkaracchāyāṃ pañcāśadbījagarbhitām ।
kesarāmāṃ catuṣṣaṣṭhyā karṇikāṃ pūjayettataḥ ॥

	oṃ (l.3) sāṃ karṇikāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ bījebhyo namaḥ । ityevaṃ samastarūpaṃ padmaṃ vibhāvya । oṃ sāṃ padmāsanāya namaḥ । iti saṃpūjayet । yadvā । (l.4) samuditarūpaṃ padmakarṇikāṃ ca yajet । tataḥ pūrvādīśāntakesareṣu vidyeśvarādhiṣṭhā-triśaṅkunyodyaṣṭau karṇikāyāṃ guhaśakti(l.5)mevaṃ vāmapārśve । oṃ sāṃ devasenāsanāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ devasenāmūrtaye namaḥ । oṃ devasenāya namaḥ । (l.6)

pītāmutpaladhāriṇīṃ śacisutāṃ divyālambarālaṅkṛtām
   vāme lambakarāṃ mahendratanayāṃ mandāramālyādharām ।
devairarcitapādukā *śaśimukhāṃ deveśa va(vā)me sthitām* (l.7)
   senā divyavibhūṣaṇāṃ dvinayanāṃ devīṃ tribhaṅgīṃ bhaje ॥

	iti dhyātvā । oṃ sāṃ devasenāyai namaḥ । (p.70a, l.1) ityāvāhanādyupacāraiḥ saṃpūjya । dakṣiṇapārśve । oṃ sāṃ vallīśvaryāsanāya namaḥ । vallīśvarīmūrtaye namaḥ । 

śyāmām utpaladhāriṇīṃ maṇilasattāṭaṅkakarṇojvalām । (l.2)
savye lambakarāṃ kirīṭamakuṭāṃ tuṅgastanīṃ kañcukām ॥
anyonvīkṣaṇasaṃyutāṃ śaravaṇodbhūtasya savye sthitām ।
kuñjamālyadharāṃ pravālavadanāṃ vallīśvarīṃ bhāvayet ॥ (l.3)

	iti dhyātvā । oṃ sāṃ vallīśvaryai namaḥ । iti saṃpūjya । āvāhanādyupacāraiḥ saṃpūjya । dalāgre । oṃ sāṃ śaṅkunyai namaḥ । revatyai namaḥ । pūtanāya (l.4) namaḥ । mahāpūtanāya namaḥ । niśidhanyai namaḥ । mālinyai namaḥ । śītalāyā namaḥ । buddhyai namaḥ । viśvatomukhyai namaḥ । iti saṃpūjyārghyaṃ datvā । dalāgravṛtte । (l.5) oṃ sāṃ sūryamaṇḍalāya namaḥ । iti raktaṃ bhāskararūpaṃ tadadhipatim ।

brahmā caturmukho pītaścaturdordaṇḍamaṇḍitaḥ । 
sṛgdaṇḍākṣakuśopetaṃ *sūryamaṇḍalamadhyagam ॥* (l.6)

	iti dhyātvā । oṃ sāṃ sūryamaṇḍalādhipataye brahmaṇe namaḥ । ityabhyarcya । kesarāgravṛtte । oṃ sāṃ (l.7) soma-maṇḍalāya namaḥ । iti śvetarūpamaṃ maṇḍalādhiṣṭhitam ।

viṣṇuścuturbhujo mālī śaṅkhacakragadābjadhṛk ।
nīlāmbudapratīkāśaḥ somamaṇḍalamadhyagaḥ ॥ (l.8)

	iti dhyātvā । oṃ sāṃ somamaṇḍalādhipataye viṣṇave namaḥ । ityabhyarcya । karṇigāgravṛtte । oṃ sām agni-maṇḍalāya namaḥ । iti raktamaṇḍalaṃ tadadhipatim । (p.70b, f.95a, l.1)

agnimadhye sthitaṃ rudraṃ nīlakaṇṭhaṃ trilocanam ।
vyākhyāmudrānalajñānaṃ śūlahastaṃ caturbhujam ॥
sarvālaṅkārasaṃyuktaṃ nāgayajñopavītinam ।

	iti dhyātvā । oṃ sām agnimaṇḍalādhipataye (l.2) rudrāya namaḥ । ityabhyarcya । madhye oṃ sāṃ subrahmaṇyāsanāya namaḥ । iti pīṭhe saṃpūjya । oṃ sāṃ subrahmaṇyamūrtaye namaḥ । iti mūrtiṃ tejorūpām avabhaktāvayavāṃ (l.3) guhatattvātmakaparabinduvyāptikāṃ vibhāvya । kumbhaveṣṭane niveśayet । asyāṃ tejomayamūrtau mūrdhāmukhahṛtguhyapādādisarvāṅgeṣu (l.4) oṃ som īśānamūrdhāya namaḥ । oṃ seṃ tatpuruṣavaktrāya namaḥ । oṃ sūm aghorahṛdayāya namaḥ । oṃ sīṃ vāmadeva-guhyāya namaḥ । oṃ saḥ sadyojāta(l.5)mūrtaye namaḥ । evaṃ vaktrabhaṅgyā vinyasyā(sya) । tataḥ । mātrabhaṅgyā ca । am amareśāya namaḥ । mūrdhni । ārādhyāya namaḥ । lalāṭe । im indusutāpriyāya namaḥ । (l.6) dakṣiṇanetre । ī īśasutāya namaḥ । vāmanetre । um umāputrāya namaḥ । dakṣiṇakarṇe । ūm ūrdhvarete namaḥ । vāmakarṇe । ṛṃ rudrabhuve namaḥ । (l.7) dakṣiṇanāsāyām । ṝṃ kumārāya namaḥ । vāmanāsikāyām । ḷṃ lāvaṇyāya namaḥ । dakṣiṇagaṇḍe । ḹṃ kālakaṇṭhasutāya namaḥ । vāmagaṇḍe । eṃ vallipriyāya namaḥ । ūrdhvoṣṭre । aiṃ aiśvaryapradāya namaḥ । adharoṣṭre । om ojasvine namaḥ । ūrdhvadantapaṅktau । aum aupamyarahitāya namaḥ । adhodantapaṅktau । am ambujāsanāya (p.71a, l.1) namaḥ । tālumadhye । aḥ akṣiṇi tejasāya namaḥ । bhrūmadhye । dakṣiṇahasteṣu kakārādiṅakārāntaṃ kaṃ kamaṇḍaludharāya namaḥ । khaṃ khaḍgapāṇaye namaḥ । gaṃ gaṅgāsutāya (l.2) namaḥ । ghaṃ ghaṇṭahastāya namaḥ । ṅaṃ ṅapriyaṃśabdajananāya namaḥ । iti vinyasya । cakārādiñakārāntaṃ vāmahasteṣu nyaset । caṃ candraśekharāya namaḥ । chaṃ chandomayāya namaḥ । jaṃ (l.3) jagadbhuve namaḥ । jhaṃ jhaṇḍīśāya namaḥ । ñaṃ ñānadāya namaḥ । iti vinyasya । ṭakārādiṇakāraparyantaṃ dakṣiṇapādeṣu nyaset । (l.4) ṭaṃ ṭaṅkahastāya namaḥ । ṭhaṃ ṭhasvarūpāya namaḥ । ḍaṃ ḍambhāya namaḥ । ḍhaṃ ḍhakkapriyāya namaḥ । ṇaṃ ṇagamyāya namaḥ । iti nyaset । (l.5) takārādinakāraparyantaṃ vāmapādeṣu nyaset । taṃ tattvarūpāya namaḥ । thaṃ sthaviṣṭhāya namaḥ । daṃ daṇḍapāṇaye namaḥ । dhanuḥ (l.6) pāṇaye namaḥ । naṃ nagarandhrāya namaḥ । iti nyaset । dakṣiṇapārśve । paṃ pāśahastāya namaḥ । phaṃ phaṇibhukvāhanāya namaḥ । (l.7) vāmapārśve । baṃ bahulāputrāya namaḥ । bhaṃ bhavātmajāya namaḥ । dakṣiṇapṛṣṭhe । maṃ mahāsenāya namaḥ । vāmapṛṣṭhe । yaṃ yajñamūrtaye namaḥ । kaṭipradeśe । (p.70b, f.99a, l.1) raṃ ramaṇīśāya namaḥ । laṃ lambodarānujāya namaḥ । kaṭisūtre । vaṃ vacadbhuve namaḥ । kuṇḍalopari । śaṃ śaravaṇabhavāya namaḥ । ṣaṃ ṣaḍānanāya namaḥ । hṛdaye । (l.2) saṃ sāramocanāya namaḥ । makuṭe । haṃ harātmajāya namaḥ । meḍhradeśe । ḻaṃ ḻakulīśāya namaḥ । nābhideśe । kṣaṃ kṣmāmūrtaye namaḥ । iti (l.3) mātṛkā-nyāsam । tataḥ kalābhaṅgyā । oṃ sāṃ jagadbhuve jayāyai namaḥ । īśānamūrdhni । oṃ sāṃ bahuto balāyai namaḥ । pūrvamūrdhni । (l.4) oṃ sām utamunnadāyai namaḥ । dakṣiṇamūrdhni । oṃ sāṃ vacadbhuvaṃ vāmāyai namaḥ uttaramūrdhni । oṃ sāṃ namaste'stu nandinyai namaḥ । paścima(l.5)mūrdhni । oṃ sāṃ viśvabhuve jayāyai namaḥ । naiṛtimūrdhni । oṃ sāṃ jagadbhuvo'dhipataye jayāyai namaḥ । īśānavaktre । oṃ sāṃ (l.6) senāni senāyai namaḥ । pūrvavaktre । oṃ sāṃ mayūrapriyāyai māyāyai namaḥ । dakṣiṇavaktre । oṃ sāṃ ṣaḍānanāṅgāya śaktyai namaḥ । (l.7) uttaravaktre । oṃ sāṃ satvayāhaṃ namaste'stu satyāyai namaḥ । paścimavaktre । oṃ sāṃ sukhaṃ vahaḥ sūkṣmāyai namaḥ । naiṛtavaktre । iti saṃpūjya । oṃ sāṃ (p.72a, l.1) subrahmaṇyāya sumatyai namaḥ । hṛdaye । oṃ sāṃ bṛhaspatisutaprajñāya namaḥ । gaḻe । oṃ sām āsyapadmayonaye amṛtāya namaḥ । dakṣiṇabāhu । oṃ sāṃ yasyātmāya (l.2) yaśase namaḥ । vāmabāhu । oṃ sām a(va)hevahantu variṣṭhāyai namaḥ । nābhau । oṃ sāṃ subrahmaṇyasupratiṣṭhāyai namaḥ । kukṣau । oṃ sāṃ rudrabhuve rucāyai namaḥ । (l.3) pṛṣṭhe । oṃ brahmabhuve brāhmiṇyai namaḥ । urasi । oṃ sāṃ bhuvadbhuve bhṛtyai namaḥ । guhye । oṃ sām amṛtāya mānyai namaḥ । śebhe(phe) । oṃ sāṃ (l.4) jagadbhuve jīvitāyai namaḥ । dakṣiṇaūrau । oṃ sāṃ subrahmaṇyaśuddhāyai namaḥ । vāmorau । oṃ sāṃ kṛttikāsutakṛpāyai namaḥ । (l.5) dakṣiṇajānuni । oṃ sāṃ ṣaṣṭhāṅgāya satvarāya namaḥ । vāmajānuni । oṃ sāṃ jagadbhuve jagatyai namaḥ । dakṣiṇajaṅghāyām । oṃ sāṃ (l.6) vacadbhuve varadāyai namaḥ । vāma-jaṅghāyām । oṃ sāṃ skandaviśākhātmane kāmadāyai namaḥ । dakṣiṇapādai । oṃ sāṃ havyaṃ yasmāt (l.7) kāriṇyai namaḥ । vāma-pāde । oṃ sāṃ ṣaṇmukhāya śyāmāyai namaḥ । vyāpakāñjaliḥ । evaṃ sva sva mudrāṣṭatṛṃśatkalānyāsam । 

kṣityādikuṭilā*prāntaṃ mātra(tṛ)siṃhāsanasthitam ।* (p.72b, f.97a, l.1)
śuddhasphaṭikasaṃkāśaṃ dvātriṃśallakṣaṇānvitam ॥
padmāsanasthaṃ ṣaḍvāsyaṃ prativaktraṃ trilocanam ।
dṛkkṛyecchāviśālākṣaṃ *jñānacandrakalānvitam ॥* (l.2)

	iti kumbhe mūrtiṃ dhyātvā । oṃ sāṃ subrahmaṇyāsanāya namaḥ । subrahmaṇyamūrtaye namaḥ । 

abhayakaṭuvihastaṃ jñānaśakti*praśastam ।* (l.3)
samatanusamapādaṃ vidrumābhaṃ dvinetram ॥
smitayutavadanenduṃ kuṇḍaloddīptakarṇam ।
kanakamakuṭayuktaṃ skandarūpaṃ namāmi ॥ (l.4)

	iti dhyātvā । oṃ subrahmaṇyāya namaḥ ।

sañcintya mūrtirupariśaktimātravijṛmbhaṇam ।
guhākāropamardena bhāvena guhatāṃ *nyajet ॥* (l.5)

	iti mūrtiṃ dhyātvā । oṃ sāṃ sauṃ vidyādehāya namaḥ । brahmarandhrāntavyāptikaṃ prāsādamuccārya guṇatattvānta(l.6)-vyāpakaṃ saudāminīnibhaṃ sṛgañjalā vāvirbhūtaṃ dhyātvā । guhārohe vidyādehaṃ samuditirūpaṃ vinyasya । oṃ sāṃ sauṃ netrebhyo namaḥ । (p.73a, l.1) iti netraṃ sarvajñatābījamukhanetreṣu vinyasya । puṣpairañjalimāpūrya । mūlamantraṃ medhādikalopetaṃ pracaṇḍa-mārtāṇḍopamaṃ brahmādikāraṇatyāga(l.2)krameṇa layāntamuccārya । 

sarvakartāraṃ niṣkalaṃ vibhuṃ jñānānandamayaṃ svarūpaprakāśam ।

	sañcintya । tanmayībhūtaṃ sṛṣṭikrameṇa dvādaśāntāt pataṅgapiṅgābhaṃ (l.3) lalāṭe'vatārya । candrakoṭisūryakoṭi-sadṛśaṃ tiryak (ka?) sudhāpiṇḍapāṇḍuritadiṅmaṇḍalaṃ dhyātvā । prāṇapravāhavartmanā (l.4) vina(ni)rgatyāñjaligataṃ vicintyālakṣya mūrtāvāvāhinyā hṛdayena sadyojātenāvāhya sthāpinyā vāmadevena saṃsthāpya । sannidhāyā(l.5)ghoreṇa sannidhāpya । niṣṭhirayā tatpuruṣeṇa nirodhyā(dhya) tarjanībhyāmīśānenāvakuṇṭhya । nirodhārghyaṃ datvā । svāgataṃ (l.6) te mahābhāgeti vijñāpya । vatsa susvāgatamiti devenoktaṃ vibhāvya ।

svāmin sarvajagannātha yāvatpūjāvasānakam ।
tāvat *saṃ(tvaṃ)prītibhāvena kumbhe'smin sannidhiṃ kuru ॥* (p.73b, f.98a, l.1)

	iti vijñāpya । svāgatārghyaṃ datvā । hṛdayādikarāntasthāneṣu hṛdayādyaṅgāni vinyasya । mūlenaikatvaṃ vibhāvya । (l.2) astreṇa saṃrakṣya । kavacenāvakuṇṭhya । amṛtamudrāṃ datvā । vauṣaḍantamūlena mahāmudrayā paramīkṛtya ṣaṇmukhamudrāmābandhya । sahṛdā namontena (l.3) pādayoḥ pādyam । svadhāntena īśānādimukhapañcakeṣu ācamanaṃ svāhāntena । evaṃ śiraṣva(śya)rghyaṃ datvā । mūlena dūrvāpuṣpākṣatādīni (l.4) dadyāt । sahasradā svāhāntamūlena dhūpadīpau datvā ca vāmahaste ghaṇṭāṃ ca tāḍayan pūrvavadācamanārghyau datvā vighnārthaṃ (l.5) vijñāpya । viśeṣārghyajalena vyomavyāpini devamabhiṣicya । pādyācamanārghyau ca datvā sūkṣmavāsasā nimṛjya । (l.6) candanāgarukarpūrakuṅkumamiśra(śri)tagandhākṣatadūrvāhemakaupīna-mekhalopavītakuṇḍalābharaṇāni vicitravastrāṇi manasā bhaktyā nivedya । (l.7) sapuṣpāñjalitrayeṇa oṃ sāṃ saum ātmatattvādhipataye guhāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ sauṃ guhatattvādhipataye guhāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ sauṃ (p.74a, l.1) śivatattvā-dhipataye guhāya namaḥ । iti mūlenābhyarcya । iṇḍādipuṣpadāmādibhiralaṅkṛtya । astreṇa ghaṇṭāṃ tāḍayan dhūpadīpācamanādi datvā manoratha(l.2)mudrāṃ darśayitvā । tato brahmāṅgāni layāṅgasthāneṣu sampūjya । kāmāni vijñāpya । āvaraṇapūjārtham adhyeṣye labdhānujño guhamūlamantreṇābhyarcya । (l.3) tasya śiro mūrdhādipādaparyantam । oṃ sām īśānajagadbhuve namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ tatpuruṣavacatbhuve namaḥ । oṃ sūm aghoraviśvabhuve namaḥ । (l.4) oṃ sīṃ vāmadevaṛgbhuve namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ sadyojāta-brahmabhuve namaḥ । oṃ soṃ seṃ sūṃ sīṃ sarvatrabhuve namaḥ । iti daṇḍabhaṅgyā (l.5) vinyasya । īśānādiṣaṇmukheṣu īśānādi-ṣaṇmukhamantrānarcayet । oṃ sāṃ jagadbhuvaṃ jayāyai namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ bahuto (l.6) balāyai namaḥ । oṃ sām unnatamunnadāyai namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ vacadbhuvaṃ vāmāyai namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ namaste'stu nandinyai namaḥ । oṃ (l.7) sāṃ viśvabhuve vijayāya namaḥ । iti saṃpūjya । oṃ sāṃ hṛdayāya namaḥ । oṃ sīṃ śirase svāhā । oṃ sūṃ śikhāyai vauṣaṭ । oṃ saiṃ kavacāya (p.74b, f.99a, l.1) huṃ phaṭ । oṃ sauṃ netrebhyo vauṣaṭ । oṃ saḥ astrāya phaṭ । iti muṇḍabhaṅgyā ta(tat)sthāneṣu vinyasya layāṅgaṃ pūjya । 

(bhogāṅgapūjā -)
bhogāṅgaṃ pūjayāmīti prārthya (l.2) īśānādimantrān tattatsthāneṣu ādāya īśānādipañcadaleṣvabhyarcya oṃ sauṃ netrebhyo vauṣaṭ । iti īśānasyottarabhāge (l.3) oṃ sāṃ hṛdayāya namaḥ । ityāgneyadale oṃ sāṃ śirase svāheti īśānadale netrasyottarabhāge oṃ sūṃ śikhāyai (l.4) vauṣaṭ । iti naiṛtadale oṃ saiṃ kavacāya huṃ phaḍiti vāyavyadale oṃ saḥ astrāya phaṭ । pūrvādicaturdaleṣu । (l.5) tatpuruṣādibhyo vāmabhāge । iti yajet । etān guṇavadāvānasthāpanasannidhāna-sannirodhana-pādyācamanārghya-(l.6)puṣpadānāntairaṣṭabhiḥ samskāraiḥ samskṛtya । pūjayet । samskārāṣṭakaṃ samuditameva sarvaṃ samskṛtya(tyā)rghyaṃ datvā īśānādīnāṃ brahmaṇā dhenupadmatriśūla(l.7)makarasṛgākhyamudrāḥ aṅgānāṃ ca samskāramudrāṃ darśayitvā – paścānmūlena guhāya puṣpairañjalitrayaṃ datvā mahāmudrāṃ darśayitvā । sarveṣāṃ (p.75a, l.1) guhenasārdhamaikyaṃ vibhāvya । tato dhūpadīpācamanārghyān ca datvā । naivedyācamaṃ vividhaṃ pānīyaṃ catuḥsamskārasaṃśuddhaṃ svāhāntamūlena datvā । brahmāṅgānāṃ (l.2) sva sva mantrairdatvā । abhāve ekasminpātre nivedyācamanaṃ ca kṛtvā । hastodvartanatāmbūlamukhavāsamarghyaṃ ca datvā । punarabhyarcya dhūpadīpācamanārghyāṇyupāhṛtya । (l.3) dūrvākṣatasahitaṃ puṣpāñjaliṃ gṛhītvā oṃ sāṃ sauṃ guhatattvā-dhipataye guhāya namaḥ । iti pavitraṃ datvā ārātrikaṃ datvā catuḥ samskāra(l.4)saṃśuddhaṃ svāhāntamūlenātha ūrdhvavāratri(tra)yaṃ paribhrāmyāvatārya ācamanārghyau ca datvā darpaṇachatracāmarāṇyupāhṛtya sita(l.5)vāsastālavṛnte ca datvā jayantādibhi-stūyamānaṃ santuṣṭaṃ guhaṃ dhyātvā dviraṣṭakalopetaṃ prāsādoccāraṇena puṣpāñjaliṃ (l.6) datvā । mahāmudrayā prarocya ṣaṇmukhīṃ datvā haste puṣpaṃ gṛhītvā hṛtsaṃpūjitayākṣamālayā mūlamantreṇāṣṭottaraśataṃ japtvā astrakavacahṛdayamantrerhṛdayakavacāstramantraiḥ sampuṭīkṛtya astrarakṣitakavacāvakuṇṭhitadakṣiṇahaste gṛhītvā (p.75b, f.100a, l.1) sārghyajalamudbhavamudrayā bhūmiṣṭhajānuḥ om । 

guhyātiguhyagoptā tvaṃ gṛhāṇāsmatkṛtaṃ japam । 
siddhirbhavatu me yena tvatprasādāt va(tva)yi sthite ॥ (l.2)

	oṃ sāṃ yatkiñcitkarma he deva sadā sū(su) kṛtaduṣkṛtam tan me guhapadastasya bhuṅkṣvābhayaśaṃ guha । oṃ sāṃ guho dātā guho bhoktā guhaḥ sarvamidaṃ (l.3) jagat । guho yajati sarvatra yo guho so'hamevat(meva tat) । sahṛdā śokatrayeṇa japaṃ karma tathātmānaṃ krameṇa guhasya varadahaste (l.4) nivedayet । kevale hṛdayā nivedya brahmāṅgānāṃ daśāṃśaṃ japtvā nivedayet । brahmāṅgakānāṃ prathamāvaraṇaṃ syāt (l.5) vidyeśvarāṇāṃ ca gaṇeśvarāṇāṃ dikpālakānāṃ ca daśāyudhānāṃ guhasya pañcāvaraṇakrameṇa oṃ sām īśānajagadbhuve namaḥ । (l.6) oṃ siṃ tatpuruṣavacadbhuve namaḥ । oṃ sūm aghoraviśvabhuve namaḥ । oṃ seṃ vāmadevarudrabhuve namaḥ । oṃ saiṃ sadyojāta(l.7)vṛhadbhuve namaḥ । naiṛte sarvavaktrabhuvadbhuve namaḥ āgneye । oṃ sāṃ hṛdayāya namaḥ īśāne । oṃ siṃ śirase svāhā naiṛte । oṃ sūṃ (l.8) śikhāyai vauṣaṭ । vāyavye । oṃ saiṃ kavacāya huṃ caturdikṣu । oṃ saḥ astrāya phaṭ । iti prathamāvaraṇam । oṃ sāṃ jayantāya namaḥ । oṃ sām (p.76a, l.1) agniśikhāyai namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ kṛttikāputrāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ bhūtapataye namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ senānye namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ guhāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ hemaśūlāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ (l.2) viśālākṣāya namaḥ । ityabhyarcya । iti dvitīyāvaraṇam ॥

uttare । oṃ sāṃ devasenāyai namaḥ । dakṣiṇe । oṃ sāṃ mahāvalyai namaḥ । pūrve oṃ sāṃ gajendrāya (l.3) namaḥ । paścime oṃ sāṃ mayūrāya namaḥ । āgneyādicatuṣkoṇeṣu । oṃ sāṃ sudehāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ sumukhāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ sumitrāya (l.4) namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ medhāyai namaḥ । iti tṛtīyāvaraṇam ॥

caturthāvaraṇe । oṃ sām indrāya namaḥ । oṃ sām agnaye namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ yamāya namaḥ । oṃ (l.5) sāṃ niṛṛtaye namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ varuṇāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ vāyave namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ kuberāya namaḥ । oṃ sām īśānāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ brahmaṇe (l.6) namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ viṣṇave namaḥ । iti caturthāvaraṇam ॥

pañcamāvaraṇe । oṃ sā(sāṃ) vajrāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ śaktaye namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ daṇḍāya namaḥ । (l.7) oṃ sāṃ khaḍgāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ pāśāya namaḥ । oṃ sām aṅkuśāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ gadāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ triśūlāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ padmāya namaḥ । oṃ (p.76b, f.101a, agnikāryam, l.1) sāṃ cakrāya namaḥ । iti pañcāvaraṇam ॥
	sarvopacāraiḥ saṃpūjayet । iti śrī skandaprokte kumāratantrāgame subrahmaṇyamaṇṭapapūjāvidhiḥ samāptaḥ ॥ (l.2) śubhamastu hariḥ om ॥

(agnikāryam -)
athāgnikāryārthamadhyeṣaaye । labdhānujñaḥ । kuṇḍāntikaṃ gatvā upaviśya mūlena nyastvā । cakṣuṣā kuṇḍasya nirīkṣaṇam astreṇa huṃ phaḍiti prokṣaṇam । (l.3) tāḍanaṃ huṃ phaḍantāstreṇābhyukṣaṇaṃ kavacena huṃ phaḍantāstreṇa khātvoddhṛtya । hṛdayenāpūrya । samīkṛtya । varmaṇā nyasitvā (l.4) kavacenānulipya । madhyamādipaścimāntaṃ oṃ sāṃ śāntyatītakalāyai namaḥ । oṃ saiṃ śāntikalāyai namaḥ । oṃ sūṃ vidyākalāyai namaḥ । (l.5) oṃ sviṃ pratiṣṭhākalāyai namaḥ । oṃ svāṃ nivṛttikalāyai namaḥ । iti saṃpūjya । kavacena tra(tri)sūtreṇa kuṇḍe saṃveṣṭya । oṃ sāṃ (l.6) kalāmayāya kuṇḍāya namaḥ । ityabhyarcya । huṃ phaḍantāstreṇa  rekhātrayamudīcīnāmekarekhāṃ prācīnāṃ kṛtvā huṃ phaḍantāstreṇobhayatra (l.7) tra(tri)śūlākṛtinā kuśatrayeṇa vajrīkaraṇaṃ kṛtvā । pūrvottarāgrakuśadvayena hṛdayena catuṣpadā nyāsaṃ kṛtvā ityaṣṭādaśasamskārānantaram akṣapāṭārthaṃ (p.77a, l.1) kuṇḍasya kaṇṭhe kuśacatuṣṭayaṃ kavacena nyastvā kuṇḍanābhau hṛdayena viṣṭaraṃ datvā । tadupari –

nābhimadhye tu vāgīśīṃ nīlotpaladalaprabhām ।
ṛtusnātāṃ sakūrcasthāṃ *dhyāyettanmūlamantrataḥ ॥* (l.2)

	oṃ sāṃ sauṃ vāgīśvarābhyāṃ namaḥ । ityabhyarcya । araṇeḥ sūryakāntāt dvijagra(gṛ)hādvā tāmrapātre'gnimādāya kuṇḍasyāgre (l.3) nidhāya huṃ phaḍantāstreṇa agnikhaṇḍaṃ niṛṛtideśe visṛjya mūlena nirīkṣya astreṇa prokṣya tāḍya kavacenābhyukṣya । oṃ sāṃ vahnimūrtaye (l.4) namaḥ । ityutpatattārākāramudrayā pūrakeṇa gṛhītvā lalāṭamadhyasthāgninā ekīkṛtya oṃ hrīṃ hṛdayāya namaḥ । suṣumnanāḍikayā utyu(l.5)tpataṅgamagninibhaṃ nābhikuṇḍāgninā ekīkṛtya ṣaḍaṅgenā-bhimantrya kavacenāvakuṇṭhya astreṇa saṃrakṣya hṛdayenābhyukṣya śakti(l.6)mantreṇa dhenumudrayāmṛtīkṛtya bhūmiṣṭhajānunāgninā pātraṃ kuṇḍopari triḥ paribhrāmya । sumukho'gnikuṇḍe vinyasya vāgīśvareṇa (l.7) īśānadigbhirasau dhyātvā oṃ sāṃ śiva-bījamiti dhyātvā vāgīśvarīgarbhanāḍikāyām ātmasammukhaṃ vāgīśvareṇa nikṣipyamāṇaṃ vibhāvya । (p.77b, f.102a, l.1) tato nikṣiptanābhau hṛnmantreṇa kuṭīkṛtya । oṃ hāṃ vāgīśvarīvāgīśvarābhyāṃ svāhetyarghyajalena śaucācamanaṃ datvā kavacena sa digdhanairācchādya (l.2) astreṇa prajvālya oṃ hāṃ garbhāgnaye namaḥ । iti saṃpūjya astreṇa śatābhimantritadarbhagaṅkaṇa-vāgīśvarī vāmakare dhyātvā dakṣiṇamekhalopari nidhāya (l.3) garbhādhānārthaṃ sadyojātenāvāhya hṛdayena tilāhutipañcakaṃ datvā iti garbhādhānaṃ tri(tṛ)tīye māsi puṃsavanārthaṃ vāmadevenābhyarcya (l.4) śirasāhutipañcakaṃ hutvā puryaṣṭakadehāya namaḥ iti śivabhūtaṃ jalabinduṃ dadyāt । iti puṃsavanaṃ ṣaṣṭhe māsi sīmantonnayanārtham (l.5) aghoreṇābhyarcya । śikhayāhuti-pañcakaṃ hutvā vaktrakalpana-vaktrodghāṭana-niṣkṛtyarthaṃ śikhayaiva tilāhutipañcakaṃ (l.6) dadyāt । iti sīmantonnayanaṃ daśame māsi jananārthaṃ tatpuruṣeṇābhyarcya kavacena tilāhutipañcakaṃ datvā sadyaḥ sūtakanivṛttaye (l.7) kuṇḍamastreṇa saṃprokṣya darbhakaṅkaṇamunmucya । svarṇakaṅkaṇaṃ kṛtamiti dhyātvā vāgīśvarīṃ śuddhāmeva dhyātvā prāṅmukho hi tato'gniśca (p.78a, l.1) prāṅmukhāścaiva ṛtvijāḥ prāṅmukhā hi praṇītādyāḥ pratyaṅmukhasthito bhavet । pariṣecanaparistīryamantrapūtena vāriṇā । havāddharuṇadṛṣṭvā pratyaṅ(l.2)mukhasthito bhavet । eṣa hi devaṃ pratiśonusarvāḥ pūrvo hi jātaḥ sau garbhe antaḥ savijāyamānaḥ sa janiṣyamāṇaḥ pratyaṅmukhāstiṣṭhati viśvato (l.3) mukhaḥ । vahnirakṣārthaṃ madhyamekhalāsu pūrvottarāgradvā-triṃśaddarbhanirmitān bāhumātraparimitān viṣṭarān pūrvādikrameṇāstreṇa vinyasya (l.4) dakṣiṇānuttarānvinyasya utrānadharān vinyasya । pātrottarāsanaṃ brahmadakṣiṇāsanaṃ praṇītottarāsanaṃ vinyasya । sṛksṛvau (l.5) ca ghṛtasthālīṃ praṇītāṃ prokṣaṇīṃ tathā samitkūrcaṃ ca paridhiṃ darbhāṃ caiva kamaṇḍaluṃ dravyapātrāṇi sarvāṇi vāme kāṃśapari (l.6) nyaset । adhomukhāni pātrāṇi dvindvarūpāṇi sādhayet । ājyasthālīṃ sṛvaṃ sādaitvā juhūṃ praṇītāṃ sādaitvā (l.7) pūrvaprokṣaṇīmidhmāṃ sādaitvā tatpūrve dravyāṇi pātrāṇi sādaitvā prokṣaṇīpātramādāya jalenaiva pūrayet vitastimātra(p.78b, f.103a, l.1)darbhāgraṃ madhyagrandhi-samanvitamaṅguṣṭhānāmikābhyā(bhyāṃ) dvābhyāmudako plavanaṃ kṛtvā pātrāṇi unmukhāni kṛtvā kavacena prokṣaṇījalena saṃprokṣya saptadarbhakṛta(l.2)kūrcaṃ kuṇḍasya dakṣiṇabhāge nidhāya brahmāṇamabhyarcya । praṇītāpātramādāya jalenāpūrya puṣpa-kūrcopari vinyasya । tra(tri)rutplavanaṃ kṛtvā atha ūrdhvaṃ (l.3) kuśadvayasaṃyuktaṃ kuṇḍasya vāmabhāge nidhāya viṣṇumabhyarcya sṛksṛvau sakuśāvūrdhvavadanau adhomukhau gṛhītvā nirīkṣaṇādicatuḥ saṃskāraṃ (l.4) kṛtvā hṛdayena vāratrayaṃ pratāpya kuśamūlena mūlaṃ madhyena madhyam agreṇāgraṃ saṃspṛśya । taṃ kuśamagnau nikṣipya kavacena (l.5) tra(tri)sūtreṇa veṣṭya kuṇḍasya dakṣiṇabhāge kuśopari saṃsthāpya tattvatrayaṃ tadadhipatiṃ ca saṃpūjya sṛci śaktaye namaḥ । sṛve śambhave namaḥ । (l.6) tadūrdhve ghṛtasahitapātraṃ vinyasya । darbholkaṃ ghṛtamukhe darśayitvā tadulkamagnau nikṣipya punardarbhadvayāgraṃ saṃgṛhya prokṣaṇī(l.7)jalena saṃprokṣya ghṛtamadhye pātramuddhṛtya kuṇḍasyopari pradakṣiṇaṃ triḥ paribhrāmya sumukhena vinyasya svadehaṃ brahmamayaṃ dhyātvā (p.79a, l.1) kuśadvayāgraṃ gṛhītvā hṛdaye tatvatsammukhaṃ ghṛtabindudvayaṃ nikṣipya vahnikoṇe nidhāya svadehaṃ viṣṇumayaṃ dhyātvā viṣṇave svāhetyadhiśravaṇaṃ kuṇḍanābhau nidhāya । (l.2) svadehaṃ rudramayaṃ dhyātvā rudrāya svāheti hutvā ityudvāsanam । prādeśamātradarbhāgradvayanirmitaṃ madhyagranthipavitrakaṃ ghṛtamadhye nikṣipya utplavanamadhogamanaṃ (l.3) saṃplavanaṃ kṛtvā granthiṃ visṛjya prokṣaṇajalena saṃprokṣya agnau visṛjya । ūrdhvamekhalāsu pūrvottarāgrān bāhumātraparimitān kaniṣṭhapariṇāhān (l.4) paridhīn pūrvadakṣiṇottarapaścimakrameṇa vinyasya । paridhiṣu brahmaviṣṇurudrānantānabhyarcya viṣṭareṣu indrādilokapālānabhyarcya agniṃ (l.5) pariṣicya aditenumanyasva iti dakṣiṇataḥ anumatena manyasveti sarasvatena manyasveti uttarataḥ prācīna....devasavitaḥ prasū(su)veti samantaṃ pradakṣiṇaṃ (l.6) lālāpanodanāyāgneḥ puṣṭaye ca mūlamadhyaghṛtāktānupañcadaśaidhmā sakṛnmūlena hutvā ghṛtapātraṃ pūrvāgraṃ vinyasya śuklapakṣe (l.7) piṅgaleḍāsuṣumnā-nāḍītrayaṃ vibhāvya madhye suṣumnāṃ smṛtvā sṛksraveṇa ghṛtadakṣiṇabhāgāt ādāya agnaye svāheti hutvā agnidakṣiṇa(l.8)netre vāmabhāgādājyamādāya । somāya svāheti hutvā agnivāmanetre ghṛtamadhyādādāya agnīṣomābhyāṃ svāheti hutvā agnimadhyanetre (p.79b, f.104a, l.1) bhāgatrayādājyamādāya agnaye sviṣṭakṛte svāhetyagnimukhe hutvā kṛṣṇapakṣe tu iḍāpiṅgalānāḍīdvayaṃ vibhāvya madhye suṣumnāṃ saṃsmṛtya ghṛtavāmabhāgāt sṛveṇādāya (l.2) somāya svāheti hutvā dakṣiṇabhāgādājyamādāya agnaye svāheti hutvā agnidakṣiṇanetre madhyādājyamādāya somāgnibhyāṃ svāhā agnimadhyanetre bhāgatrayādājya(l.3)mādāya agnaye sviṣṭakṛte svāheti hutvā agnivaktre ca hutvā ghṛtaṃ ṣaḍaṅgenābhimantrya kavacenāvakuṇṭhya astreṇa saṃrakṣya hṛdayenābhyarcya śaktimantreṇa (l.4) dhenumudrayāmṛtīkṛtya oṃ hāṃ sadyojātāya svāha(hā) oṃ hiṃ vāmadevāya svāhā oṃ huṃ aghorāya svāha(hā) oṃ heṃ tatpuruṣāya svāhā oṃ hoṃ (l.5) īśānāya svāhā iti vaktrakalpanam oṃ hāṃ hīṃ sadyojātavāmadevābhyāṃ svāhā oṃ hiṃ huṃ vāmadevāghorābhyāṃ svāha(hā) oṃ huṃ heṃ (l.6) aghoratatpuruṣābhyāṃ svāha(hā) oṃ heṃ hoṃ tatpuruṣaīśānābhyāṃ svāhā । oṃ hoṃ heṃ īśānasadyojātābhyāṃ svāhā iti vaktrānusandhānaṃ (l.7) oṃ hāṃ hīṃ huṃ he hoṃ sadyojātavāmadevāghoratatpuruṣaīśānebhya(bhyaḥ) svāhā iti niṛṛti-koṇādīśānakoṇāntam agnikoṇādvāyu(l.8)koṇāntaghṛtadhārayā juhuyāt । iti vaktraikīkaraṇaṃ tato nāmakaraṇārtha-mīśenābhyarcya astreṇa tilāhutipañcakaṃ hutvā śivāgne tvaṃ (p.80a, l.1) hutāśaneti agnernāmakaraṇaṃ kṛtvā vahnibījaṃ samuddhṛtya juhuyāt pañcaviṃśati annaprāśanacaulopanayanavedābhyāsavivāhāntaṃ sadyādibrahma(l.2)pañcakairyathākrameṇa juhuyāt saptāhutiṃ pṛthak pṛthak kṛtvā nāmāntaṃ bālamagniṃ syāt dā?vratāṃ tu yauvanam । vivāha(hā)ntaṃ tu vṛddhaṃ ca agnitraya-mudāhṛtam । gārhakā(l.3)havanīyāgnibālayauvanasaṃjñakau । dakṣiṇāgniśca vṛddhāgni sthāpakādiśivotsave nityapūjāvasāne tu yogyaśca samudāhṛtaḥ । oṃ hauṃ (l.4) vāgīśvarīvāgīśvarābhyāṃ svāhā । viṃśatiśatakaṃ japtvā tau visṛjya kuṇḍamānaṃ yathābhilaṣitaṃ vaktraṃ parikalpya bāhau tu dakṣiṇe pūrve (l.5) cottarābhimukho sthitaḥ । anyeṣu prāṅmukho bhūtvā homaṃ kuryādvicakṣaṇaḥ । karmaparipūraṇārthaṃ pūrṇāhutiṃ kṛtvā । tadyathā । sarpiṣā (l.6) sra(sṛ)cimāpūrya puṣkaloparyadhomukhaṃ sra(sṛ/sru)vaṃ kṛtvā tadagre puṣpaṃ vinyasya vāmadakṣiṇahastābhyāṃ śaṅkhavatsaṃsaktābhyāṃ punaḥ savyena (l.7) saṃlagnau tāvupādāyordhvakāyo nābhyāṃ tiryagvidhāya sragagradattadṛṣṭirvauṣaḍantamūlamantramuccaran vāmastanāntare sṛṅmūlamānīya (l.8) anudvigno yavapramāṇa-dhārayā samastamājyaṃ vahnau heti kṣipet iti pūrṇāhutiṃ hutvā oṃ haṃ hṛdayāya svāheti ghṛtabinduprakṣepaṇāt anyaghṛtaṃ (p.80b, f.105a, l.1) śodhayet । mūrtimūrtipā sva sva kuṇḍe samskāraṃ kuryāt । navakumḍapakṣe navadhā pañcakuṇḍapakṣe pañcadhā carvarthaṃ cāgniṃ vibhajyā(jya) (l.2) tattaddikṣu kuṇḍeṣu tattadvibhajyamānāgniṃ nikṣipya avaśeṣitāgniṃ tasmin kuṇḍe prajvālya tasminnāsanādyāvaraṇāntaṃ guhaṃ sāṅgaṃ sampūjya (l.3) ekakuṇḍapakṣe guhaṃ saṃpūjya mūrtipān sva sva kuṇḍe vahnirakṣārthaṃ darbhaviṣṭarān pātrottarāsanaṃ brahmadakṣiṇāsanaṃ praṇītottarā(l.4)sanaṃ vinyasya pātrasamskāraṃ ca pūrvavat kuryāt ājyapātre ajyam agniṃ nikṣipya paridhiviṣṭareṣu devān sampūjya pañcakuṇḍa(l.5)pakṣe pūrvādicatuṣkuṇḍeṣu īśvarādikāraṇeśvarān vinyasya sampūjayet navakuṇḍapakṣe pūrvādyaṣṭakuṇḍeṣu bhavādīn (l.6) saṃpūjayet । ghaṭasthaṇḍila-vahnisthadarbheṇa saṃhāramudrayā mūrtipaiḥ saha nāḍīsandhānaṃ kuryāt । ūrṇātantunibhaṃ nāḍīmuditena (l.7) vinirgatāṃ anutena viśajñānaṃ nirgatāmuditena ca ātmanāmuditenaiva viśantīṃ cintayettataḥ । nāḍīsandhānārtham īśānamūrdhāya svāheti (p.81a, ratnanyāsam, l.1) ekaikāhutidānena tarpaṇam । deśakālapādanadurnimittapādanahetave mūlena śataṃ hutvā pāśupatāstreṇa śataṃ hutvā samidādīn yathoktavajjuhuyāt । samidā(l.2)dājyacaruṃ lājānmāṣaśāliyavastilam । saktisarṣapamudgāni sadyāstrāntaihunetsudhīḥ । iti agnikāryavidhipaṭalaḥ samāptaḥ ॥ śubhamastu ॥ (l.3)

(ratnanyāsam –)
atha ratnanyāsavidhiṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ । tatra kuṇḍasamīpaṃ gatvā saṅkalpya । vitānadhvajapūgadarbhapuṣpamālādibhiralaṅkṛtya tatra bhadrāsane saṃsthāpya tadagre (l.4) sthaṇḍilaṃ kalpya hastamātrapravistaraṃ likhya navapadaṃ tatra tatpade pātre saṃsthitam । 

tanmadhye padmarāgaṃ syāt aindre vaiḍūryameva ca ।
āgneye *vajravinyāsaṃ puṣparāgaṃ tu dakṣiṇe ॥* (l.5)
naiṛte sphaṭikaṃ caiva vāruṇe mauktikaṃ tathā ।
indranīlaṃ tu vāyavyāṃ māṇikyamuttare tathā ॥
aiśāne *tu pravālaṃ syāt sthāpayennavaratnakam ।* (l.6)
puṣpaṃ pavitrakaṃ caiva toyaṃ ratnena saṃyutam ॥
catuḥ samskārasaṃyuktaṃ ṣaḍudhvāsanamarcayet । (l.7)
dhyānapūrvaṃ tu saṃpūjya dhūpadīpanivedanam ॥
bimbāṅgaṃ vinyaseduktaṃ puṣpagandhaiśca saṃyutam ।
madhye lalāṭakaṇṭhe ca hṛdayenābhipādayoḥ ॥ (p.81b, f.106a, nayanonmīlanam, l.1)
bāhudvaye ca saṃsthāpya saṃhitāmantramarcayet । 
aṣṭapuṣpeṇa saṃpūjya abhiṣekamathācaret ॥
ratnanyāsaṃ tathā kṛtvā nayanonmīlanaṃ kramāt ।
iti kumāratantre ratnanyāsavidhiḥ *samāptaḥ ॥ om ॥* (l.2)

(nayanonmīlanam -)
	nayanonmīlanavidhiṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ ।
tato vedikādakṣiṇe bhāge sthaṇḍilaṃ kṛtvā tadupari śāntikumbhān saṃsthāpya astra(l.3)mantreṇābhimantritān kṛtvā nayanonmīlanārthaṃ snānamaṇṭapaṃ pūjayeyuḥ । snānakāle adhivāsanamaṇṭapābhyantare dīkṣitāḥ saṃhitāṃ japeyuḥ । (l.4) adīkṣitā(tāḥ) maṇṭapādbahiḥ śāntiṃ paṭheyuḥ guhapañcagavyādijale ca saṃsnāpya beraṃ sthaṇḍile phalakopari pūrvābhyāṃ vinyasya (l.5) pracchannapaṭenāvartyā(rtya) ācāryājñayā śilpi(lpiḥ) susnātvā śuklavastropavītabhasmadhāraṇāni kṛtvā paścimarekhāṃ kṛṣṇajyoti(l.6)rmaṇḍalaṃ vilikhya evaṃ nayanonmīlanaṃ kṛtvā śilpinaṃ visṛjya । beram anyasthaṇḍile vinyasya guru hemasūcyādinā netramantra(l.7)manusmaran netronmīlanaṃ kṛtvā hemarekhāṃ kṛtvā tarjanīmadhyamānāmikā-ṅgulibhiḥ sūryasomāgnyādhidevatāṃ saṃsmaran netra(p.82a, pratimāśuddhi, grāmapradakṣiṇam, l.1)mudrāṃ darśaityavā madhuvātā ṛtāyata iti mantreṇa madhu ca ghṛtamimaṃ kṣīreti ghṛtaṃ ca āpyāyasveti mantreṇa kṣīraṃ ca darśaitvā etatpūrvaṃ pratyekaṃ pratyekaṃ (l.2) prasthamātraṃ kāmsyapātre prapūrya paścāt madhusarpibhyāṃ hemadarūrdhvāya netre sammṛjya sa brahmajñānamantreṇa bhūsuraṃ darśayitvā paścāt sarve (l.3) janā(nāḥ) hara hara śabdaṃ vadanto darśanaṃ kurvantu । 

	vidhivat paścāt pañcagavyaṃ saṅkalpya tatra madodakaṃ kāṣāyodakaṃ pallavodakaṃ kṣīraṃ (l.4) ratnodakaṃ gulodakaṃ phalasāraṃ puṣpodakaṃ kumbhānastreṇa prokṣitān nirīkṣaṇādicatuḥ samskārasamskṛtān saṃsthāpya teṣu (l.5) jalaṃ pūrya kṣīrādisamudrānabhyarcya tajjalaiḥ saṃsnāpya abhyaṅgaṃ kṛtvā āpohiṣṭheti mantreṇa pañcagavyena saṃsnāpya (l.6) hiraṇya-varṇāmiti mantreṇa mṛdodakakāṣāyodakapallavodakaphalasārakṣīrodakaiśca saṃsnāpya antarāntarā (l.7) śuddhajalena saṃsnāpya skandamūlena śuddhajalena saṃsnāpya varmajaptena pītavāsasācchādya upavītaṃ samarpya gandhapuṣpai(p.82b, f.107a, जलाधि-वासनम्, l.1)ralaṅkṛtya kṣamasveti vijñāpayet । rathādivāhane saṃsnāpya devaṃ sarvato'dya vitānachatracāmaranānāvinodaiḥ saha svastiṃ japadbhi(l.2)rbrāhmaṇaiḥ saha bhaktajanaiḥ saha yajamānaḥ grāmapradakṣiṇaṃ kuryāt । śrī dakṣiṇāmūrtigurave namaḥ । (l.3)

jalādhivāsanam – jalatīre śuddhadeśe devaṃ vinyasya vastrādibhūṣaṇairalaṅkṛtya navavastreṇācchādya lambakūrcaṃ badhvā jalamadhye (l.4) vitānadhvajakadalīstambhayuktāyāṃ prapāsāyāṃ? kaṇṭhamagna-nābhimagnajale vā phalakopari taddakṣiṇatā mahāvallīmuttare (l.5) devasenāṃ prākśirordhvavadanaṃ vinyasya pūrvādyaṣṭakalaśeṣu lokapālānabhyarcya jalatīre caturdikṣu vedādhyayanam । (l.6) ekarātraṃ dvirātraṃ vā pañcarātraṃ jaloṣitam । jalādberāṇyutthāpya ārdravastraṃ lambakūrcaṃ visṛjya । śuddhavastrā(l.7)dibhi-ralaṅkṛtya rathādivāhane āropya nṛttagītavādyadhūpadīpavinodaiḥ pa(pra)tiphalalājapuṣpāvakīraṇavāryaśilpiyajamānasahitaṃ (p.83a, śayanādhivāsanam, l.1) pradakṣiṇakrameṇa yāgamaṇṭapasya paścimadvāramānīyāvatārya hṛdayena vauṣaḍantena sāṣṭā-ṅgārghyaṃ dadet । vrīhitaṇḍulalājapuṣpahṛdayakuśenā(l.2)stīrya carmajāti(di)pañcaśayanamṛduvastrayugmāni tatrāstīrya astreṇa prokṣya kṣityādimāyāntaṃ sapadhāsane? vedimadhye devaṃ cottarābhimukhaṃ (l.3) vinyasya mahāvallīdevasene pārśvadvaye vinyasya । gandhapuṣpādibhiralaṅkṛtya pratisarabandhaṃ ca kṛtvā । prākśiraścordhvavaktraṃ tān śāyayitvā pāda(l.4)pradeśapīṭhe vinyasya śikhā(vā)ya upasthānaṃ kṛtvā varmajaptavāsasācchādya tadupari raktavastreṇācchādayet । tato beraṃ śānti(l.5)ghaṭāmbhasā saṃprokṣaṇaṃ vinyasya mūrtim īśānamūrdhādīn vinyasya gandhapuṣpadhūpadīpādibhiḥ saṃpūjya । pādābhyaṅgārthaṃ madhu ca (l.6) sarpiśca pātreṣu jagadbhuvo'dhipadamantreṇa saṃsthāpya mūlenāvāhya devaśirobhāge dakṣiṇakarṇapradeśe skandakumbhaṃ ca (l.7) devīśirobhāge nāma nāmavardhanīdveyaṃ ca vinyasya kumbhe ādhāraśaktyādisubrahmaṇyāsanāntaṃ saṃpūjya annanigrahaṃ beraṃ tadvigrahaṃ smaran dhyāyet । (p.83b, f.108a, tattvatattveśvaram, l.1) mūrtiṃ sampūjya । dehe brahmāṅgādimūrdhnādi nyasitvā darśai(yi)tvā tejomaṇḍalamadhyame somāskandamūrtiṃ sañcintya tayormadhye skandadehā-dvipādvīpavinirgamaṃ (l.2) tejomayaṃ saṃhāramudrayā dvādaśānte vinyasya puṣpasahitenāñjalihastenāvāhanakrameṇa kumbhe āvāhya āvāhanādimudrāṃ darśayitvā mahāvallī devasenāṃ (l.3) ca vardhanyāṃ ṣaḍudhvā(tthā)sanaṃ ca datvā mūlenāvāhya ṣaḍaṅgena sampūjya nirodhya pūrvādyaṣṭadikṣu aṣṭakumbhān vinyasya praṇavādinamo'ntena (l.4) oṃ sāṃ jayantāya namaḥ । oṃ sām agniśikhāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ kṛttikāputrāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ bhūtapataye namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ senānyai namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ (l.5) guhāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ hemaśūlāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ viśālākṣāya namaḥ । ityaṣṭakumbhān abhyarcya beramūle sthitvā tattvatattveśvarā(rān) nyaset । (l.6) 

ātmavidyāśivākhyāṃ ca jānukaṇṭhaśiro'vadhiḥ । 
aṇḍaśva?śuddhividyāntaṃ nyasya mūrtyādipañcakaiḥ ॥
pūrvavadaṣṭapakṣe tu kṣmādyā vidyeśvarādhipaḥ । (l.7) 
ṣaṇmukhaṃ śaktibāṇaṃ ca kārtikeyo guhaṃ tathā ॥
skandena mayūravāhaśca senāni ca viśākhinaḥ ।
aṇḍādimohinibhrāntaṃ pīṭhakaṇṭhe'tra kalpanam ॥ ityāgamaḥ । (l.8)

(tattvatattveśvaram -)
	beramūle sthitvā mūrdhnādipādaparyantaṃ tattvatattveśvaraṃ nyaset । śirobhāge oṃ sāṃ śuddhavidyātattvāya namaḥ । śuddha-vidyātattvādhipataye oṃ sāṃ śambarāya (p.84a, l.1) namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ māyātattvāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ māyātattvādhipataye oṃ sāṃ gahaneśāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ kālatattvāya namaḥ । kālatattvādhipataye oṃ sāṃ kālātmane namaḥ । (l.2) oṃ sāṃ kalātattvāya namaḥ । kalātattvādhipataye oṃ sāṃ jñānabindave namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ niyatitattvāya namaḥ । niyatitattvādhipataye oṃ sāṃ niyamākṣāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ (l.3) vidyātattvāya namaḥ । vidyātattvādhipataye oṃ sāṃ vedavijñānāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ rāgatattvāya namaḥ । rāgatattvādhipataye oṃ sāṃ kāmadāya namaḥ । (l.4) oṃ sāṃ puruṣatattvāya namaḥ । puruṣatattvādhipataye oṃ sāṃ sūkṣmadehāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ prakṛtitattvāya namaḥ । । upasthatattvādhipataye ghanānandāya namaḥ । (l.4) oṃ sāṃ śabdatattvāya namaḥ । śabdatattvādhipataye sūkṣmanādāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ sparśatattvāya namaḥ । sparśatattvādhipataye (l.5) balavaktrāya namaḥ (p.85b, f.100a, l.1) vinyasya । 

rū(gurū)padeśāt kandādeḥ mantramuccārayet śanaiḥ । 
nādaśaktimayaṃ sūkṣmaghaṇḍā(ṭā)nādavisarpaṇam ॥
kāraṇatyāgaye nābhe brahmarandhrābhinirgatam । (l.2)
divākarasahasrābhaṃ vidyutkoṭisamaprabhām ॥
sphuradratnivitānena pṛthakpītā digantaram ।
mantreṇa manasā bhinnaṃ prāptānandasamodayam ॥
dvādaśāntāt samānīya *niṣkalaṃ vyāpakaṃ gurum ।* (l.3)
aṣṭatriṃśatkalopetaṃ sahasrakiraṇojva(jjva)lam ॥
sarvaśaktimayaṃ sāṅgaṃ dhyātvedvā?calasannibham ।
tejobimba*lalāṭasthaṃ gaṇḍikādvāramānayot ॥* (l.4)
suṣumnā nirgataṃ dhyātvā sphurattārakasannibham ।
viśantaṃ brahmarandhreṇa sakalaṃ vyāpakaṃ nyaset ॥ (l.5)
ji(jī)vanyāsa(saṃ) bhaveddeva guhe sarvārthasādhakam ।

	oṃ sauṃ sūṃ skandāya namaḥ । āvāhanasthāpanasannidhānasannirodhanāvakuṇṭhanā(l.6)mṛtamudrāṃ darśaitvā pādyācamanārghyapuṣpāṇi datvā raktavastreṇācchādyā(dya) । bhasmadarbhatilaiḥ prākāraṃ vidadhyāt pīṭhordhvakaṇṭhā(l.7)dhobhāgeṣu । trikhaṇḍakalpanā-pīṭhordhvamāyātattvādi । oṃ sāṃ māyātattvāya namaḥ । māyātattvādhipataye oṃ sāṃ gahaneśāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ kāla-(l.8)tattvāya namaḥ । namaḥ । (l.5) oṃ sāṃ manastattvāya namaḥ । manastattvādhipataye oṃ sāṃ saṅkalpāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ śrotratattvāya namaḥ । śrotratattvādhipataye oṃ sāṃ (l.6) vyogocarāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ tvaktattvāya namaḥ । tvaktattvādhipataye oṃ sāṃ sarvāṅgāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ cakṣustattvāya namaḥ । (l.7) cakṣustattvādhipataye oṃ sāṃ prakāśakāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ jihvātattvāya namaḥ । jihvātattvādhipataye oṃ sāṃ mahāvaktrāya namaḥ oṃ (l.8) sāṃ nāsātattvāya namaḥ । nāsātattvādhipataye oṃ sāṃ vilumpa(mba)kāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ vāktattvāya namaḥ । vāktattvādhipataye oṃ sāṃ (p.86b, f.101a, l.1) dundubhāya namaḥ । oṃ namaḥ । iti vinyasya । tritattvanyāsam । pīṭhordhvakaṇṭhā-dhobhāgeṣu । oṃ sāṃ skandaśivatattvāya namaḥ । skanda(l.8)śivatattvādhipataye oṃ sām icchāyai namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ skanda-śaktividyātattvāya namaḥ । skandaśaktividyātattvādhipataye oṃ sāṃ jñānyai namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ skandaśaktyātma(p.87a, l.1)-tattvāya namaḥ । skandaśaktyātmatattvādhipataye oṃ kuṣṭā(guhā)yai namaḥ । iti vinyasya pratibhāgaṃ pūrvādiṣu so kṣmāmūrtaye namaḥ । kṣmāmūrtyadhipataye oṃ sāṃ śaṅkunyai? namaḥ । oṃ (l.2) sāṃ vahnimūrtaye namaḥ । vahnimūrtyadhipataye oṃ sāṃ revatyai namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ yajamānamūrtaye namaḥ । indu-mūrtyai namaḥ । (l.4) indumūrtyādhipataye oṃ sāṃ sītalāyai namaḥ । khamūrtyai namaḥ । khamūrtyadhipataye oṃ sāṃ mūrtyai namaḥ । iti vinyasya nirodhyā(dhya) kriyāśaktyai namaḥ । yadvā (l.5) devīyuktaṃ cait । dakṣiṇabhāge । oṃ ghrīṃ mahāvalyai namaḥ । vāmabhāge । oṃ ghrīṃ devasenāyai namaḥ । ityāvāhyā(hya) । saṃsthāpya । nirodhayet । (l.6) śaktibhyāṃ saha pratiṣṭhā cet । śirokaṇṭhajānudeśeṣu pīṭhavat tattvatattveśvarān icchākriyātattvādhipati pratibhāgam । pūrvādyaṣṭadikṣu । mūrtimūrtīśvarī (l.7) ca vinyasya tattanmūlenāvāhya । saṃsthāpya । nirodhayet । eteṣāṃ bhasmatilaiśca pratyai(tye)kaṃ prākāratrayaṃ vidadhyāt । sva sva dik lokapālā(l.8)nadhvarakṣārthamadhyeṣya । kuṇḍāntikaṃ gatvā । kuṇḍamadhye āsanāvaraṇakrameṇa devīṃ śaktidvayaṃ ca saṃpūjyā(jya) । nābhau kṣayāśaktiṃ vinyasyā(sya) । (p.87b, f.102a, l.1) ghaṭasthaṇḍilavahnisthena guhena mūrtipaiḥ saha nāḍīsandhānaṃ kuryāt । ūrṇātantunibhānāḍīmuditena pracoditām । gurūditena mārgeṇa viśantīṃ tāṃ vinirgatām । (l.2) ātmanānuditenaiva viśantīṃ cintayennidam । nāḍīsandhānaṃ kuryāt । nāḍīsandhānārthamāhutitrayaṃ datvā । oṃ sāṃ skandātmatattvāya namaḥ । skandātmatattvādhipataye oṃ sāṃ (l.3) brahmaṇe namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ hvīṃ skandavidyātattvāya namaḥ । skandavidyātattvādhipataye oṃ sāṃ viṣṇave namaḥ । oṃ hāṃ huṃ skandaśivatattvāya namaḥ । skandaśivatattvā(l.4)dhipataye oṃ hāṃ rudrāya namaḥ । iti saṃpūjya । āhutitrayaṃ datvā pūrvādyaṣṭakuṇḍeṣu mūrtimūrtīśvarān sampūjya āhutitrayeṇa santarpya mūrtipāścā(l.5)ṣṭacatvāre sva sva kuṇḍeṣu devaṃ nyastvā ādhāraśaktiṃ vinyasya āhutitrayeṇa tarpaṇaṃ
