pradhānādyagnikuṇḍānāṃ samidhāḥ samprakīrtitāḥ । (l.8) 
samidājyacaruṃ lājān yavaṃ saktuṃ ca sarṣapam ॥
tilamudgagulaṃ veṇuṃ madhukṣīraṃ punardadhim ।
dravyatrayodaśaṃ proktaṃ homayettu *yathākramam ॥* (p.88a, l.1)
mūlamastraṣaḍaṅgaiḥ samidādīni mantrataḥ ।
śatamardhaṃ tadardhaṃ vā sarvakuṇḍeṣu mūlataḥ ॥
vaktrāṅgānāṃ daśāṃśa(śaṃ) syāt pūrṇāṃ tu śirasā hunet ।
dravyānte *vyāhṛtiṃ hutvā sviṣṭakṛt savya vāhutitaḥ ॥* (l.2)
dravyakālānurodhena śatamardhaṃ tadardhaṃ vā ।

	skandamūlena hutvā । tataḥ sarve guṇā saha devasamīpaṃ gatvā । guruhṛdayena (l.3) teṣāṃ sannidhīkṛtya । śānti-kumbhajalena prokṣya । kuśamūlena skandajānvantadeśaṃ saṃspṛśyā(śya) homasaṅkhyayā japtvā oṃ sāṃ sīṃ skandātmatattva-vidyātattvābhyāṃ (l.4) namaḥ । iti vidyātattve taṃ sañcintya । mūrtimūrtīśvarān saṃpūjya । punaḥ vahnisadanaṃ prāpya oṃ sāṃ skandātmatattvavidyātatvābhyāṃ namaḥ iti (l.5) vidyātattve'vabhṛtaṃ sañcintya । skandavidyātattvāya namaḥ । skandavidyā-tattvādhipataye oṃ sāṃ viṣṇave namaḥ । iti sampūjya । āhutitrayaṃ datvā taṃ (l.6) cādanāya pūrvavaddhṛtvā devasamīpaṃ gatvā śāntyambhasā prokṣya homasaṅkhyayā japtvā । kaṭimadhyāntaṃ na devagalāntaṃ saṃspṛśya (l.7) vidyātattvāṃ tadīśaṃ ca saṃpūjya । nirodhya । oṃ sāṃ vidyātattvaśivatattvābhyāṃ namaḥ iti śivatattvekabhūtaṃ sañcintyā(ntya) punaḥ pūrvavahnisandhānaṃ gatvā (l.8) oṃ sāṃ skandaśivatattvābhyāṃ namaḥ । iti śivatattvekabhūtaṃ sañcintyāhutitritayaṃ datvā taccodanāya pūrvavaddhṛtvā devasamīpaṃ gatvā pīṭhakaṇṭhabhāge (p.88b, f.103a, l.1) kuśamadhyena saṃspṛśya homasaṅkhyayā japtvā śiva-tattvāṃ tadīśaṃ ca sampūjya tattvādhipāṃ hṛdā sannirodhyā(dhya) sampūjya । punarvahnisadanaṃ gatvā oṃ sāṃ pīṭhātmatattvāya (l.2) namaḥ । pīṭhātmatattvādhipataye oṃ sāṃ kṣayāyī namaḥ iti sampūjya । āhutitrayaṃ datvā । pīṭhasamīpaṃ gatvā । pīṭhātmatattvāntatadīśaṃ ca sampūjya tattvādhipānnirodhya । (l.3) śāntyambhasā prokṣya । oṃ sāṃ skandapīṭhātmatattva-vidyātattvābhyāṃ namaḥ iti vidyātattvekabhūtaṃ sañcintya sampūjya (l.4) pūrvavahnisadanaṃ gatvā oṃ sāṃ skandapīṭhātma-tattvābhyāṃ namaḥ iti vidyātattvekabhūtaṃ sañcintya sampūjya āhutitrayeṇa mūrtimūrtīśvarīṃ ca (l.5) santarpyā(rpya) pīṭha-samīpaṃ gatvā pīṭhakaṇṭhakaṇṭhabhāge kuśamadhyena saṃspṛśya homasaṅkhyayā japtvā śāntyambhasā prokṣya vidyātattvā(l.6)dīśaṃ ca sampūjya । nirodhya । oṃ sāṃ vidyātattvaśivatattvābhyāṃ namaḥ iti śivatattvekabhūtaṃ sañcintya sampūjya । mūrtimūrtīśvarīṃ ca sampūjya (l.7) āhutitrayeṇa santarpya mūrtimūrtīśvarīṃ ca santarpya sampūjya । pīṭhasamīpaṃ gatvā । pīṭhordhvabhāge kuśatrayeṇa santarpya saṃspṛśya (p.89a, l.1) homasaṅkhyayā japtvā śāntyambhasā prokṣya śivatattva tadīśaṃ ca saṃpūjya hṛdā nirodhya saṃpūjya śaktidvayayuktaṃ cet pīṭhavat tattvatattveśvarīṃ nyāsādi (l.2) sampūjya । pratyekaṃ vahnau daśatarpaṇapūrṇāṃ vidadhyāt । tato deśāpādana-kālāpādana-durnimittaśānti-sunimittāpādanahetave । (l.3) skandamūlena śataṃ śataṃ hutvā । pūrṇāṃ hutvā juhuyāt sampātāhutiṃ hutvā । nūnātiriktaṃ nirharaṇārthaṃ skandamūlenā(l.4)ghoreṇa śataṃ hutvā pūrṇāṃ hutvā ācamanatāmbūladhūpadīpaṃ datvā guhaṃ nirodhya vyāhṛtīṃ hutvā । antarbalibahirbaliṃ ca kṛtvā (l.5) śrotrācamanaṃ datvā devasamīpaṃ gatvā devasya dakṣiṇe śravaṇe । 

bhagavan jñānato mohāt brahmādyaṅgatri(tra)ye'pi yat । (l.6) 
kṛtaṃ karma dhanaṃ vāpi tvadhikaṃ bhaktavatsala ॥
tatsarvam astasampūrṇaṃ tvatprasādādihādhvare ।
oṃ namo bhagavate svāmin guhāya śara*janmane ॥* (l.7)
karma pūrṇamapūrṇaṃ vā kṛpayā pūrṇa gṛhyatām । iti vijñāpya । 
homairvedādighai(gho)ṣaiśca gītanṛttamahotsavaḥ । 
śaktitaḥ saptarātraṃ *vā pañcarātraṃ trirātrakam ॥* (p.89b, f.104a, l.1)
ekarātramadho karma kuryāddevādhivāsanam ।
hotavyaṃ pratyahaṃ sva sva mantrairhomaśataśatam ॥
skandakumbhādipūjāṃ *ca balidānaṃ nivedayet ।* (l.2)
sahamūrtidharaiḥ prāśyā(śya) pañcagavyādikaṃ svapet ॥
homaśeṣaṃ tu bhuñjīyāt saptapañcatrirātrakaiḥ । iti adhivāsanakramam ॥ (l.3)

	vasupakṣe - caturthadivase prātaḥkāle nityāhnikadvayaḥ । sūryapūjābhūtaśuddhyantaryāgaviśeṣārghyadravyamantraṇa-skandabhāva-skanda(l.4)kumbhādi-lokapāla-gaṇapati-guru-sthaṇḍilapūjāṃ ca kṛtvā । devamaṣṭapuṣpikābhyarcya pīṭhaśtidvayaṃ ca saṃpūjya । mantratarpaṇaṃ ca kṛtvā (l.5) mūrtimūrtipāṃ ca sva sva kuṇḍeṣu devamabhyarcya । sva sva mantrān pīṭhaśaktidvayaṃ ca sampūjya mantratarpaṇaṃ ca kṛtvā । adīkṣitā yaḥ kartā (l.6) dakṣayekta(tta) mahāntare carupākaṃ kṛtvā devaṃ vijñāpya । labdhānujñaguruḥ huṃphaḍantāstramuccaran garbhagṛhaṃ praviśya vighnānutsāṭyā(l.7)streṇa prokṣyā(kṣya) ālayasyāntaratiryaviśatisūtrakaṃ vi(viṃ)śatpade guhavistāra(ram) apare saptamaṃ padaṃ madhye kañcit īśāna(p.90a, l.1)māśritya pratiṣṭhāsthāne oṃ sūm ādhāraśaktaye namaḥ । ityabhyarcya tvameva paramāśakti tvamevāsanadhārikā । candrārkatārakaṃ yāvat sthātavyamiha sarvadā । iti (l.2) vijñāpya । nirudhya । tatra pūrvādi-dikṣu । navagarteṣu ratnadhātuoṣadhīsyākaṃ prabhāte hayartuvīryārogyatri(tṛ)ptiśaktirvidhān vibhāvya । lokapālaśambaraiḥ (l.3) lū rūṃ kṣūṃ puṃ yuṃ sūṃ śūṃ ḻaṃ yaṃ saum iti viṣṇukrānticandanakṛṣṇāgarukaṇṭhaśāripāṃ kuṣṭharam avapuṣpam oṣadhidravyaṃ godhūma(l.4)tilamāṣamudgayavanīvāraśyāma iti dhānyāni ca vinyasya madhye hemaṃ yavāsammukhaṃ mayūrābhilakṣaṇam । sa devānāṃ tatvamantreṇa (l.5) skandamūlena vinyaset । apyeṣāmalābhe vajrahemaṃ haritālaṃ sahadevītapaśca sarvagarteṣu vinyaset । sarvatra pāraṃ vā vinyasyā(sya) (l.6) sampūjyāstreṇa saṃprokṣyā(kṣya) varmajaptavāsasācchādya lokapālabaliṃ datvācamya sakalīkṛtya sūtracyuticchidra(l.7)nivāraṇāya skandamūlenā-streṇa śatamardhaṃ tadardhaṃ vā hutvā । vāstudevatā ekaikāhutyā santoṣayet । tadyathā । vāstvadhi(p.90b, f.105a, l.1)pataye brahmaṇe svāha(hā) oṃ sāṃ marīcaye svāhā । oṃ sāṃ vivasvate svāhā oṃ sāṃ mitrāya svāhā oṃ sāṃ dharādharāya svāhā oṃ sām āpāya svāhā (l.2) oṃ sām āpavatsāya svāhā । oṃ sāṃ sāvitre svāhā oṃ sāṃ sāvitrāya svāhā oṃ sām indrāya svāhā oṃ sām indrajayāya svāhā । oṃ sāṃ rudrāya (l.3) svāhā oṃ sāṃ rudradāya svāhā । oṃ sām īśānāya svāhā oṃ sāṃ parjanyāya svāhā oṃ sāṃ jayantāya svāhā oṃ sāṃ (l.4) mahendrāya svāhā oṃ sām ādityāya svāhā oṃ sāṃ satyakāya svāhā oṃ sāṃ bhraṃśakāya svāhā । oṃ sām antarikṣakāya svāhā । (l.5) oṃ sām aryamṇe svāhā । oṃ sāṃ pūṣṇaye svāhā । oṃ sāṃ vitadhāya svāhā oṃ sāṃ gahakṣantāya? svāhā oṃ sāṃ yamāya (l.6) svāhā oṃ sāṃ gandharvāya svāhā oṃ sāṃ bhṛṅgarājāya svāhā oṃ sāṃ mṛgarājāya svāhā oṃ sāṃ niṛṛtaye svāhā oṃ sāṃ dauvārikāya (l.7) svāhā oṃ sāṃ sugrīvāya svāhā oṃ sāṃ puṣpadantāya svāhā oṃ sāṃ varuṇāya svāhā oṃ sām asurāya svāhā oṃ sāṃ (p.91a, l.1) śoṣaṇāya svāhā oṃ sām arogāya svāhā oṃ sāṃ vāyave svāhā oṃ sāṃ nāgāya svāhā oṃ sām asurāya svāhā oṃ sāṃ mukhyāya svāhā oṃ (l.2) sāṃ phallāṭakāya svāhā oṃ sāṃ somāya svāhā oṃ sām ṛgnāya svāhā oṃ sāṃ ādityāya svāhā oṃ sām ālāhāya svāhā oṃ sāṃ dhīrāya svāhā (l.3) oṃ sāṃ varyai svāhā oṃ sāṃ vitāriryai(vidāryai) svāhā oṃ sāṃ pūtanāya svāhā oṃ sāṃ pāparakṣasī svāhā oṃ sandāya svāhā (l.4) oṃ sām aryamṇāya svāhā oṃ sāṃ balipiñchāya svāhā ityādi hutvā

pradhānakuṇḍeṣu - adhvaram anyakuṇḍeṣu yeka(l.5)vāraṃ hutvā jayādirabhyādhānaṃ ca rāṣṭramabhyarcya pūrṇāṃ datvā naivedyatāmbūladhūpadīpādidatvā kṣamasveti vijñāpya (l.6) kuṇḍasthaṃ devaṃ saṃyojya śayanāt bumbamutthāpya dhūpadīpau datvā yajamānaḥ ācāryāya dakṣiṇāṃ dāpayet (l.7) aśvagobhūmi-dānādivastrādyairabhipūjayet । 

uttamaṃ daśaniṣkaṃ tu saptaniṣkaṃ tu madhyamam ।
adhamaṃ pañcaniṣkaṃ tu tra(tri)guṇaṃ cottamottamam ॥ (p.91b, f.106a, l.1)
mūrtipānāṃ caturthāṃśa vedajñaṃ śīlanaṃ samam ।
jāpakāṃ paricārāśca tadarthenaiva toṣayet ॥
bhūridānaṃ tataḥ kuryāt vittaśāḍhyaṃ na kārayet । (l.2)

	paścāddevaṃ ca devyau ca vedaghoṣasarva(rvā)todyavādyaiḥsaha arghyapātrāṇi guruyajamānaśilpināsaha ālayaṃ pradakṣiṇī-kṛtya devābhi(l.3)mukhaṃ pratiṣṭhāpya । aṣṭabandhanaṃ kṛtvā । śilpinaṃ visṛjya । śāntikumbhajalena prokṣya । liṅgamudrayā pīṭhaṃ devau saṃspṛśya । oṃ hrīṃ (l.4) saum icchāśaktiguhābhyāṃ namaḥ । ityevam ityevaṃ(rep.) bubhokṣo mumukṣostu oṃ sauṃ hrāmiti saṃspṛśya । tritattvanyāsam । (l.5) 

śirasi - oṃ sāṃ skandaśivatattvāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ skandaśivatattvādhipataye rudrāya namaḥ । 
hṛdaye – oṃ sāṃ skandavidyātattvāya namaḥ । skanda(l.6)vidyātattvādhipataye viṣṇave namaḥ । 
jānvadhaḥ – oṃ sāṃ skandātmatattvāya namaḥ । skandātmatattvādhipataye brahmaṇe namaḥ । iti vinyasya । (p.7)

pīṭhādhobhāge – ādhārādiśaktiṃ vinyasya । pṛthivītattvādimāyātattvāntānvinyasya । yathā । oṃ sāṃ pṛthivī-tattvāya namaḥ । oṃ sām (p.92a, l.1) aptattvāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ tejastattvāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ vāyutattvāya namaḥ । oṃ sām ākāśatattvāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ (l.2) śabdatattvāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ sparśatattvāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ rūpatattvāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ rasatattvāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ śabdatattvāya namaḥ । oṃ sām upasthatattvāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ pāyu(l.3)tattvāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ pāṇitattvāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ pādatattvāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ vāktattvāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ ghrāṇatattvāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ jihvātattvāya (l.4) namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ cakṣustattvāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ tvaktattvāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ śrotratattvāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ cittattvāya namaḥ । oṃ sām ahaṅkāratattvāya namaḥ । (l.5) oṃ sāṃ buddhitattvāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ manastattvāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ puruṣatattvāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ rāgatattvāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ niyatitattvāya namaḥ । oṃ (l.6) sāṃ vidyātattvāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ kalātattvāya namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ māyātattvāya namaḥ । iti praṇavādinamontena vinyasya oṃ sāṃ skandā(l.7)sanāya namaḥ । iti saṃpūjya । mūrtiṃ sañcintya ।

dehe – oṃ sāṃ skandamūrtaye namaḥ । iti saṃpūjya । (p.92b, f.107a, l.1) devaśirovāṅmananābhiguhyapādeṣu – īśānādijagadbhuvamantrānvinyasya ṣaṇmukhīmudrāṃ darśyā(rśya) (l.2) punapunaretān mantrān vaktrabhaṅgyā ca vinyaset । īśānādiprāgdakṣiṇottarapaścimanaiṛtivaktreṣu sampūjyā(jya) । kalābhaṅgyā skandakalaśān nyāsaṃ (l.3) kuryāt । oṃ sāṃ jagadbhuvaṃ jayāyai namaḥ । īśānamūrdhni । oṃ sāṃ bahuto balāyai namaḥ pūrvamūrdhni । oṃ sāṃ hutamunnadāyai (l.4) namaḥ dakṣiṇamūrdhni vacadbhuvaṃ vāmāyai namaḥ uttaramūrdhni । oṃ sāṃ namaste'stu nandinyai namaḥ paścimamūrdhni oṃ sāṃ viśvabhuve (l.5) vijayāyai namaḥ । naiṛtamūrdhniḥ । oṃ sāṃ jagadbhuvodhipatirjayāyai namaḥ īśānavaktre । om senāni senāyai (l.6) namaḥ pūrvavaktre oṃ sāṃ mayūrapriyo māyā namaḥ dakṣiṇavaktre oṃ sāṃ ṣaḍānanāṅgāya śānte namaḥ । uttaravaktre (l.7) satvayāhaṃ namaste'stu satyāyai namaḥ paścimavaktre । oṃ sāṃ sukhaṃ vahaḥ sūkṣmāyai namaḥ naiṛtavaktre । oṃ sāṃ (p.93a, l.1) subrahmaṇyasumatyai namaḥ hṛdaye । oṃ sāṃ bṛhaspate sutaprajāyai namaḥ । gale oṃ sām āsyapadmayonaye amṛtāyai (l.2) namaḥ । dakṣiṇabāhau oṃ sāṃ yasyātmayaśase namaḥ । vāmabāhau । oṃ sāṃ vahasehnavahantu variṣṭhāyai namaḥ nābhau oṃ sāṃ subrahmaṇya-(l.3)supratiṣṭhāyai namaḥ kukṣau । oṃ sāṃ rudrabhuve rucāyai namaḥ pṛṣṭhe । brahmabhuve brāhmaṇyai namaḥ । urasi । oṃ sāṃ bhuvadbhuve bhūtyai namaḥ (l.4) guhye । oṃ sāṃ mātṛtāya manyave namaḥ । śephe । oṃ sāṃ jagadbhuve jīvitāyai namaḥ dakṣiṇorau । oṃ sāṃ subrahmaṇyaiśuddhāyai namaḥ । (l.5) vāmorau । kṛttikāsutakṛpāyai namaḥ dakṣiṇajānuni । oṃ sāṃ ṣaṣṭhāṅgāya satvarāyai namaḥ vāmajānuni oṃ sāṃ jagadbhuve (l.6) jagatryai namaḥ dakṣiṇajaṅghāyām । oṃ sāṃ vacadbhuve vāmāyai namaḥ vāma-jaṅkhā(ṅghā)yām oṃ sāṃ skandaviśākhine kāmadāyai namaḥ । (l.7) dakṣiṇapāde oṃ sāṃ havyaṃ yasmāt hariṇyai namaḥ vāmapāde oṃ sāṃ ṣaṇmukhaśyāmāyai namaḥ (p.93b, f.108a, subrahmaṇyamātṛkānyāsam, l.1) vyāpakāñjaliṃ nyastvā । tataḥ subrahmaṇyamātṛkāṃ nyāsamācaret ।

(subrahmaṇyamātṛkānyāsam -) tadyathā । 

keśāntamukhavaktre ca netrayo śrotranāsayoḥ ।
gaṇḍayoraṣṭhayo(yoḥ) *dantapṛṣṭhemūrdhnimukhasthirān ॥* (l.2)
kavargaṃ dakṣiṇe haste cavargaṃ vāmahastake ।
ṭavargaṃ dakṣiṇe ūrau tavargaṃ vāma vinyaset ॥
va(pa)phau paścādvayā? *nyasyāt bakāraṃ pṛṣṭhadeśake ।* (l.3)
yakāraṃ hṛdaye nyā(nya)set sahau śigne ca guhyake ॥
bhukāraṃ bhujayornyasyāt kṣakāravyāpakādiṣu । (l.4)

subrahmaṇyamātṛkānyāsaḥ –
am amareśāya namaḥ । mūrdhni ā rum ārādhyāya namaḥ lalāṭe । im indrasutāpriyāya namaḥ dakṣiṇanetre । (l.5) īśā(śa)sutāya namaḥ । vāmanetre । um umāputrāya namaḥ । dakṣiṇakarṇe । ūm ūrdhvaretase namaḥ vāmakarṇe । ṛṃ rudrabhuve namaḥ dakṣiṇanāsāyām ṝṃ (l.6) kumārāya namaḥ vāmanāsāyām ḷṃ lāvaṇyāya namaḥ dakṣiṇagaṇḍe ḹṃ kālakaṇṭhasutāya namaḥ । vāmagaṇḍe । eṃ vallipriyāya namaḥ ūrdhvoṣṭhe aim (p.94a, l.1) aiśvaryapradāya namaḥ adharoṣṭhe om ojasvine namaḥ ūrdhvadantapaṅktau । aum aupamyarahitāya namaḥ adho dantapaṅktau am ambujāsanāya namaḥ (l.2) tālumadhye aḥ akṣiṇi tejase namaḥ bhrūmadhye । kakārādi dakṣiṇahasteṣu vanyasya । kaṃ kamaṇḍaludharāya namaḥ gaṃ (l.3) gaṅgāsutāya namaḥ ghaṃ ghaṇṭahastāya namaḥ ṅaṃ ṅapriśabdajananāya namaḥ iti vinyasya । cakārādi vāmahasteṣu vinyasya । caṃ candraśekharāya namaḥ (l.4) chaṃ chandomayāya namaḥ । jaṃ jagadbhuve namaḥ । jhaṃ jhaṇḍīśāya namaḥ । jñaṃ jñānadāya namaḥ iti vinyasya । ṭakārādi dakṣiṇapādeṣu (l.5) vinyasya । ṭaṃ ṭaṅkahastāya namaḥ । ṭhaṃ ṭhasvarūpāya namaḥ । ḍaṃ ḍambhāya namaḥ । ḍhaṃ ḍhakkapriyāya namaḥ । ṇaṃ ṇagamyāya namaḥ । iti vinyasya । (l.6) takārādi vāmapādeṣu vinyaset । taṃ tattvarūpāya namaḥ thaṃ sthaviṣṭhāya namaḥ daṃ daṇḍapāṇaye namaḥ dhaṃ dhanuṣpāṇaye namaḥ naṃ (p.94b, f.109a, subrahmaṇyakavacam, l.1) nagarandhra-kṛte namaḥ iti vinyasya । dakṣiṇapārśve – paṃ pāśahastāya namaḥ । phaṃ phaṇabhugvāhanāya namaḥ iti vinyasya vāmapārśve baṃ bahulāputrāya namaḥ । (l.2) bhaṃ bhavātmajāya namaḥ । iti vinyasya । maṃ mahāsenāya namaḥ । dakṣiṇapṛṣṭhe । yaṃ yajñamūrtaye namaḥ vāmapṛṣṭhe । kaṭipradeśe raṃ ramaṇīśāya namaḥ । laṃ lambodarā(l.3)nujāya namaḥ । kaṭusūtre vaṃ vacadbhuve namaḥ । kuṇḍalopari śaṃ śaravaṇabhavāya namaḥ ṣaṃ ṣaḍānanāya namaḥ iti vinyaset । saṃ saṃhāramocanāya (l.4) namaḥ । hṛdaye haṃ harātmajāya namaḥ । makuṭe ḻaṃ ḻakuḻīśāya namaḥ meḍhradeśe । kṣaṃ kṣmāmūrtaye namaḥ nābhideśe iti mātṛ(l.5)kalānyāsa(saḥ) samāptaḥ ॥ hariḥ om । 

	skandakumbhadvayaṃ vardhanīṃ ca dvayaṃ ca vidyeśvarakumbhānudvāsya guruḥ ācāryapāṇiḥ tu(stu)tya (l.6) subrahmaṇyasya kavacaṃ japtvā । tadyathāsya śrī subrahmaṇyasya kavacastotramahāmantrasya । bhagavān brahmaṛṣiḥ anuṣṭupchabdaḥ(chandaḥ) śrī subrahmaṇyo devatā hrāṃ bījaṃ hrīṃ śaktiḥ । hrūṃ kīlakaṃ mama sarvābhīṣṭasiddhyarthe subrahmaṇyaprasādasiddhyarthe jape viniyogaḥ । oṃ sām aṅgu(p.95a, l.1)ṣṭhābhyāṃ namaḥ oṃ sīṃ tarjanībhyāṃ namaḥ oṃ sūṃ madhyamābhyāṃ namaḥ oṃ saim anāmikābhyāṃ namaḥ oṃ sauṃ kaniṣṭhikābhyāṃ namaḥ oṃ saḥ karatalakarapṛṣṭhābhyāṃ namaḥ (l.2) oṃ sāṃ hṛdayāya namaḥ । oṃ sīṃ śirase svāhā oṃ sūṃ śikhāyai vauṣaṭ oṃ saiṃ kavacāya hum oṃ sauṃ netratrayāya vauṣaṭ । oṃ saḥ astrāya (l.3) phaṭ । oṃ bhūrbhuvasvaromiti digbandhaḥ । 

dhyānam –
sindūrāruṇamindukāntivadanaṃ keyūrahārādibhiḥ
     divyairābharaṇairvibhūṣitatanuṃ svargasya *saukhyapradam ।* (l.4)
ambhojābhayaśaktikukkuṭadharaṃ raktāṅgarāgāṃśukam
     subrahmaṇyamupāsmahe praṇamatāṃ bhītipraṇāśo'dyatām ॥ (l.5)

subrahmaṇyo'grataḥ pātu senāni pātu pṛṣṭhataḥ ।
umājño dakṣiṇe pātu pāvakī pātu vāmataḥ ॥
śiraḥ pātu mahāsenaṃ *skando rakṣo lalāṭakam ।* (l.6)
netrayo dvādaśākṣaṃ ca śrotre rakṣatu viśvabhuk ॥
mukhaṃ tu ṣaṇmukhaḥ pātu nāsikāṃ śaṅkarātmajaḥ । (l.7)
oṣṭhau vallīpatiḥ pātu jihvāṃ pātu ṣaḍānanaḥ ॥
devasenāpatirdantāścubukaṃ bāhulāsutaḥ ।
kaṇṭhaṃ tārakajit pātu *bāhu dvādaśabāhumān ॥* (p.95b, f.120a, l.1)
hastau śaktidharaḥ pātu vakṣaḥ pātu śirodbhavaḥ ।
hṛdayaṃ brahmabhuk pātu kukṣiṃ pātvambikāsutaḥ ॥
ūrū pātu *gajārūḍho jānudve jāhnavīsutaḥ ।* (l.2)
jaṅghe viśākhajaḥ pātu pādayoḥ śikhivāhanaḥ ॥
sarvāṇyaṅgāni bhūteśa saptadhātuśca pāvakī ।
sandhyākāle *niśīdhanyāṃ madhyāhne dustare jale ॥* (l.3)
durgame ca mahāraṇye rājadvāre sahāyake ।
tumule raṇamadhye ca sarpadaṣṭamṛgādiṣu ।
corādi*bhayabhedye ca jvarādivyādhipīḍane ॥* (l.4)
duṣṭagrahādibhīte ca durnimittādibhīṣaṇe ।
astraśastranikhāte ca trātu māṃ krauñcarandhrakṛt ॥ (l.5)
subrahmaṇyaṃ ca kavaca iṣṭasiddhipradaṃ paṭhet ।
tasya tāpatrayaṃ nāsti satyaṃ satyaṃ vadāmbayam ॥
tārakāsurahantāraṃ mayūravaravāhanam । (l.6)
devasenāpatiṃ devaṃ skandaṃ vande jagadgurum ॥

	oṃ sāṃ śaravaṇabhavāya ṣaṇmukhāya kumārāya kukkuṭadhvajāya (l.7) huṃ phaṭ svāhā । kukkuṭadhvajāya vidmahe vajrahastāya dhīmahi tanno skanadaḥ pracodayāt । evaṃ japtvā । kumbhamudrāṃ pradarśya । (p.96a, antarbalibahirbali, l.1) kumbhādbījamuddhṛtya tārakākāraṃ sañcintya । saṃhāramudrayādāya āvāhanamudrayā hṛtpradeśe saṃsthāpya । prāṇapratiṣṭhāṃ kṛtvā nirudhya । (l.2) aṅgāni sva sva sthāne vinyasya śaktidvayamevaṃ stanapradeśe saṃsthāpyābhyarcya । pādyācamanārghyaṃ puṣpāṇi hṛdayena datvā dhūpadīpau datvā kṣamasvevi (l.3) vijñāpya । kuṇḍasamīpaṃ gatvā kuṇḍādhidevatān sampūjya pariṣicya । vidhisampādanārthaṃ chidrakāraṇārthaṃ pradhānakuṇḍe (l.4) skandamūlena pūrvādidikṣu - skandāstreṇāghorāstrapāśupatāstrakṣurikāstrāṇi । āgneyādiṣu – mālāmantraṃ skandagāyatrī (l.5) para iti oṃ namo bhagavate mahāpuruṣāya iti mantreṇa yathāśaktiṃ hutvā prāyaścittārthaṃ skandāstreṇa śataṃ (l.6) hutvā kṣamasveti guhaśirasā pūrṇāṃ datvā namaskṛtya । brahmodvāsanaṃ paridhipraharaṇaṃ ca kṛtvā antarbali-bahirbaliṃ ca kṛtvā । (l.7) svadakṣiṇadigbhāge maṇḍaladvayaṃ niṣpādya prathamamaṇḍale । pūrvādyuttarāntadikṣu । oṃ sāṃ rudrebhyo namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ mātṛbhyo namaḥ । (p.96b, f.121a, l.1) oṃ sāṃ gaṇebhyo namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ yajñebhyo namaḥ । īśānādikoṇeṣu oṃ sāṃ grahebhyo namaḥ । oṃ sām asurebhyo namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ rākṣasebhyo (l.2) namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ rāgebhyo namaḥ । maṇḍalamadhye īśānāgneyanaiṛteṣu oṃ sāṃ kṣetrebhyo namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ rāśibhyo namaḥ । oṃ sāṃ (l.3) viśvebhyo namaḥ । vāyuvaruṇayormadhye । oṃ sāṃ kṣetrapālāya namaḥ । ityabhyarcya । svāhāntamarghyaṃ datvā । dvitīyamaṇḍale pūrvādīśānta-dikṣu (l.4) oṃ sām indrāya svāhā । oṃ sām īśānāya svāhā । aiśānyāṃ brahmaṇe svāhā । naiṛtyām oṃ sāṃ viṣṇave svāhā । madhye oṃ (l.5) sāṃ nīlalohitāya svāhā । iti saṃpūjyārghyaṃ datvā । pūrvaṃ rudrādibhyo baliṃ svāhāntaṃ kṣiptvā-camanaṃ datvā indrādibhyo (l.6) tadhaiva balyācamanaṃ datvā । kṣamasveti vijñāpya । lokapāladevatān udvāsya । devasammukhaṃ gatvā । pañcāmṛtakalaśenā(l.7)ṣṭottaraśatakalaśena pañcaviṃśatikalaśena pañcagavyakalaśena saṃsnāpya । gandhādinā sarvato lipya । vastrābharaṇayajñopavītādyai(p.97a, l.1)ralaṅkṛtyā(tya) । dehe layāṅgaṃ sampūjya bhogāṅgaṃ pīṭhadalamūle dalāgre kaṇṭha-pīṭhādhobhāgeṣu sampūjya । arghyaṃ datvā dhenupadma(l.2)triśūlamakarasaṃnamaskāramudrāṃ ca kṛtvā । arghyaitvā dhūpadīpau datvā devasya nāmakaraṇaṃ kṛtvā । candrārkatārakaṃ yāvat mantraiśca mūrtipaiḥ saha (l.3) svecchayā kṛtayā nātha sthātavyamiha mandire iti vijñāpya । yajamānaavakāśāt prabhūtahavyanivedyatāmbūladhūpadīpanīrāñjana(l.4)bhasmadarpaṇachatracāmarādi datvā ।

maghe'smin karmavākkāyamanoguṇatame prabho ।
nyūnaṃ vāpyadhikaṃ vāpi kṣamasva *kṛpayā iha ॥* (l.5)
	iti vijñāpyā(pya) sāṣṭāṅgaṃ namaskṛtya ।

pratiṣṭhākāle yathā tuṣṭo gurustasmāt guhastathā ।
mokṣacā(yā) *labhate mokṣaṃ bhogān kāmān viśeṣataḥ ॥* (l.6)

॥ iti kumāratantre subrahmaṇyapratiṣṭhāvidhiḥ samāptaḥ ॥ śrīdakṣiṇāmūrtigurave namaḥ ॥ śubhamastu ॥ (p.97b, f.122, vighneśvaramaṇṭapapūjāvidhi(dhiḥ), l.1)

(vighneśvaramaṇṭapapūjāvidhiḥ -)
praṇamya vighnarājasya maṇṭapārādhanakramam ।
candrajñānoktamārgeṇa vakṣyāmi śruṇu pārvatī ॥ śivaḥ ॥

	atha yathoktalakṣaṇācāryaḥ । vihitakāladeśa(l.2)vihitadravyāṇi samādāya yathoktalakṣaṇaṃ maṇṭapaṃ vidhāya nityāgnikadvayaḥ । parityaktabhojanam । yajñadhāmamalaṅkṛtya pitṛprasūnādiviśeṣa(l.3)sandhyāṃ kṛtvā indrānalamadhyakumbhe śivasūrya(ryaṃ) nityavadabhyarcya । visṛjyeva samācamya sakalīkṛtya । puṇyāhavācana(l.4)pūrvaṃ praṇavārghyaṃ saṅkalpya । pūrvadvāramastreṇa saṃprokṣya । oṃ gāṃ śāntikalādvārāya namaḥ ।

raktavarṇā caturbāhu *mahābalasamanvita ।* (l.5)
padmākṣamālāmabhayadhvajaḥ sarpopavītika ॥

	iti dhyātvā । iti dhyātvā (rep.), āsanamūrtyādidhūpadīpa(l.6)naivedyajapāntaṃ kṛtvā । oṃ hāṃ śāntitoraṇāya namaḥ । iti saṃpūjya । dakṣiṇe । oṃ gāṃ vidyākalādvārāya namaḥ । 

trivaktrā *kṛṣṇavarṇā ca kapardendu caturbhuja ।* (l.7)
abhayāmakṣamālābjā śaktyā vidyāgnisannibha ॥

	iti dhyātvāsanamūrtyādinaivedya (p.98a, l.1) japāntaṃ kṛtv । oṃ gāṃ vidyātoraṇāya namaḥ । iti saṃpūjya । uttare – oṃ gāṃ pratiṣṭhākalādvārāya namaḥ । 

ekavaktrā caturbāhu śuddhasphaṭikanirmala(lā) ।
abjābhayākṣamālā ca pāśaratnādibhūṣita ॥

	iti dhyātvāsanamūrtyādidhūpadīpanaivedyajapāntaṃ kṛtvā oṃ gāṃ pratiṣṭhātoraṇāya nama iti saṃpūjya । (l.3) paścime – oṃ gāṃ nivṛttikalādvārāya namaḥ । 

taptakāñcanavarṇābha caturvaktrendumaulika ।
dambholyāmakṣamālābjāni nivṛtyāma*bhayaprada ॥* (l.4)

	iti dhyātvāsanamūrtyādidhūpadīpanaivedyajapāntaṃ kṛtvā । oṃ gāṃ nivṛttitoraṇāya namaḥ । ityabhyarcya । tathaiva (l.5) tacchākhayo(yoḥ) sadakṣiṇavāmasaṃsthayoḥ pūrvādārabhya – 

caturbāhvekavaktrī ca raktavarṇo mahābali ।
triśūlī cākṣasūtrī *ca abjābhayavarānvitā ॥* (l.6)

	iti dhyātvā । oṃ gāṃ nandine namaḥ । ityāsanamūrtyādijapāntaṃ kṛtvā । āgneye –

kṛṣṇāṅīgā ca *triṇetrī ca babhrabhrūśmaśrurūrdhvagaḥ ।* (l.7)
khaḍgakheṭakamuṇḍī ca mahākālo triśūladhṛk ॥

	iti dhyātvā । oṃ gāṃ mahākālāya nama (p.98b, f.123a, l.1) ityāsanamūrtyādijapāntaṃ saṃpūjya । yāmye – 

śveto daṇḍākṣasūtrī ca bhṛṅginī māṃsavigrahaḥ । 
triṇetraḥ saśikho nṛtyo śivadhyānaparāyaṇa ॥

	iti dhyātvā । (l.2) oṃ gāṃ bhṛṅgiṇe namaḥ ityāsanamūrtyādidhūpadīpanaivedyajapāntaṃ kuryāt । naiṛte – 

raktapadmāsanāsīno ekavaktrākṣasūtradhṛk । (l.3)
triṇetro hemavarṇābho jayanto rudradāsakaḥ ॥

	iti dhyātvā । oṃ gāṃ jayantāya namaḥ । ityāsanamūrtyādidhūpadīpanaivedyajapantaṃ (l.4) kṛtvā । vāruṇe –

tryakṣo dharmasvarūpī ca vṛṣasphaṭikavigrahaḥ ।
svānto rudraikabhāvatvo śaraṇāgatavatsala ॥

	iti smaran । (l.5) oṃ gāṃ vṛṣabhāya nama ityāsanamūrtyādidhūpadīpanaivedyajapāntaṃ samarpya । vāyavye – 

kanakābho caturbāhu skandaḥ *kaumārarūpadhṛk ।* (l.6)
kakkuṭaśśaktihastaśca varadānabhayapradaḥ ॥

	iti dhyātvā । oṃ gāṃ skandāya namaḥ ityāsanamūrtyādidhūpadīpa(l.7)naivedyajapāntaṃ kṛtvā । uttare – 

dvārasyābhimukhī śyāmaratnapuṣpaughavarṣaṇī ।
bhujadvayākṣamālābjā dhāriṇī indirāya ca ॥ (p.99a, l.1) 

	iti dhyātvā । oṃ gaṃ mahālakṣmyai namaḥ । ityāsanamūrtyādidhūpadīpanaivedyajapāntaṃ samarpya । aiśānyām –

caturbāhuṃ caturvaktraṃ triṇetraṃ caṇḍikeśvaram । (l.2)
bibhrat kamaṇḍaluṃ śūlamakṣasūtraṃ paraśvadham ॥

	evaṃ dhyātvā । oṃ gāṃ caṇḍeśvarāya namaḥ । ityāsanamūrtyādijapāntaṃ kṛtvā । tadbahiḥ pūrvādīśāntam – (l.3)

dādhiśaṅkhatuṣārābhaṃ kṣorodārṇavasambhavam ।
namamāmi satataṃ somaṃ śambhormakuṭabhūṣamam ॥

	iti dhyātvā । oṃ gāṃ somāya nama ityāsana(l.4)mūrtyādijapāntaṃ kṛtvā । 

dharaṇīgarbhasambhūtaṃ vidyutkañcanasannibham ।
kumāraṃ śaktihastaṃ ca maṅgalaṃ praṇamāmyaham ॥ (l.5)

	iti dhyātvā । om aṅgārakāya namaḥ । ityāsanamūrtyādijapāntaṃ kṛtvā । 

priyaṅgukalikābhāsā pratimambudhamavyayam ।
saumyaṃ *sarvaguṇopetaṃ namāmi śaśinandanam ॥* (l.6)

	iti dhyātvā । oṃ gāṃ budhāya nama ityāsanamūrtyādijapāntaṃ kṛtvā ।

devānāṃ ca *ṛṣīṇāṃ ca guruṃ kāñcanasannibham ।* (l.7)
vandanīyaṃ trilokeśca(śa) taṃ namāmi bṛhaspatim ॥ 

	iti dhyātvā । oṃ gāṃ bṛhaspataye namaḥ । ityāsanamūrtyādi(p.99b, f.124a, lokapālau பூசை, ஸாமிமண்டபபூஜைலே வாஶீ, l.1)japāntaṃ kṛtvā ।

himakundamṛṇālābhaṃ daityānāṃ paramaṃ gurum ।
sarvaśāstrapravaktāraṃ bhārgavaṃ praṇamāmyaham ॥

	iti dhyātvā । oṃ gāṃ śukrāya namaḥ । (l.2) ityāsanamūrtyādijapāntaṃ kṛtvā ।

nīlāñjanamahābhāsaṃ sūryasūnuṃ mahograkam ।
chāyāyā garbhasaṃbhūtaṃ praṇamāmi śanaiścaram ॥ 

	iti dhyātvā । (l.3) oṃ gāṃ śanaiścarāya namaḥ । ityāsanamūrtyādijapāntaṃ kṛtvā । 

ardhakāyaṃ mahāvīryaṃ candrādityavirodhinam ।
siṃhikāgarbha*sambhūtaṃ taṃ rāhuṃ praṇamāmyaham ॥* (l.4)

	iti dhyātvā । oṃ gāṃ rāhave namaḥ । ityāsanamūrtyādijapāntaṃ kṛtvā ।

palāladhūmasaṃkāśaṃ *tārakāgrahastakān ।* (l.5)
rudrātmakān ghorāntān ketūn praṇamāmyaham ॥

	iti dhyātvā । oṃ gāṃ ketubhyāṃ namaḥ । ityāsanamūrtyādi(l.6)dhūpadīpanaivedyajapāntaṃ kṛtvā । tadadhaḥ । oṃ gām indrāya namaḥ । aiśānyāya namaḥ । īśāne – brahmaṇe namaḥ । naiṛte – viṣṇave namaḥ । (p.100a, l.1) iti sampūjya । paścime nityoktavat dvārapālānārādhya । tadyathā । praṇavārghyeṇa dvāramastreṇa saṃprokṣya । 

oṃ kumbhaṃ kāñcanavarṇābhaṃ pradyodita*digantaram ।* (l.2)
dauvārikaṃ gaṇeśasya uttarābhimukhaṃ sadā ॥

	iti dhyātvā । oṃ gāṃ kumbhāya namaḥ । ityabhyarcya । 

nikumbhaṃ vāmanākāraṃ nīlāmbuda*samaprabham ।* (l.3)
khaḍgakheṭakahastau ca dakṣiṇābhimukhaṃ bhavet ॥

	iti dhyātvā । tayormadhye – 

kumbhodaraṃ viśālākṣaṃ mahoragopa*vītakam ।* (l.4)
pāśāṅkuśadharaṃ śubhradevālokanatatparam ॥

	iti dhyātvā । oṃ gāṃ kumbhodarāya nama ityārādhya । śākhayoḥ । 

caṇḍaṃ *śubhraṃ viśālasya mabjābhayakaradvayam ।* (l.5)
mauniṃ paramaśobhāḍhyaṃ śāstāraṃ gaṇasainyakam ॥

	iti dhyātvā । oṃ gāṃ caṇḍāya namaḥ । (l.6) ityabhyarcya ।

āmodaṃ mauktikābhāsaṃ śaktimudgaradhāriṇam ।
ekāsyaṃ ca tṛ(tri)ṇetrāḍhyam auṇapadhyānatatparam ॥

	iti dhyātvā । oṃ gām (l.7) āmodāya namaḥ । iti saṃpūjya । 

pramodaṃ gauravarṇābhaṃ gadāhastaṃ bhayānakam ।
bhaktyā paravaśaṃ nityaṃ śucismitamukhaṃ bhavet ॥

	iti (p.100b, f.125a, l.1) dhyātvā । oṃ gāṃ pramodāya nama ityārādhya ।

sumukhaṃ ca mahāprājñaṃ sarvalakṣaṇalakṣitam ।
nāgopavītinaṃ śāntaṃ śaktitomaradhāriṇam ॥

	iti dhyātvā । (l.2) oṃ gāṃ sumukhāya namaḥ । iti saṃpūjya । 

durmukhaṃ raktavarṇāṅgaṃ ḍamaruṃ pāśadhāriṇam ।
īpsitārthapradaṃ vīraṃ samarāṅkaṇakaṅkaśam ॥

	iti dhyātvā । oṃ gāṃ durmukhāya (l.3) namaḥ । ityabhyarcya । vighnānutsārya । dvārāṇyanyāstreṇa saṅkhaṭya । paścimadvāreṇāntaḥpraviśyāstramudumbare rakṣāyai saṃpūjyā(l.4)valokitayāgopakaraṇadravyajātaṃ prātaḥ pradakṣiṇaṃ gatvā । naiṛte – 

haṃsārūḍhaṃ caturvaktraṃ vāstubrahmāṇamavyayam । (l.5)
sṛgdaṇḍākṣakuśopetaṃ sarvālaṅkārasaṃyutam ॥

	iti dhyātvā । oṃ gāṃ vāstvadhipataye brahmaṇe nama ityārādhyā(l.6)straprākārakavacāvakuṇṭhanābhyāṃ yāgagṛhaṃ saṃrakṣya । brahmasthāddakṣiṇataḥ । kṛṣṇājinādau saumyāsyopaviśyā(l.7)camya vibhūtidhāraṇaṃ kṛtvā । karanyāsaṃ vidhāya । nityavaddhāraṇādibhirdehaṃ viśoddhyātmānamvāhya hṛdyāsanamūrtiṃ vinyasya (p.101a, l.1) pūrvavaddaṇḍabhaṅgyā brahmāṇi vinyasya ūrdhvapūrvadakṣiṇottarapaścimavaktreṣu । vaktrabhaṅgyā ca vinyasettataḥ । kalābhaṅgyā ca । īśānaḥ sarvavidyānāṃ śaśinyai (l.2) namaḥ । ūrdhvamūrdhni । oṃ homīśvaraḥ sarvabhūtānām aṅgadāyai namaḥ । pūrvamūrdhni । oṃ hoṃ brahmādhipati brahmaṇo'dhipati brahmeṣṭhāyai namaḥ । (l.3) dakṣiṇamūrdhni । oṃ hoṃ śivo me astu marīcyai namaḥ । uttaramūrdhni । oṃ hoṃ sadāśive jvālinyai namaḥ । paścimamūrdhni । evamīśāna(l.4)kalāḥ pañcakam । oṃ hom avyaktakalāyai namaḥ । ūrdhvavaktre oṃ heṃ tatpuruṣāya vidmahe śāntyai namaḥ । pūrvavaktre । oṃ (l.5) heṃ mahādevāya dhīmahi vidyāyai namaḥ dakṣiṇavaktre । oṃ heṃ tanno rudrapratiṣṭhāyai namaḥ uttaravaktre । oṃ heṃ pracodayā(l.6)nnivṛtyai namaḥ paścimavaktre । iti tatpuruṣakalāścatasraḥ । oṃ hum aghorebhyastamāyai namaḥ grīvāyām । oṃ hum (l.7) aghorarakṣāyai namaḥ । dakṣiṇāṃse । oṃ ham aghoratarebhyo niṣṭhāyai namaḥ vāmāṃse । oṃ huṃ śarvataḥ śarvamṛtyave namaḥ nābhau । (p.101b, f.126a, l.1) oṃ huṃ śarvebhyo māyāyai namaḥ jaṭhare । namaste'stu rudrabhayāyai namaḥ pṛṣṭhe । oṃ huṃ rūpebhyo jarāyai namaḥ urasi । iti bahurūpakalāṣṭakam । (l.2) oṃ hiṃ vāmadevāya namo rajāyai namaḥ guhye । oṃ hiṃ jyeṣṭhāya namo rakṣāyai namaḥ liṅge । oṃ hiṃ rudrāya namo ratyai namaḥ dakṣiṇorau । oṃ (l.3) hiṃ kalāya namo phālyai namaḥ vāmorau । oṃ hiṃ kalakāmāyai namaḥ dakṣiṇajānuni । oṃ hiṃ vikaraṇāya namaḥ saṃyaminyai (l.4) namaḥ vāmajānuni । oṃ hiṃ balakriyāyai namaḥ dakṣiṇajaṅghāyām । oṃ hiṃ vikaraṇāya namo buddhyai namaḥ vāmajaṅghāyām । (l.5) oṃ hiṃ balakriyāyai namaḥ dakṣiṇasphiṭi । oṃ hiṃ pramathanāya namo dhātre namaḥ vāmasphici । oṃ hiṃ sarvabhūtadamanāya namo brahmiṇyai (l.6) namaḥ kaṭyām । oṃ hiṃ manomohinyai namaḥ dakṣiṇapārśve । oṃ hiṃ unmanāya namo bhayāyai namaḥ vāmapapārśve iti vāmadeva(l.7)kalāstrayodaśa । oṃ hiṃ sadyojātaṃ prapadyāmi siddhyai namaḥ dakṣiṇapāde । oṃ haṃ sadyojātāya vai namo ṛddhyai namaḥ vāmapāde । (p.102a, l.1) oṃ haṃ bhavedyutyai namaḥ dakṣimapāṇau । oṃ haṃ bhavellakṣmyai namaḥ vāmapāṇau । oṃ ham anādibhavenmedhāyai namaḥ nāsāyām । oṃ haṃ (l.2) bhajasva māṃ kāntyai namaḥ śirasi । oṃ haṃ bhava svadhāyai namaḥ dakṣiṇabāhau । oṃ ham udbhavāya namo sthityai namaḥ vāmabāhau । iti sadyojāta-(l.3)kalāṣṭakam । evaṃ sva sva mudrābhiraṣṭatriṃśatkalānyāsaṃ vidhāya । vidyādehamāpādya । netranyāsānantaraṃ mūlamāvāhyā-ṅgāni (l.4) yathāsthānaṃ vinyasya । sakalīkuryāt । tato vihitāntaryajanahavanadhyānatālatrayādinā dhāma saṃrakṣya । (l.5) viśeṣārghyaṃ saṅkalpya । tajjalena svaśiraḥ saṃprokṣya । sakuśena yāgopakaraṇadravyajātamastreṇa saṃprokṣyāmṛtī (l.6) kuryāt । tataścandanena lalāṭe tilakaṃ kṛtvātmānamārādhya । mantraśuddhiṃ vidhāyātmani sāsanaṃ sabrahmāṅgaṃ sakalaṃ śivaṃ (l.7) vinyasya । sṛṣṭimārgeṇa śodhyātmānaṃ kalākhyaṃ vinyasettadyathā । oṃ hauṃ śāntyatītakalāyai namaḥ । oṃ haiṃ śāntikalāyai (p.102b, f.127a, l.1) namaḥ । oṃ hrūṃ vidyākalāyai namaḥ । oṃ hiṃ pratiṣṭhākalāyai namaḥ । oṃ hlāṃ nivṛttikalāyai namaḥ । brahmarandhralalāṭāntaṃ tasmā(smād) grīvāvadhiṃ tataḥ । (l.2) nābhyantajānuparyantaṃ pādāntaṃ kramaśaḥ kalā iti tadvyāpakaṃ niṣkalaṃ śivamāviṣkṛtyāṅganyāsāvakuṇṭhanādikaṃ vidhāyātmānaṃ gandhādyuṣṇī(l.3)ṣāntaiḥ prasādhanairalaṅkṛtya । gaṇapatihastaṃ kuryāt । tato dakṣahastatale vāmāṅguṣṭhena gandhalepite āsanamūrtimadhye (l.4) vinyasyāṅguṣṭhādiṣvīśānādipañcakaṃ vinyasya । vidyādehanetranyāsau vidhāya gaṇapatimāvāhyāṅgāni (l.5) kaniṣṭhādiṣu vinyasya । paramīkṛtya । svaśirasyadhiropya । brahmarandhrapraviṣṭena tejasāvāhyābhyantaraṃ tamaḥpaṭalamādhūya (l.6) pradyoditadigantaraṃ saṃbhāvya । jñānakhaḍgaṃ saptadhā-strajaptamādāya maṇṭale karmāṇaṃ sākṣitvena kalaśe yajñarakṣakatvena (p.103a, l.1) vahnau homādhikaraṇatvena sthitaḥ sa evāhamiti samāropitaśivādvayaḥ pañcagavyaṃ sādhayet । pañcagavyaṃ vidhivadvidhāya । tataḥ lājacandanasiddhārtha(l.2)bhasma-puṣpakuśākṣatān saptadhā śuddhalājān vikirānastreṇa saptadhābhimantrya naiṛtyāṃ sthitvā pañcagavyārghyajalairastreṇa yāgagṛhaṃ (l.3) saṃprokṣya । kuśakūrcyā vardhanyāsanāyai īśānakoṇavedikāyāṃ samāhṛtya । naiṛte vāstunāthaṃ saṃpūjya । 

tundilaṃ *gaurahaṃsasthaṃ caturāsyaṃ caturbhujam ।* (l.4)
vāmadaṇḍākṣasūtraṃ ca dakṣiṇe sṛkkamaṇḍalum ॥

	oṃ hāṃ vāstvadhipataye brahmaṇe (l.5) namaḥ ityabhyarcya । paścime oṃ hāṃ mahālakṣmyai namaḥ ityabhyarcya । tata-ścaiśānyāṃ śivakumbhāstravardhanyau calācalasanaṃ (l.6) vṛṣabhāsane ādhārādyanantadharmajñānavairāgyaiśvaryapaṅkajaiḥ saṃpūjya । tasmin sāṅgaṃ bhagavantaṃ śivam āvaraṇarodharahitaṃ (p.103b, f.128a, l.1) saṃpūjya । taddakṣiṇe । vardhanyāṃ paścimā-bhimukhāṃ siṃhāsanādhirūḍhāṃ sāsanaṃ pāśupatāstramāvāhya sakalīkṛtya saṃpūjya । maṇṭapābhyantare (l.2) lokapālān vāhanāyudhayuktāṃ dhyātvā । prācyādidikṣu – oṃ hām indrāya namaḥ । īśāne – brahmaṇe namaḥ । naiṛte – viṣṇave namaḥ । iti saṃpūjya । bho bho (l.3) śakra tvayā sasyāṃ diśi vighnapraśāntaye । sāvadhānena yāgāntaṃ yāva(vat)stheyaṃ gaṇapatyājñaye-tyanena krameṇa sarvān (l.4) lokapālān gaṇeśājñāṃ śrāvayet । tadanu । pūrvaṃ vardhanīmādāya । tajjalādhārānugaṃ śivakumbham oṃ namaḥ sarvātmakāyetyuktvā । (l.5) mūlamantrāstradurgamanusmaran īśānādīśānāntaṃ bhrāmaitvā । puna(naḥ) svasthāne pūrvaṃ kumbhaṃ paścādvardhanīṃ ca (l.6) saṃsthāpya । sthirāsanaṃ saṃpūjya । śivamūlenāvāhyāṅgāni vinyasya । saṃpūjya । vardhanyāmastrama-rcayet । oṃ hām astrāsanāya huṃ phaṇṇamaḥ । (p.104a, l.1) oṃ hām astramūrtaye huṃ phaṇṇamaḥ ।

sūryakoṭipratīkāśaṃ pralayāmbudanisvanam ।
tryakṣaṃ nāgopavītaṃ ca caturvaktraṃ caturbhujam ॥
pradīpta*daśanaprāntaṃ dīptabhrūśmaśrumūrdhvajam ।* (l.2)
śaktimudgaraśūlāsihastaṃ candrārdhaśekharam ॥

	evaṃ dhyātvā । oṃ hāṃ ślīṃ paṃ śum astrāya huṃ phaṇṇamaḥ । (l.3) ityāvāhya । oṃ hāṃ hṛdayāya huṃ phaṇṇamaḥ । oṃ hāṃ ślīṃ śirase huṃ phaṇṇamaḥ । oṃ hāṃ pāṃ śikhāyai huṃ phaṇṇamaḥ । oṃ hāṃ śuṃ kavacāya huṃ (l.4) phaṇṇamaḥ । oṃ hāṃ netrebhyo huṃ phaṇṇamaḥ । oṃ hāṃ huṃphaḍantāstrāya huṃ phaṇṇama ityaṅgaiḥ sakalīkṛtya tau gandhādibhiḥ (l.5) sāvaraṇau sarvopacāraiḥ saṃpūjya । oṃ bhrūṃ śivaśaktibhyāṃ nama iti liṅgamudrayāṅguṣṭhena kumbhaṃ spṛśya muṣṭyā (l.6) vardhanīṃ spṛśet ।

umāyai bhagarūpiṇyai liṅgarūpadharāya ca ।
śaṅkarāya namastubhyamiti stutvānumodya ca ॥
yajñasyāsya patiḥ tvaṃ *hi mūrtireṣā tavācalā(la) ।* (p.104b, f.129a, l.1)
eṣa te jñānanistṛṃ(striṃ)śat tatgṛhāṇaḥ(ṇa) tvamāyudham ॥

	iti jñānakhaḍgaṃ samarpya mūlenāṣṭottaraśataṃ japtvā । nivedya । (l.2) taddaśāṃśato vardhanyāmastraṃ japtvā nivedya । māyāpravṛtti tacchāyaṃ yajvanā bhavataḥ kratuḥ rakṣaṇīyaḥ tvayā tarhi samāptiryāvadasya hi । iti rakṣāṃ (l.3) vijñāpya nirodhārghyaṃ datvā । vikiroddhāstrairyāgagṛhamapasāritā śeṣavighnaṃ samantāt bhāvayet । vāyavye । (l.4) vighnavināyakamabhyarcayet । 

yatsureśādhvaro'smābhiḥ paścā(ścāt) anugrahahetave । 
pravartitaprayātvaṃ tam avighnaṃ tava sannidhau ॥ (l.5)

	iti vijñāpya । aiśānyāṃ gurūṃ(ruṃ) dṛṣṭvādhyeṣya । brahmasthāne sarvatobhadrādyanyatamamaṇḍale kumbhe vārcayet parama(l.6)kāraṇamadhvarapatim ।

vighneśaṃ sāsanaṃ śodhyādhvanyāsapūrvamāvāhya । 
śāntyatītakalāmūrdhvaṃ śāntivaktrasaroruham ॥
vidyāvatata-*vakṣo'haṃ pratiṣṭhāguhyamaṇḍalam ।* (p.105a, pūrvādyaṣṭakalaśapūjai, l.1)
nivṛttijānujaṅghāṅghriṃ bhuvanādhvā tanūruham ॥
varṇatvaṃ mantrarudhiraṃ padamāṃsasiktavirājitam ।
tattvāsthimajjāśuklādidhātu*sadbhogamokṣadam ॥* (l.2)
śivāntatattvagarbhāya vyāpinaṃ sarvadā śivam ।

	evaṃ dhyātvā । nityoktavallayāṅgaparyantamabhyarcya । tadyathā । oṃ gaṃ gaṇapa(l.3)tyāsanāya namaḥ । oṃ gaṃ gaṇapatimūrtaye namaḥ । oṃ gaṃ gaṇapataye namaḥ । oṃ kuṅkumavarṇasvarūpam । iti dhyātvā । saṃpūjyārghyaṃ (l.4) datvā । pūrvādyaṣṭadigghaṭeṣu । pūrve – 

varadaṃ sarvaśobhāḍhyaṃ muktāmauktikabhūṣaṇam ।
nāgapāśadharaṃ tryakṣaṃ bhaktābhīṣṭaphalapradam ॥ (l.5)

	iti dhyātvāvāhya । saṃpūjyārghyaṃ datvā । āgneye –
varānanajaṭājūṭamadhyacandravibhūṣitam ।
nāgayajñopavītāḍhyamāhvārūḍhaṃ *caturbhujam ॥* (l.6)

	iti dhyātvāvāhyābhyarcyārghyaitvā । yāmye –

vighneśaṃ ca mahākāyaṃ candrārghakṛtaśekharam ।
bālasūryaprabhaṃ devam *abhayaṃ ca caturbhujam ॥* (l.7)

	iti dhyātvāvāhya(hyā)bhyarcyārghyaṃ datvā । naiṛte –

gajānanaṃ viśālākṣaṃ nīlameghadyutipradam ।
dantaṃ pāśāṅkuśa*muñci(mauñji)dhāriṇaṃ viśvavandinam ॥* (p.105b, f.130a, l.1)

	iti dhyātvāvāhyābhyarcyārghyaṃ datvā । vāruṇe – 

bhaktapriyamumāsūnuṃ lamboṣṭhaṃ vikaṭotkaṭam ।
mauñjīkṛṣṇājinadharaṃ *vande dantāvalaṃ vibhum ॥* (l.2)

	iti dhyātvāvāhyābhyarcyārghyaṃ datvā । vāyavye –

gajakarṇaṃ viśālākṣaṃ bālacandrakalādharam ।
anādibrahmacārīyaṃ koṭyarka*samatejasam ॥* (l.3)

	iti dhyātvāvāhyābhyarcyārghyaṃ datvā । uttare –

vināyakaṃ namastubhyaṃ bālenduśakalādharam ।
nāgayajñopavītāḍhyaṃ *pāśāṅkuśadharaṃ bhaje ॥* (l.4)

	iti dhyātvāvāhyābhyarcyārghyaitvā । īśāne –

lambodaraṃ hastimukhaṃ pakvamāmraphalādinam ।
tryakṣaṃ *nāgākṣasūtrī ca abhayaṃ caturbhujam ॥* (l.5)

	iti dhyātvāvāhyābhyarcyārghyaitvā । tadvidikṣu । aṣṭavidyeśvarānāgneyādiṣu –

viśvarūpaṃ *jagannātham abhayaṃ ca caturbhujam ।* (l.6)
triṇetraṃ vāmanākāramīpsitārthaphalapradam ॥ 	iti dhyātvāvāhayet । 

oṃ viśālākṣa triṇetraṃ ca jaṭā*makuṭamaṇḍalam ।* (l.7)
prasannavadanaṃ śāntaṃ ḍamaruṃ pāśadhāriṇaṇ ॥ iti dhyātvāvāhayet । 
akṣayaṃ sarvaśobhāḍhyaṃ triṇetraṃ ca gajānanam । (p.106a, l.1)
kapitthaphalabhakṣyaṃ ca suprasannam umāsutam ॥ 	iti yajet । om ।

mandavibhramamānandaṃ kapoladyutivigraham ।
dantaṃ pāśāṅkuśakaraṃ nīlameghadyutiṃ bhaje ॥ 

	iti (l.2) dhyātvāvāhyābhyarcyārghyaṃ datvā । 

unmattaṃ kāñcananibhaṃ triśūlābhayapāṇinam ।
stūyamānaṃ mahāvīryaṃ bālacandrakalādharam ॥ 	iti yajet । om ।

lalitaṃ śubhra*varṇāṅgaṃ paraśuṃ khaḍgadhāriṇam ।* (l.3)
vajradamṣṭrākarālaṃ ca taptakāñcanasannibham ॥ 

	iti dhyātvāvāhya(hyā)bhyarcyārghyaṃ datvā । om ।

bhīmaṃ bhīmāṭṭahāsaṃ *ca daṃṣṭriṇaṃ piṅgaladyutim ।* (l.4)
raudraṃ triṇetraṃ sa śubhaṃ bālacandradharaṃ param ॥ iti dhyātvāvāhayet । om ।

tīkṣṇadaṃṣṭrākarālaṃ ca *abhayaṃ ca caturbhujam ।* (l.5)
devāsuraṛṣirvandyaṃ sarvalakṣaṇalakṣitam ॥

	iti dhyātvāvāhayet । tadūrdhve । oṃ prabhūtāya namaḥ । oṃ suvaktrāya (l.6) namaḥ । oṃ śaṅkhapālāya namaḥ । oṃ mahāvīrāya namaḥ । oṃ bhairavāya namaḥ । oṃ vīrabhadrāya namaḥ । om ugrāya namaḥ ।

śuklaṃ śubhraṃ haritālaṃ *śyāmaraktaṃ tu śālmalam ।* (l.7)
kāśmīraṃ dhūmravarṇaṃ tu suprabhūtādivarṇakam ॥
khaḍgakheṭakahastāśca bhīmarūpān sudaṃṣṭrakān ।
sarvābharaṇa*saṃyuktānūrdhvakeśagalānvitān ॥* (p.106b, f.131a, l.1)
dvinetrān kālavadanān svanāmādyakṣaraistathā ।

	indrādidikṣūpavedikopari om avighnāya namaḥ । oṃ vighnarājāya namaḥ । (l.2) oṃ bhakṣyāśī pañcahastāya namaḥ । oṃ hastivaktrāya namaḥ । oṃ lamboṣṭhāya namaḥ । oṃ vighneśvarāya namaḥ । oṃ gaṇādhipataye namaḥ । oṃ vināyakāya namaḥ । (l.3) om ekadantāya namaḥ । oṃ dvau bhakṣyāya namaḥ । om ahimekhalāya namaḥ । iti saṃpūjayet । etān gaṇapativadāvāhanasthāpanasannidhāna(l.4)sannirodhanapādyācamanārghyadānāntairaṣṭabhiḥ samskāraiḥ samskṛtya । oṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ ślauṃ gaṃ mahāgaṇapataye namaḥ । iti mūla(l.5)mantreṇa sarveṣāṃ sārdhamaikatvena bhāvayet । tato dhūpadīpanaivedyapānīya-tāmbūladakṣiṇacchatracāmarālavaṭṭanīrāñjana(l.6)mantrapuṣpādyaiḥ santoṣya । mahāgaṇapatimūlamantramaṣṭottaraśataṃ japitvā । nivedyākṣama-svetyabhisandhāya vandayettataḥ । (l.7) kuṇḍasamīpaṃ gatvā kuṇḍasamskārādināmakaraṇapūrṇāntaṃ kṛtvā । tato mantrāṇāṃ tarpaṇadīpanaṃ ca vidadhyāt । oṃ hom īśānamūrdhāya (p.107a, l.1) svāhetyekaikayāhutyā tarpaṇam । oṃ hūṃ hauṃ huṃ śivāya huṃ phaṭ । oṃ hūṃ hoṃ hūṃ īśānamūrdāya huṃ phaḍityādityāhutyā dīpanam । (l.2) prāyaścittārthamaghoreṇāsinā śataṃ pūrṇāsahitaṃ juhuyāt । caruśeṣaṃ nivedyācamanatāmbūladi datvā śivamagniṃ nirudhyā(dhya) nirodhārghyaṃ datvā (l.3) vyāhṛtirhutvā śrotravandanaṃ kṛtvā । 

bhagavan jñānato mohāt brahmādyaṃśatrayepi yat । 
kṛtaṃ karma mayā nyūnamadhikaṃ bhaktavatsala ॥ (l.4)
tatsarvamastu saṃpūrṇaṃ tvatprasādādiha(hā)dhvare । iti vijñāpya । 

homairvedādighoṣaiśca gītanṛttamahotsavaiḥ । 
bhaktitaḥ *saptarātraṃ vā pañcarātraṃ trirātrakam ॥* (l.5)
ekarātramatho sadyaḥ kuryāddevādhivāsanam ।
॥ iti candrajñānoktavighneśvarapratiṣṭhā(l.6)dhivāsanavidhiḥ samāptaḥ ॥ 

	atha prātaḥ kṛtanityavidhirguruḥ sūryadvārapūjāṃ bhūtaśuddhyantaryāgaviśeṣārghyādi (p.107b, f.132a) lokapālādi-sthaṇḍilapūjāntaṃ kṛtvā gaṇapatimaṣṭapuṣpābhyarcya । vahnau pūrvavacchivaṃ saṃpūjya । mantratarpaṇāntaṃ kṛtvā । mūrtipāśca sva sva kuṇḍe (l.2) gaṇapatimabhyarcya santarpya । carupākaṃ kṛtvā nivedya vāhanādiyāgadevān parāṅmukhārghyaṃ datvāntarbalibahirbaliṃ ca kṛtvā (l.3) agnisthadevān kumbhe saṃyojya । vedavādyaturyaghoṣaiśca sahitaṃ umbhānālayaṃ pradakṣiṇaṃ kṛtvā sthiralagna-(l.4)sumuhūrte kumbhābhiṣekaṃ kṛtvā datvārcanaṣaḍadhvārcanādikaṃ kṛtvā daśadānabhūridāna-brāhmaṇabhojana-(l.5)mahānaivedyādikaṃ kṛtvā yāgaṃ samāpyācāryaṃ sādhakaṃ mūrtipāṃ paricārakaśilpinaiḥ saha dakṣiṇatāmbūlādi (l.6) datvā । santoṣya । gaṇapatyāntaṃ gatvā karma samarpya īśānakumbhe caṇḍāṃ saṃpūjya । karma samāpya । (p.108a, l.1) dīnāndhakṛpaṇān sarvān yajamānaḥ prapūjayet iti varuṇācāryatapaddhatyāṃ candrajñānoktavighneśvara(l.2)pratiṣṭāmaṇṭapapūjāvidhiḥ samāptaḥ ॥ śubhamastu ॥ śrī dakṣiṇāmūrtigurave namaḥ ॥ (remaining place blank, p.108b, l.1) …..cāruvadanaṃ caturbāhuṃ susaṃhitaṃ divyāmbaradharaṃ devaṃ jaṭāmakuṭamaṇḍitaṃ mṛgīparaśusaṃyuktaṃ varadābhayahastakaṃ kuñcitaṃ vāmapādaṃ tu (l.2) .......pādaṃ pra....taṃ praṇavārghya-prabhāyuktaṃ sarvālaṅkārasamyutam ॥ iti dhyātvā ........
dvibhujāṃ cāruvadanāṃ sustanāṃ ca *suyauvanām ।* (l.3)
............kiñcitprabhāsamanvitāṃ sarvābharaṇasaṃyuktāṃ prasane haravat sthitām । iti dhyātvā ।
